*Biagio Montaruli,Andrea Oliveri,Savino Dambra,Davide Balzarotti*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次针对macOS系统提出使用领域特定静态特征（如嵌入式证书、权限、持久化技术和关键系统API）训练机器学习恶意软件检测器，在41,129个样本上达到98.50%的检测率，并在9,000个新样本上保持99.50%的检测率，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: macOS用户和企业系统日益普及，但恶意软件研究主要集中在Windows和Android上，macOS恶意软件检测问题相对未被探索。

Method: 首次引入macOS领域特定的静态特征（嵌入式证书、权限、持久化技术、关键系统API），结合机器学习训练恶意软件检测器。

Result: 在41,129样本数据集上达到98.50%检测率，比现有方法平均提升16%；在9,000个新样本上达到99.50%检测率，比现有方法提升50%；移除领域特定特征后性能下降15.92%。

Conclusion: macOS领域特定静态特征对恶意软件检测至关重要，所提方法具有优异的检测性能和泛化能力，数据集已开源。

Abstract: Despite the growing popularity of macOS among end users and enterprise systems, malware research has primarily focused on Windows and Android operating systems, leaving the problem of macOS malware detection relatively unexplored. Indeed, the specificity of the operating system and the unique characteristics of the Mach-O file format can play a fundamental role in the classification of unknown samples, drastically increasing the detection rate. In this work, for the first time in the literature, we employ new domain-specific features, i.e., static features specific to macOS binaries, such as embedded certificates, entitlements, persistence techniques and key system APIs, to train a machine learning malware detector. We perform a comprehensive experimental evaluation on a novel dataset of 41,129 samples, comprising 11,413 benign and 29,716 malicious executables, and demonstrate that our solution achieves state-of-the-art detection performance (98.50%), outperforming all existing approaches, with an average improvement of 16% in terms of detection rate. We also provide an in-depth analysis of the importance of the individual features, showing that our detector effectively leverages the new domain-specific features. Then, in order to evaluate the generalization capabilities of our detector over time, we perform a real-world evaluation on a new dataset of 9,000 fresh macOS executables. The results show that (i) our detector maintains a very high detection rate (99.50%), (ii) outperforms the state-of-the-art by 50%, and (iii) the domain-specific features are crucial for generalizing to novel malware samples, as their removal leads to a 15.92% drop in detection performance. Finally, we also release our dataset to the research community.

</details>


### [28] [Privilege Risk Evolution for Non-Human Identities: A Temporal Fiber Model for Cloud IAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03289)
*Christophe Parisel*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出云权限治理中非人类身份(NHI)的等价关系包含结构等价(图纤维化)和时间等价(光纤转移图中的强连通分量)，后者称为特权回路。构建了三层框架：空间商图、谱系划分、窗口SCC分析，并在Azure租户上验证了棘轮型特权回路可预测长期结构稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有云权限治理将权限等价视为静态关系，忽视了非人类身份(NHI)权限随时间演化的动态特性，需要更精细的等价模型来捕捉权限结构的时空变化。

Method: 提出三层框架：1) 通过图纤维化对权限图进行空间商化；2) 谱系划分组织稳定的转移区间；3) 在谱系内进行窗口化SCC分析作为时间商化。定义特权回路为时间等价下的等价类。

Result: 在大型Azure租户上的实证评估支持该框架的有效性。回测表明，早期观察到棘轮型特权回路可预测长期结构稳定性。

Conclusion: 权限等价应同时考虑结构（空间）和时间两个不可约维度，特权回路是理解非人类身份权限演化的关键结构，可用于预测权限治理的长期稳定性。

Abstract: Cloud permission governance implicitly treats permission equivalence as a static relation. We show that for non-human identities (NHIs), equivalence has two irreducible components: structural equivalence, capturing identical permission profiles at a snapshot via graph fibration, and temporal equivalence, capturing recurring permission states via strongly connected components (SCCs) in a fiber transition graph. We call the equivalence classes under temporal equivalence privilege circuits.
  We formalize a three-layer framework: (1) a spatial quotient of the permission graph via fibration, (2) a lineage partition organizing stable transition compartments, (3) windowed SCC analysis as a temporal quotient within lineages.
  Empirical evaluation on a large Azure tenant supports the framework. Backtesting demonstrates that early observation of ratchet-type privilege circuits predicts long-term structural stability.

</details>


### [29] [The Security Budget of Code LLMs: An Information-Theoretic Capacity-Security Bound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03308)
*Jianwei Tai*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了代码大语言模型中功能容量与扰动保持之间的信息论权衡，提出了一个理论界限，并通过实验验证了该界限的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编程助手将自然语言提示作为软件开发接口，提示的微小扰动会带来可用性和安全性风险，因此需要从信息论角度理解代码LLM的功能能力与扰动敏感性之间的根本权衡。

Method: 建立信息论框架，定义功能容量Cap= I(c*;c_π)和扰动保持Sec= I(c_π;ĉ_π)，推导出Cap+Sec ≤ H(c*)+I(p;p̃)界限；提出确定性嵌入推论和tokenizer/gzip伴随界；通过六种验证配置（两种模型、两种数据集、INT4/BF16精度）进行实证验证，并使用23种扰动池的适应性压力测试。

Result: 嵌入检查(Cap+max_T Sec)/(H(z*)+max_T I(p;p̃)) ≤ 1在所有六个验证行中成立，饱和度0.27-0.92，定理松弛2.36-26.94 nats；上下文混合余弦与pass@1显著相关（CodeLlama-HumanEval ρ=0.36, Qwen-HumanEval ρ=0.22, CodeLlama-MBPP ρ=0.225）；适应性压力测试均留有正松弛。

Conclusion: 证明了代码LLM的功能容量与扰动保持之间存在信息论界限，该界限在多种实验条件下得到验证，为理解代码LLM在扰动下的行为提供了理论基础。

Abstract: AI programming assistants make natural-language prompts a software-development interface, so small prompt perturbations become usability and security risks. We study an information-theoretic trade-off for code LLMs between functional capacity, $\Cap=\rmI(c^*;c_π)$, and perturbation retention, $\Sec=\rmI(c_π;\tilde c_π)$. Here $\Sec$ is a retention-channel quantity, not a direct measure of exploit success or vulnerable-code generation. For code completion modeled as $p\to c_π$ with perturbed prompt $\tilde p$, we prove $\Cap+\Sec\le \rmH(c^*)+\rmI(p;\tilde p)$, decomposing the budget into task entropy and prompt leakage. A deterministic-embedding corollary gives the hidden-state version, and a tokenizer/gzip companion bound gives a model-agnostic ceiling on sequence-level task entropy. Empirically, we estimate embedded $\Cap$ and $\Sec$ from output-only last-token hidden states, excluding prompt context from the $\Sec$ channel. Six individual validation rows across two models, two datasets, INT4/BF16 precision, and estimator ablations satisfy the embedded check $(\Cap+\max_T\Sec)/(\rmH(z^*)+\max_T\rmI(p;\tilde p))\le1$. Saturation is 0.27--0.92 and theorem slack is 2.36--26.94 nats; a separate three-seed stability diagnostic has mean saturation 0.87. A context-mixed cosine, used only as a per-problem generation-prompt alignment signal, correlates with pass@1 on CodeLlama-HumanEval ($ρ{=}0.36$, $p{<}10^{-4}$), Qwen-HumanEval ($ρ{=}0.22$, $p{=}0.005$), and CodeLlama-MBPP ($ρ{=}0.225$, $p{=}0.0038$; all $n{=}164$). Adaptive stress tests with a 23-perturbation pool, a fixed universal suffix, and prompt-embedding PGD all leave positive slack.

</details>


### [30] [dstack-capsule: Pod-Level Remote Attestation for Confidential Workloads on Kubernetes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03323)
*Yang Yang,Kevin Wang,Yuanhai Luo,Hang Yin,Jie Cai,Shunfan Zhou,Wenfeng Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: dstack-capsule 是一个基于 Intel TDX 的 Kubernetes 平台，通过在单个机密虚拟机内共享多个 Pod 并实现硬件签名的 Pod 级远程证明，解决了现有 Confidential Containers (CoCo) 方案中"每 Pod 一 VM"带来的资源开销过高和容器级身份无法验证的问题。其核心是双层证明架构：静态平台测量值通过不可逆权限熔断机制冻结在 RTMR[3] 中，动态 Pod 身份嵌入 TDX Quote 的 report_data 字段并由硬件签名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Confidential Containers (CoCo) 方案采用"每 Pod 一 VM"的严格隔离模型，仅证明 Guest OS 堆栈，无法验证容器级身份，且每个 VM 带来的资源开销过高，不适合 LLM-as-a-Service 等机密云工作负载。

Method: 提出双层证明架构：(1) Pod 级证明协议，将 Pod spec 摘要绑定到硬件签名的 TDX Quote； (2) 权限熔断机制，原子性地将节点从设置模式切换到安全模式； (3) 多层沙箱，涵盖存储、运行时、准入、API 和网络隔离层； (4) 基于 Kubernetes 1.32、Intel TDX 和 Sysbox 的开源实现。

Result: 实现了 Pod 粒度的远程证明验证，且避免了每 VM 隔离的资源开销，证明了安全属性、证明正确性和性能特征。

Conclusion: dstack-capsule 通过允许多个 Pod 共享单个机密 VM 并保持独立硬件支持的证明身份，实现了 Pod 级远程证明，在不增加每 VM 资源开销的前提下提供了更细粒度的安全保证。

Abstract: The rise of LLM-as-a-Service and other confidential cloud workloads demands cryptographic proof that user data is processed in a trusted, untampered environment. Existing solutions, notably Confidential Containers (CoCo), enforce a strict "one Pod per VM" model that attests only the Guest OS stack, leaving container-level identity unverified and incurring prohibitive per-VM resource overhead. We present dstack-capsule, a Kubernetes platform that enables Pod-level remote attestation on Intel TDX by allowing multiple Pods to share a single Confidential VM while each retains independent, hardware-backed proof of identity. Our key insight is a two-layer attestation architecture: static platform measurements are frozen in RTMR[3] via an irreversible privilege fuse, while dynamic Pod identities (pod_uid, pod_spec_hash, workload_id) are embedded in the TDX Quote's report_data field and signed by hardware on every request. dstack-capsule introduces (1) a Pod-level attestation protocol binding Pod spec digests to hardware-signed Quotes; (2) a privilege fuse mechanism that atomically transitions a node from setup mode to secure mode; (3) a multi-layer sandbox spanning storage, runtime, admission, API, and network isolation layers; and (4) a complete open-source implementation based on Kubernetes 1.32, Intel TDX, and Sysbox. We evaluate the security properties, attestation correctness, and performance characteristics of dstack-capsule, demonstrating that it achieves Pod-granularity verification without the resource overhead of per-VM isolation.

</details>


### [31] [RogueMerge: Robust and Unified Attacks against LLM Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03344)
*Jinghuai Zhang,Yetian He,Kunlin Cai,Han Zhao,Fnu Suya,Yuan Tian*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出RogueMerge框架，首次系统性地解决了针对生成式大语言模型（LLM）的模型融合攻击问题。该框架通过联合优化、随机最小-最大优化和分布鲁棒优化，分别应对自回归解码导致攻击退化、未知融合配置和攻击提示泛化三大挑战，在多种威胁、融合算法和模型上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型融合通过聚合来自未验证公开平台的向量来组合专门能力，这暴露了严重的供应链攻击面：攻击者提供的任务向量可直接写入模型权重，从而植入或放大多种下游威胁。现有研究仅针对分类器使用静态算术启发式进行后门攻击，无法有效处理生成式LLM的攻击。

Method: 针对自回归生成：用联合优化替代静态算术，显式保证融合后攻击成功；针对未知融合设置：将攻击注入建模为随机最小-最大问题，通过元学习风格的模拟求解；针对异构攻击提示泛化：采用分布鲁棒优化，推导出LLM规模下可计算的一阶泰勒近似，并给出可证明的误差界。

Result: 在四种威胁、六种融合算法和超过170个融合LLM上，RogueMerge一致优于现有攻击方法，在不同融合设置下保持稳定，并能抵抗标准防御手段。

Conclusion: RogueMerge是首个原则性、统一的框架，能够有效解决生成式LLM模型融合中的攻击注入问题，揭示了现有静态攻击方法的不足，并为模型融合供应链安全提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Model merging composes specialized capabilities into a single LLM by aggregating task vectors sourced from unverified public platforms, exposing a critical supply-chain attack surface: Because any malicious behavior can be encoded into a task vector, and merging grants third-party vectors direct write access to model weights, an attacker-provided task vector can enable or amplify diverse downstream threats. Prior work studies only backdoor attacks against model merging for classifiers using static arithmetic heuristics, which fail to effectively handle diverse attacks on generative LLMs for three reasons. (i) LLMs rely on autoregressive decoding, where the minor parameter drift introduced by merging compounds across tokens and rapidly degrades the attack. (ii) Attackers have no knowledge of the victim's merging configurations, causing a static attack vector optimized in isolation to be easily diluted or destroyed. (iii) Practical threat induction must generalize to attack prompts unseen during optimization, which static vectors cannot adequately encode. We present RogueMerge, the first principled, unified framework that addresses all three challenges. To handle autoregressive generation, we replace static arithmetic with a joint optimization that explicitly enforces attack success after merging. To handle unknown merging settings, we formulate attack injection as a stochastic min-max problem and solve it via meta-learning-style simulation. To generalize across heterogeneous attack prompts, we employ distributionally robust optimization and derive a tractable first-order Taylor approximation at LLM scale, with a provable error bound. Across four threats, six merging algorithms, and over 170 merged LLMs, RogueMerge consistently outperforms existing attacks. It also remains stable across diverse merging settings and resists standard defenses.

</details>


### [32] [ImageAuditor: Membership Inference Attack against Image-based Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03354)
*Jinghuai Zhang,Pengyue Yu,Zhexiao Lin,Kunlin Cai,Fnu Suya,Yuan Tian*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出首个针对图像检索增强生成（IRAG）系统的成员推断攻击方法ImageAuditor，通过奖励引导策略优化（RGPO）解决跨模态检索难题，并设计专用提取策略，仅需四次查询即可实现超过80%的AUROC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对文本RAG的成员推断攻击无法直接迁移到IRAG，主要面临两个挑战：一是跨模态检索困难（图像无法嵌入文本查询，且图像描述无法弥合模态差距），二是判别信号提取困难（T2I生成器输出图像而非遵循问答指令）。因此需要专门针对IRAG的审计方法。

Method: 将每个攻击查询分解为检索段和提取段。检索段采用奖励引导策略优化（RGPO），从奖励排序候选中更新随机策略以导航跨模态嵌入空间，具有有限样本最优性保证。提取段分析MIA分数分布以指导提示策略和评分规则的协同设计，针对T2I和Q&A任务分别定制。通过K均值聚类跨查询聚合信号进行成员决策。

Result: 在多种IRAG系统上，ImageAuditor仅需四次查询即可超过80%的AUROC，并在多种设置下保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 首次填补了IRAG成员推断攻击的空白，验证了通过专用优化解决跨模态检索和信号提取挑战的有效性，为IRAG系统中版权合规审计提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Image-based Retrieval-Augmented Generation (IRAG) conditions a frozen generator on reference images retrieved from an external database, supporting both text-to-image (T2I) and question answering (Q&A) tasks. Because these databases are opaque and web-scraped, copyright holders need ways to audit whether specific images appear in them. While prior work employs membership inference attacks (MIAs) to audit uni-modal, text-based RAG, they fail to transfer to IRAG due to two key challenges. First, cross-modal retrieval: text-RAG MIAs force retrieval of the target passage by injecting its content into the query, which is unavailable in IRAG since images cannot be embedded into text queries; even accurate image captions fail to bridge the modality gap. Second, discriminative signal extraction: text-RAG MIAs extract membership signals by prompting the generator to answer multiple questions over the target passage, whereas T2I generators in IRAG produce images rather than follow Q&A commands. To fill this gap, we introduce the first MIA tailored to IRAG, ImageAuditor, which decomposes each attack query into a retrieval segment and an extraction segment, enabling dedicated optimization for each challenge. For retrieval, we propose Reward-Guided Policy Optimization (RGPO), which updates a stochastic policy from reward-ranked candidates to navigate the cross-modal embedding landscape and admits finite-sample optimality guarantees to balance exploration and exploitation. For extraction, we analyze the distribution of the MIA score to guide the co-design of the prompting strategy and scoring rule, and derive task-specific instantiations for T2I and Q&A tasks. We aggregate signals across queries via K-means clustering for reliable membership decisions. Across various IRAG systems, ImageAuditor exceeds 80% AUROC with only four queries per audited image and remains robust across diverse settings.

</details>


### [33] [AI Model Extraction Attacks: Bypassing Single-Client Assumptions in Defenses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03381)
*Maxime Schwarzer,Johannes F. Loevenich,Gustavo Sánchez,Laurin Holz,Thies Möhlenhof,Tobias Hürten,Roberto Rigolin F. Lopes,Veit Hagenmeyer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了现有AI模型提取攻击防御策略的核心缺陷——单一客户端假设（SCA）在高级持续性威胁（APT）等协同攻击者面前无效。作者提出了模块化开源框架CerberusAI，验证了PRADA等主流防御机制可通过简单的轮询查询分布策略被绕过，且全局聚合方法在自适应流量混合下也会失效，呼吁转向有状态的、不依赖身份的防御架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对模型提取攻击的防御策略普遍基于单一客户端假设（SCA），即默认攻击来自孤立身份。然而在军事指挥控制（C2）系统等关键场景中，攻击者往往以协同方式（如APT）发起攻击，SCA的假设基础实际上不成立，亟需验证并暴露该漏洞。

Method: 提出模块化开源框架CerberusAI用于可复现的模型窃取研究，通过模拟分布式攻击场景，采用轮询查询分发策略和自适应流量混合方法，对PRADA等现有防御机制进行实证评估。

Result: 实验表明：PRADA等防御机制被基本轮询策略显著降低检测性能；全局聚合方法在自适应流量混合下丧失实用价值。SCA在协同攻击者面前完全失效。

Conclusion: 模型提取攻击防御需要范式转移，从依赖身份标识的架构转向有状态的（stateful）、不依赖身份的防御架构。

Abstract: Ensuring the protection of Artificial Intelligence (AI) models deployed in military Command and Control (C2) systems and critical infrastructure is essential for maintaining information superiority. Model Extraction Attacks (MEAs) pose a significant threat, as they enable adversaries to replicate proprietary models, compromise protected information, and prepare offline adversarial attacks. However, current defense strategies predominantly rely on the Single Client Assumption (SCA), which is the implicit assumption that attacks originate from isolated identities. This work systematically demonstrates that the SCA is fundamentally invalid in the presence of coordinated threat actors, such as Advanced Persistent Threats (APTs). We introduce a modular, open-source framework called CerberusAI for reproducible model-stealing research, and use it to simulate distributed attack scenarios. Our empirical evaluation shows that well-established defense mechanisms, such as Protecting Against Deep Neural Network Model Stealing Attacks (PRADA), can be bypassed by basic round-robin query distribution strategies, resulting in a significant reduction in detection performance. Furthermore, we demonstrate that even global aggregation approaches can be rendered operationally useless through adaptive traffic mixing. These results highlight the need for a paradigm shift towards stateful, identity-independent defense architectures in the field of model extraction attacks. This paper was originally presented at the International Conference on Military Communication and Information Systems (ICMCIS), organized by the Information Systems Technology (IST) Scientific and Technical Committee, IST-224-RSY - the ICMCIS, held in Bath, United Kingdom, 12-13 May 2026 and won the best paper award.

</details>


### [34] [Operationalizing Cyber Attack Prediction: A Gap-Prioritized Framework with Dataset and Model Selection Guidelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03386)
*Aminu Muhammad Auwal*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了AI驱动的网络攻击预测从研究到部署的五大实施障碍（数据集过时、攻击范围狭窄、模型可解释性、对抗鲁棒性不足、隐私伦理问题），提出了间隙优先级排序框架和实用实施路线图，并将45个基准数据集分类为生产就绪、仅限研究和不可用三类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI与机器学习在网络攻击预测方面虽有进展，但理论研究与实际操作部署之间存在显著差距，亟需识别并弥合这一鸿沟。

Method: 对150多个基准数据集和200多项研究进行综合分析，提出间隙优先级排序框架（基于检测影响、实施成本和修复时间三个维度评估），并构建数据集质量评估框架。

Result: 数据集过时和对抗鲁棒性不足是最高优先级的间隙；模型可解释性是资源受限环境下最具成本效益的改进路径；45个基准数据集被分为生产就绪、仅限研究和不可用三类。

Conclusion: 本研究将学术发现转化为可操作的决策支持工具，为构建鲁棒、面向生产的AI驱动网络防御提供了实用路线图。

Abstract: While AI and machine learning for cyber attack prediction have advanced, a critical gap persists between theoretical research and practical operational deployment. Building on Ankalaki et al. (2025), this paper provides a comprehensive analysis of 150+ benchmark datasets and 200+ studies to identify and prioritize five implementation hurdles: (1) temporal dataset obsolescence, (2) narrow attack scope, (3) real-time model interpretability, (4) inadequate adversarial robustness, and (5) privacy/ethical concerns. We introduce a novel gap-prioritization framework that evaluates these limitations based on detection impact, implementation cost, and remediation time. Our analysis identifies dataset obsolescence and adversarial robustness as the highest-priority gaps, while highlighting model interpretability as the most cost-effective path for resource-constrained environments. To bridge the research-practice divide, we provide a practical implementation roadmap and a dataset quality assessment framework that classifies 45 benchmarks into production-ready, research-only, and unusable categories. This work translates academic findings into actionable decision-support tools for robust, production-oriented AI-driven cyber defense.

</details>


### [35] [Bastet: A Fine-Grained Expert-Labeled Dataset for DeFi Smart Contract Vulnerability Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03387)
*Wan-Hsuan Hsu,Wei-Hsin Wang,Cheng-Yu Liou,Ting-Rui Ke,Kentaroh Toyoda*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了Bastet，一个由专家标注的DeFi智能合约漏洞数据集，包含从394份Code4rena审计报告中收集的4,402个发现，其中849个由白帽安全研究人员通过双标注者共识流程进行完整标注，解决了现有数据集存在的过时Solidity版本、幻觉标签噪声和粗粒度标注三大问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DeFi智能合约漏洞数据集存在三个根本问题：基于过时Solidity版本（如v0.4）、依赖自动化或LLM标注导致的幻觉标签噪声、以及粗粒度单层标注无法捕捉真实业务逻辑漏洞的语义复杂性，需要构建高质量、符合现代DeFi实践的标注数据集。

Method: 从394份Code4rena竞争性审计报告（2021年4月至2024年11月）中收集4,402个发现，由DeFiHackLabs社区的白帽安全研究人员通过双标注者共识工作流进行人工专家标注，并采用两层分类体系（46个标签和77个子标签）。

Result: 构建了包含4,402个发现的Bastet数据集，其中849个发现已完成完整的人工专家标注，所有标注均通过双标注者共识流程确保准确性。

Conclusion: Bastet通过基于真实世界审计结果、人类专家讨论共识标注和两层语义分类体系，有效解决了现有漏洞数据集存在的过时版本、标签噪声和粗粒度标注问题，为LLM驱动的漏洞检测提供了更可靠的评估基准。

Abstract: Smart contract vulnerabilities in Decentralized Finance (DeFi) protocols resulted in over 1.49 billion USD in confirmed losses in 2024 alone, across 192 incidents [1]. As LLM-based vulnerability detection emerges as a promising approach to address these threats, the quality of evaluation datasets has become a critical bottleneck. Existing datasets suffer from three fundamental problems: they are built on outdated Solidity versions (e.g., v0.4) that no longer reflect modern DeFi contracts [5][6][7]; they rely on automated or LLM-generated annotations that introduce hallucination-driven label noise [9][10]; and they apply coarse single-layer labeling that fails to capture the semantic complexity of real-world business logic vulnerabilities [6][7][11][12]. We present Bastet, an expert-labeled DeFi smart contract vulnerability dataset that addresses all three problems through real-world audit findings (2021-2024), human expert annotation with discussion-based consensus, and a two-layer taxonomy of 46 Tags and 77 Subtags. Bastet comprises 4,402 findings collected from 394 Code4rena competitive audit reports spanning April 2021 to November 2024, of which 849 findings are fully annotated by white-hat security researchers from the DeFiHackLabs community. All annotations are produced through a two-annotator consensus workflow, ensuring label accuracy grounded in real-world vulnerability root causes.

</details>


### [36] [FlowGuard: Flow Matching for Identity-Independent Detection of Data-Free Model Stealing Attacks on Energy System Intrusion Detection Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03430)
*Maxime Schwarzer,Laurin Holz,Tobias Huerten,Johannes Loevenich,Thies Moehlenhof,Roberto Rigolin F. Lopes,Veit Hagenmeyer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: FlowGuard是一种基于流匹配的身份无关防御方法，通过检测查询是否为OOD来抵御针对能源基础设施中AI入侵检测系统的模型窃取攻击，在单客户端和分布式Sybil场景下均保持稳定的检测率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能源基础设施中基于AI的入侵检测系统容易受到模型窃取攻击，现有防御方法在分布式攻击者（Sybil）面前无效（身份绑定查询监控），或无法适用于硬标签IDS部署（预测投毒）。

Method: 提出FlowGuard，一种基于连续归一化流（Continuous Normalizing Flow）的身份无关防御方法。利用数据无关模型窃取攻击生成的合成查询处于低维流形这一特性，通过计算查询在合法数据训练流上的对数似然来检测OOD查询。

Result: 针对PRADA和FDINet，使用MAZE和DisGUIDE攻击在单客户端和100客户端Sybil场景下评估：PRADA在分布变化时检测率降至0%，而FlowGuard在两种场景下均保持稳定的检测率。

Conclusion: FlowGuard在无需身份信息的情况下实现了对模型窃取攻击的有效防御，讨论了方法的适用范围和局限性，并指出其可能扩展应用于数据依赖型攻击。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI)-based Intrusion Detection Systems (IDS) deployed in energy infrastructure are vulnerable to model theft attacks, which allow adversaries to create evasive traffic offline. Current defences against model extraction rely either on identity-bound query monitoring, which is ineffective against distributed attackers (Sybil), or on prediction poisoning through soft-label perturbation, which is inapplicable to hard-label IDS deployments. Therefore, we propose FlowGuard, an identity-independent defence based on flow matching that classifies incoming queries as out-of-distribution (OOD) prior to IDS processing. This approach exploits the fact that queries generated synthetically for data-free model stealing attacks occupy a lower-dimensional manifold than real network traffic. This results in measurably lower log-likelihoods when using a Continuous Normalizing Flow that has been trained on legitimate data. We evaluate our method against PRADA and FDINet using MAZE and DisGUIDE attacks in single-client and distributed (100-client Sybil) settings. While PRADA's detection rate dropped to 0% when the distribution changed, our defence maintained a stable detection rate across both settings without relying on identity information. We discuss the scope and limitations of the approach, and outline potential applications to data-dependent attacks.

</details>


### [37] [A Hybrid Approach For Malware Classification Using Secondary Features Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03432)
*Raja Khurram Shahzad,Muhammad Mustaqeem,Haroon Elahi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于特征融合（API调用、固定/可变长度n-gram）和算法融合（投票集成）的自动化恶意软件检测与家族分类方法，在Microsoft数据集上达到AUC 0.989、准确率99.72%、对数损失0.01，优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 恶意软件数量快速增长，传统检测方法无法将检测到的恶意软件分类到对应家族，阻碍了有效的恶意软件缓解。

Method: 提取API调用、固定长度和可变长度n-gram等恶意软件特征，采用自定义特征选择方法进行特征融合；在预测模型层面，使用基于投票的算法融合方法。

Result: 在Microsoft数据集上的二分类和多分类实验中，AUC为0.989，准确率为99.72%，对数损失为0.01，优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的特征融合与算法融合方法在恶意软件检测和家族分类任务上有效且高效。

Abstract: The number of malware (either variant or novel) is rapidly increasing, making malware detection and mitigation a complex problem. One approach to improving malware mitigation is automatic detection and malware family classification. However, traditional malware detection methods cannot classify detected malware into their respective families, hindering effective malware mitigation. Consequently, this paper proposes a method to automate malware detection and classification of the detected malware into respective malware families. The proposed method uses feature fusion after extracting relevant malware features such as API calls and fixed and variable length n-grams with a customized feature selection method. Moreover, for the predictive model, a voting based approach is proposed for algorithm fusion. For the experimental evaluation of the proposed method, both binary and multi-class classification approaches are applied to the data set provided by Microsoft. Finally, the experimental results are compared with the state of the art. The experimental results indicate the effectiveness and efficiency of the proposed approach with an AUC of 0.989, accuracy of 99.72%, and a log loss of 0.01.

</details>


### [38] [Signals and Spoils: Speculative Oracle Extractable Value in the Era of Cross-Chain Interoperability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03434)
*Hasret Ozan Sevim,Christof Ferreira Torres*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 研究发现Layer-2区块链上存在一种新型MEV——投机性OEV，通过回溯预言机价格更新来清算不足抵押贷款。在Arbitrum、Base、Optimism上，57%的清算者（64个）和39%的清算交易（831笔）属于投机性清算。跨链分析表明，Chainlink预言机在不同链上独立发布相同价格数据的时间差异可被利用进行跨链OEV提取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Layer-2区块链缺乏公共mempool，导致MEV提取策略变为概率性（发送大量重复交易），造成交易垃圾信息和资源浪费。研究者希望量化这类投机性MEV的规模，并探索跨链预言机更新延迟是否创造了新的可提取价值机会。

Method: 提出检测投机性清算的方法论，在Arbitrum、Base、Optimism上应用；系统分析Chainlink DON配置（偏差阈值、心跳间隔、价格观测），收集63个Chainlink feeds、12,009次价格更新、超10万条预言机观测数据，关联2,986个Aave清算事件。

Result: 2025年10月10日，识别出64个投机性清算者（占Aave清算者57%），831笔成功投机性清算（占39%）。跨链分析表明Optimism上的Chainlink更新可预测Arbitrum和Base的后续更新，存在统计上可预测的跨链利用窗口。

Conclusion: 独立运行的DON几乎同时消费相同的链下价格数据，但发布时间不同，创造了可预测的跨链OEV提取机会。

Abstract: A new form of Maximal Extractable Value (MEV), termed speculative MEV, has emerged across Layer-2 blockchains. Unlike Ethereum mainnet, many Layer-2 systems lack a public mempool, forcing extraction strategies to become probabilistic: searchers emit multiple identical transactions hoping to capture an opportunity first. This generates substantial transaction spam, increasing fees and wasting block space. We investigate speculative Oracle Extractable Value (OEV), a form of MEV associated with liquidating undercollateralized loans via speculative backrunning of oracle price updates. We propose a methodology for detecting speculative liquidations in the wild and apply it across Arbitrum, Base, and Optimism. On October 10, 2025, we identify 64 speculative liquidators on Aave (57% of all detected liquidators) and 831 successful speculative liquidations (39% of all successful liquidations across the three chains). We further examine whether latency differences in oracle price feed updates across blockchains can be exploited for cross-chain OEV. Specifically, we ask whether a searcher can observe oracle updates on one chain and frontrun liquidation opportunities on another. We systematically analyze Chainlink Decentralized Oracle Network (DON) configurations (deviation thresholds, heartbeat intervals, and submitted price observations) across Arbitrum, Base, Ethereum, and Optimism. Our dataset comprises 63 Chainlink feeds, 12,009 price updates, and over 100,000 oracle observations linked to 2,986 Aave liquidations. We show that independent DONs consume largely identical off-chain price data nearly simultaneously yet publish updates at different times, creating statistically predictable cross-chain exploitation windows. We demonstrate that Chainlink updates on Optimism can predict subsequent updates on Arbitrum and Base, enabling speculative cross-chain OEV extraction.

</details>


### [39] [FORGE: Multi-Agent Graduated Exploitation and Detection Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03453)
*Farooq Shaikh*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本论文提出FORGE多智能体系统，通过渐进式利用深度桥接PoC生成、漏洞优先级排序和检测规则工程三个孤立社区，在603个CVE上实现67.8%的端到端L1+利用，成本仅1.50美元/CVE，并生成高保真检测规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 漏洞披露数量远超组织评估能力，而三个相邻研究社区（PoC生成、漏洞优先级排序、检测规则工程）彼此孤立运作，缺乏跨社区信号传递机制。

Method: FORGE采用多智能体流水线架构，包含五个专用智能体（Intel、Generator、Planner、Exploit、Detector），通过LLM主Oracle的四级利用深度评估（L0无证据到L3完全攻陷）和分层知识架构，实现从CVE元数据到检测规则的端到端自动化。

Result: 在603个CVE上达67.8% L1+利用，每CVE成本1.50美元，覆盖8种语言和187种CWE类型；L2+利用产生的检测规则在跨度归一化接地性上显著优于L1规则（p=0.035）；93.4%的Snort规则对合成良性语料库产生零误报。

Conclusion: 渐进式利用深度是桥接三个孤立社区的有效机制：更深利用产生更丰富行为轨迹用于检测工程，深度数据提供优先级排序验证的地面真值；基于模式的可达性与元数据优先级正交，表明方法互补性。

Abstract: Vulnerability disclosure volumes now far exceed organizational assessment capacity, yet three adjacent research communities (proof-of-concept generation, vulnerability prioritization, and detection rule engineering) operate largely in isolation. Existing automated exploit generation systems report binary pass/fail outcomes, discarding partial progress and producing no signal for the other two communities. This paper presents FORGE, a multi-agent system that bridges these three silos through graduated exploitation depth. Five specialized agents (Intel, Generator, Planner, Exploit, and Detector) execute in a fixed pipeline that (1) generates targeted vulnerable applications from CVE metadata, (2) conducts coached, multi-turn exploitation assessed by an LLM-primary oracle on a four-level taxonomy (L0: no evidence through L3: full compromise), and (3) produces Sigma and Snort detection rules grounded in OpenTelemetry exploitation traces. Graduated depth is the bridging mechanism: deeper exploitation yields richer behavioral traces for detection engineering, while depth data across scoring bands provides ground truth for prioritization validation. A tiered knowledge architecture accumulates intelligence across assessments, transferring build and exploitation experience to subsequent CVEs. Evaluation on 603 CVEs from the CVE-GENIE dataset achieves 67.8% end-to-end L1+ exploitation at USD 1.50 per CVE across eight languages and 187 CWE types. Exploitation rates remain near 68% regardless of EPSS or CVSS band, indicating that pattern-level reachability is orthogonal to metadata-based prioritization. Detection rules from L2+ exploitation achieve significantly higher span-normalized grounding than L1-derived rules (p=0.035), and 93.4% of generated Snort rules produce zero false positives against a synthetic benign corpus.

</details>


### [40] [Learn from Your Mistakes: Tree-like Self-Play for Secure Code LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03489)
*Wenqi Chen,Ziyan Zhang,Bing Wang,Lin Liu,Hengheng Zhang,Zhengsu Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出Tree-like Self-Play (TSP)框架，将安全代码生成建模为细粒度序列决策过程，通过构建决策树让模型在关键决策节点自我纠错，显著提升代码安全性并实现跨语言泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对齐技术（如SFT和RL）在序列层面进行粗粒度优化，无法有效处理安全缺陷的局部性——单个错误token可导致整个程序存在漏洞，亟需更细粒度的安全对齐方法。

Method: 将代码生成构建为决策树，模型探索安全"黄金路径"和易受攻击变体两种分支轨迹，通过自我对弈学习严格区分自身局部错误，在关键决策节点提供密集的在线策略学习信号以强制自我纠正。

Result: 在Python安全基准上，TSP将CodeLlama-7B的SPR@1提升至75.8%，远超SFT的57.0%；未见过的CWE类别漏洞减少24.5%；安全原则从C/C++成功迁移至Python、Go、JavaScript。

Conclusion: TSP并非简单记忆补丁，而是内化了抽象且与语言无关的安全逻辑，实现了鲁棒的跨语言分布外泛化。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) excel in code generation, they remain prone to replicating subtle yet critical vulnerabilities endemic to their training data. Current alignment techniques, such as Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) and Reinforcement Learning (RL), typically apply coarse-grained optimization at the sequence level. This approach often fails to address the localized nature of security flaws, where a single incorrect token choice can compromise an entire program. To bridge this gap, we introduce Tree-like Self-Play (TSP), a framework that reframes secure code generation as a fine-grained sequential decision process. Unlike standard methods that blindly maximize likelihood, TSP constructs a decision tree where the model explores branching trajectories--generating both secure "golden paths" and vulnerable variants. By treating code generation as a self-play game, the model learns to strictly discriminate against its own localized errors. This provides a dense, on-policy learning signal that forces self-correction precisely at the critical decision nodes where vulnerabilities typically emerge. Our experiments demonstrate that TSP fundamentally enhances model reliability. In Python security benchmarks, TSP boosts CodeLlama-7B's pass rate (SPR@1) to 75.8%, significantly outperforming SFT (57.0%) and unstructured self-play baselines. Crucially, TSP induces robust out-of-distribution generalization: the model not only reduces vulnerabilities in unseen categories (CWEs) by 24.5% but also successfully transfers security principles learned from C/C++ to diverse languages, including Python, Go, and JavaScript. This suggests that TSP does not merely memorize patches, but internalizes abstract, language-agnostic security logic.

</details>

*Peter Williams,Adam Sobey,Erisa Karafili*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一种基于排序二元分类器与显式弃权机制的高精度APT恶意软件归因方法，在训练集未知组样本占比87%的极端场景下，对94%的未知样本弃权，归因样本保持92%精确率与95%选择性准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有APT恶意软件归因方法多为封闭集分类器，无法处理训练集中未出现的APT组织样本，导致误归因。实际运营环境中分类器常遇到未知组样本，亟需能显式弃权的高精度归因方法。

Method: 为每个APT组训练两个二元分类器，根据验证性能排序后顺序应用；样本仅在分类器提供充分证据时归因，否则弃权。

Result: 在APT Malware数据集上精确率超过已有方法；在60个未知组占比87%的挑战性设置下，对94%的未知样本弃权，归因样本精确率92%，选择性准确率95%。

Conclusion: 所提基于排序二元分类器与显式弃权的方法能有效处理开放集场景下的APT归因问题，在高精度归因的同时对未知样本可靠弃权。

Abstract: Early attribution of Advanced Persistent Threat (APT) activity can help defenders prioritise investigation, select countermeasures, and reduce the impact of an intrusion. Malware provides useful attribution evidence, but automated APT malware attribution remains difficult in practice. Existing approaches are typically trained and evaluated as closed-set classifiers over a limited number of known APT groups. In operational environments, however, classifiers are likely to encounter samples from groups not represented during training. Closed-set classifiers are then forced to assign such samples to known groups, producing unsupported and potentially misleading attributions. We present a high-precision APT malware attribution method based on ranked binary classifiers with explicit abstention. Rather than training a single multi-class classifier, our approach trains and tunes two binary classifiers per APT group, ranks the classifiers by validation performance, and applies them sequentially. A sample is attributed only when a classifier provides sufficient evidence; otherwise, it abstains. We evaluate the method on the APT Malware dataset and on a larger combined dataset designed to stress-test out-of-scope behaviour. On the APT Malware dataset, the method achieves higher precision than previously published results on the same dataset. In the most challenging setting, where 87% of test samples came from 60 APT groups excluded from training, the method abstained on 94% of out-of-scope samples while maintaining 92% precision and 95% selective accuracy on the samples it classified.

</details>


### [42] [Don't Trust Us: A privacy-by-design android malware detection pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03714)
*Emmanuele Massidda,Diego Soi,Giorgio Giacinto*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种隐私内建（privacy-by-design）的Android恶意软件检测流程，通过静态分析（Drebin特征+SVM+双拒绝阈值）先进行高置信度检测，仅将不确定样本（6.7%）移交至沙箱动态分析，避免收集任何真实用户敏感数据，在2024-2025时间分割数据集上达到F1=0.87，证明无需牺牲隐私即可实现强检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Android恶意软件检测依赖收集敏感用户数据（设备标识、网络痕迹、运行时轨迹等），隐私保护（匿名化、加密、联邦学习）仅在数据收集后实施，仍需用户信任拥有特权访问权限的系统。作者认为应当从根本上消除这种数据依赖，而不是管理风险。

Method: 1) 形式化定义了一套隐私内建设计需求；2) 静态分析阶段：提取APK的Drebin特征，向量化后输入SVM分类器；3) 双拒绝阈值规则：模型对高置信度样本直接输出决策，对不确定样本拒识并移交；4) 动态分析阶段：仅对被拒识样本在沙箱环境中进行动态分析，整个流程不接触真实用户信息。

Result: 在2024-2025时间分割数据集上，第一阶段静态分析即达到F1=0.87，仅6.7%的测试样本需要移交至二次动态分析；动态沙箱分析无需提取任何敏感数据即可高置信度识别恶意性。

Conclusion: 强检测性能与用户隐私保护可以兼得，Android恶意软件检测应当采用隐私内建设计，从根本上避免对敏感数据的访问需求。

Abstract: Android malware detection increasingly relies on collecting and processing sensitive user data, including device identifiers, network artifacts, and runtime traces, while privacy is too often treated as a secondary concern. Existing privacy-aware approaches typically enforce privacy after data collection, for example, through anonymization, encryption, or federated learning, yet still require access to user information and therefore demand a high level of user trust in systems that already operate with privileged access to device activity. We argue that this requirement should be removed rather than managed. Android malware detection should be privacy-aware by design, so that effective analysis does not depend on sensitive data being accessed in the first place. To this end, we first formalize a set of design requirements for privacy-by-design detection and then implement each requirement in a comprehensive pipeline. First, static analysis is performed to extract relevant data from each APK, following the Drebin representation, which is then submitted to an SVM after vectorization. The model is equipped with a dual-reject threshold rule that either commits to a confident decision or defers uncertain samples to a dynamic analysis stage within a sandboxed environment, so that genuine user information never enters the analysis loop. Results confirm that, on a temporally split dataset spanning from 2024 to 2025, the pipeline achieves an F1 score of 0.87 with the first static analysis stage, deferring only 6.7% of test samples to secondary dynamic analysis. Additionally, dynamic sandboxing helps recognize applications' maliciousness with high confidence without extracting any sensitive data. These results demonstrate that strong detection performance is achievable without sacrificing user privacy.

</details>


### [43] [Same Weights, Different Robot: A Deployment Safety View of VLA Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03724)
*Jianwei Tai*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文指出视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略在实际部署中存在执行安全鸿沟：相同权重的模型因动作反归一化和控制器约定的差异，可产生不同的物理动作。作者将该问题形式化为可执行策略规范问题，提出ExecSpec证书用于量化动作空间语义漂移。实验表明，仅替换元数据键即可导致成功率大幅下降（如LIBERO-Goal从28/28降至2/28），证明了元数据属于可执行策略的一部分，应在部署前检查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VLA策略被视为仅由权重、提示和基准测试集定义的检查点对象，忽视了动作反归一化和控制器约定对实际执行动作的显著影响，导致安全审查通过检查点却遗漏了实际执行的机器人策略。

Method: 1) 形式化可执行策略规范问题，将VLA策略定义为包含学习模型、动作表示、元数据选择的去归一化器和控制器约定的四元组；2) 针对分位数式动作归一化，推导出闭式元数据不匹配变换；3) 提出ExecSpec证书，无需模型推理或真实部署即可测量动作空间语义漂移。

Result: 在LIBERO-Goal重放中，替换相似兄弟元数据键导致非夹爪六维动作平均漂移0.199，成功率从28/28降至2/28；LIBERO-Spatial从26/26降至0/26；四个Object替换均为0/28；Long替换为0/23或1/23。多种消融实验排除了其他简单解释。

Conclusion: 动作空间元数据是可执行策略的一部分，应在部署前检查。该结论不适用于闭环或硬件安全验证，而是支持更窄的部署安全视角。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies are often treated as checkpoint-defined objects: if the weights, prompt, and benchmark suite match, the deployment is assumed to be the same policy. Robot execution breaks this assumption because the same normalized model output can become a different physical action after action unnormalization and controller conventions are applied. This creates a deployment-safety gap: safety review can certify the checkpoint while missing the executable robot policy that reaches the controller. We formalize this gap as an executable policy specification problem: a VLA policy includes the learned model, action representation, metadata-selected unnormalizer, and controller-facing conventions. Under this view, identical checkpoints can be executable-inequivalent. For quantile-style action normalization, we derive a closed-form metadata mismatch transform and an ExecSpec certificate that measures action-space semantic drift without model inference or rollout. On LIBERO-Goal replay, substituting a plausible sibling metadata key yields mean drift 0.199 over six non-gripper action dimensions and reduces success from 28/28 to 2/28 under full substitution. On LIBERO-Spatial replay, the same substituted key reduces success from 26/26 to 0/26. The same full-substitution protocol gives 0/28 success for all four Object substitutions and 0/23 or 1/23 success on Long. Identity-key, replay-validity, no-op filtering, raw-vs-correct replay, mask/gripper, synthetic upper-bound, and OpenVLA-style unnormalizer interface checks rule out several simpler explanations. These results do not certify closed-loop or hardware safety. They support a narrower deployment-safety view: action-space metadata is part of the executable policy and should be checked before rollout.

</details>


### [44] [Collision Resistance of Single-Layer Neural Nets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03807)
*Marco Benedetti,Andrej Bogdanov,Enrico M. Malatesta,Marc Mézard,Gianmarco Perrupato,Alon Rosen,Nikolaj I. Schwartzbach,Riccardo Zecchina*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了单层二元神经网络中寻找碰撞的算法复杂度。通过识别阈值尺度 $κ=Θ(1/√α)$ 区分两种互补现象：当 $κ\ll 1/√α$ 时，存在高效在线算法产生大量碰撞；当 $κ\gg 1/√α$ 时，碰撞空间表现出重叠间隙性质（OGP），导致在线算法面临指数级下界。这是首次将OGP作为抗碰撞性的严格判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 首次系统研究单层二元神经网络中寻找碰撞的算法复杂度，揭示碰撞寻找与平均情况搜索之间的本质差异，并引入重叠间隙性质（OGP）作为抗碰撞性的严格理论判据。

Method: 识别出阈值尺度 $κ=Θ(1/√α)$（$α=m/n$）作为分界点：当 $κ\ll 1/√α$ 时设计简单在线算法；当 $κ\gg 1/√α$ 时，针对随机非周期激活函数，利用重叠间隙性质证明在线算法的指数下界。

Result: 在阈值较低时给出了高效产生大量碰撞的在线算法；在阈值较高时证明了碰撞空间具有重叠间隙性质，从而得到针对在线算法的指数级下界。

Conclusion: 首次使用重叠间隙性质作为抗碰撞性的严格判据，揭示了碰撞寻找问题中特有的"最坏情况"方面（碰撞寻找者完全控制碰撞对的选择），并将扩展到谱方法、代数方法、格方法或量子方法等方面的保证留作未来方向。

Abstract: We initiate the study of the algorithmic complexity of finding collisions in single-layer binary neural networks. Given a random matrix $\mathbf{A} \in \mathbb{R}^{m\times n}$, an input $\mathbf{x} \in \{-1,1\}^n$ is mapped to a binary output vector $\varphi(\mathbf{A}\mathbf{x})\in \{-1,1\}^m$, where $\varphi$ is an activation function with constant behavior on $[κ, \infty)$ for some threshold $κ\geq 0$.
  We identify the threshold scale $κ=Θ(1/\sqrtα)$, where $α=m/n$, as separating two complementary phenomena. When $κ\ll 1/\sqrtα$, we give a simple online algorithm that efficiently produces extensive collisions. When $κ\gg 1/\sqrtα$, for a natural \emph{randomized} non-periodic activation and suitable oscillation complexity, we prove that the extensive-collision space exhibits an overlap gap property (OGP), yielding an exponential lower bound against online algorithms.
  Ours is the first work to use the overlap gap property as a rigorous criterion for collision resistance. The key difference between collision finding and average-case search is that collision finding has a new ``worst-case'' aspect: the collision finder has full control over the choice of colliding pairs. Our lower bound is proved in the online model; extending such guarantees to broader classes of algorithms, including spectral, algebraic, lattice-based, or quantum methods, remains an open direction.

</details>


### [45] [AI Agents Enable Adaptive Computer Worms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03811)
*Jonas Guan,Tom Blanchard,Hanna Foerster,Hengrui Jia,Gabriel Huang,Nicolas Papernot*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文展示了一种基于AI Agent的新型计算机蠕虫，它能够利用被感染设备上的开源大语言模型（LLMs）进行自主推理，针对每个目标生成定制化攻击策略，并在Linux、Windows和IoT设备间传播，实现了零边际成本的自持式网络攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统蠕虫（如WannaCry）利用固定漏洞传播，可通过打补丁阻止；而AI驱动的蠕虫能够为每个目标生成针对性的攻击策略，构成全新的安全威胁。

Method: 利用被攻陷机器上的开源大语言模型（LLMs）维持推理能力并扩展攻击范围；寄生式使用被盗计算资源；通过利用真实企业网络中的常见漏洞在Linux、Windows和IoT设备间传播。

Result: 该蠕虫成功在企业网络环境中传播，攻击者每新增一个感染的边际成本为零；由于不依赖商业AI平台，传统的集中式安全控制（如服务拒绝或速率限制）对其无效。

Conclusion: 自持式AI驱动的网络威胁已不再是理论概念，需要为自主生成式对抗攻击（即无需人类操作、能够实时推理目标、适应观察并合成攻击逻辑的恶意软件系统）做好准备。

Abstract: A computer worm is malware that spreads on a network by replicating itself from one machine to another. Traditional worms, like WannaCry, exploited predetermined vulnerabilities, and their spread can be halted by patching those vulnerabilities. Here we show that artificial intelligence (AI) agents enable a fundamentally new threat: a worm that generates tailored attack strategies to each target it encounters. The worm parasitically uses compromised machines to run open-weight large language models (LLMs) to sustain its reasoning, or extend its reach for further attacks. Deployed on a network of machines spanning Linux, Windows, and IoT (Internet of Things) devices, the worm propagated by exploiting common, real-world corporate network vulnerabilities. Since the worm is powered by stolen compute, the attacker's marginal cost per new infection is zero. This creates a destabilizing economic asymmetry between attackers and defenders. Moreover, because the worm requires no commercial AI platform, centralized safety controls, such as service refusals or rate limiting, are structurally irrelevant. Our results demonstrate that self-sustaining AI-driven cyber-threats are no longer theoretical. We must prepare for autonomous generative adversaries: malware systems that propagate without human operators and are defined not by fixed exploit code, but by the capacity to reason about targets, adapt to observations, and synthesize attack logic in real time.

</details>


### [46] [TRAP: Hijacking VLA CoT-Reasoning via Adversarial Patches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23117)
*Zhengxian Huang,Wenjun Zhu,Haoxuan Qiu,Xiaoyu Ji,Wenyuan Xu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次揭示了基于Chain-of-Thought推理的视觉-语言-动作模型存在被对抗性攻击劫持行为的安全漏洞，并提出了TRAP攻击方法，通过在物理世界放置对抗性补丁（如桌布）来操控中间推理和下游动作，使机器人执行与用户指令语义不一致的危险行为（如递刀而非递苹果）。实验证明该方法在多种VLA模型上有效，凸显了保障CoT推理安全性的紧迫性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管CoT推理增强了VLA模型的泛化性和可解释性，但其安全性尚未得到充分探索。作者发现CoT推理可能被利用作为新型攻击向量，实现针对性的行为劫持，而无需修改用户指令。

Method: 提出TRAP攻击方法：通过对抗性补丁（如打印在纸上的图案）放置在物理环境中，干扰CoT推理到动作生成的路径，使模型产生与用户指令语义不一致的CoT推理，进而生成被攻击者定义的错误动作。

Result: 在三种具有不同CoT推理机制的VLA模型上验证了TRAP的有效性，并在真实物理世界中通过纸质打印补丁实现了攻击。实验表明CoT推理对动作生成具有强控制力，即便推理与输入指令语义不一致时仍主导动作输出。

Conclusion: CoT推理在VLA系统中引入新的安全风险，需要紧急加强对此类推理机制的安全防护。

Abstract: By integrating Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning, Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have demonstrated strong capabilities in robotic manipulation, particularly by improving generalization and interpretability. However, the security of CoT-based reasoning mechanisms remains largely unexplored. In this paper, we show that CoT reasoning introduces a novel attack vector for targeted behavior hijacking--for example, causing a robot to mistakenly deliver a knife to a person instead of an apple--without modifying the user's instruction. We first provide empirical evidence that CoT strongly governs action generation, even when it is semantically misaligned with the input instructions. Building on this observation, we propose TRAP, the first targeted behavior-hijacking adversarial attack against CoT-reasoning VLA models. By targeting the reasoning-to-action pathway, TRAP uses an adversarial patch (e.g., a tablecloth placed on the table) to steer intermediate CoT reasoning and downstream actions toward adversary-defined behaviors. Extensive evaluations on three representative reasoning VLAs, spanning distinct CoT reasoning mechanisms, demonstrate the effectiveness of TRAP. Notably, we implemented the patch by printing it on paper in a real-world setting. Our findings highlight the urgent need to secure CoT reasoning in VLA systems. The project page is available at https://zhengxian-huang.github.io/TRAP-website/.

</details>


### [47] [Black-box, Adaptive, Efficient, Transferable, Harmful, Applicable... Attacks Are All You Need to Break LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03647)
*Vincent Limbach,Jonas Dornbusch,David Lüdke,Stephan Günnemann,Leo Schwinn*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出间接危害优化（IHO）方法，利用掩码扩散语言模型通过迭代偏好优化训练，仅需黑盒访问即可高效评估LLM的越狱鲁棒性，在多种防御下优于现有方法，向标准化越狱评估迈出一步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像分类领域已有AutoAttack等标准化攻击用于鲁棒性评估，但LLM越狱评估缺乏等效的标准化攻击方法。已有方法无法同时满足黑盒兼容、适用于任意防御管线且高效的要求，导致鲁棒性评估不可靠，影响部署风险评估和防御比较。

Method: 提出间接危害优化（IHO），使用掩码扩散语言模型作为攻击者，通过迭代偏好优化（类似DPO）针对危害性评判器进行训练。仅需黑盒访问目标模型，同一方法既可作为单个行为的自适应攻击，也可作为泛化至未见行为和未见目标模型的高效摊销策略，无需微调。

Result: 在面对分层防御（如Circuit Breaker训练模型结合辅助检测器）时，IHO无需任何防御特定适配即可显著提升攻击成功率，优于现有最先进方法。方法在未见行为和未见目标模型上具有良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: IHO为LLM越狱评估提供了一种标准化、黑盒兼容、高效且泛化性强的攻击方法，有望填补该领域缺乏标准化评估基准的空白，提升鲁棒性评估的可靠性。

Abstract: Accurately evaluating adversarial robustness is a longstanding challenge. A flawed attack design can inflate robustness estimates, making deployment risk assessment and defense comparison unreliable. Historically, standardized attacks such as AutoAttack have largely resolved this for image classifiers, providing a reliable evaluation baseline for systematic comparison across defenses. However, no equivalent exists for LLM jailbreak evaluation yet, where designing such an attack is considerably more difficult. A reliable attack must, among other things, be black-box compatible, applicable to arbitrary defense pipelines, and efficient, which no existing method jointly satisfies. We introduce Indirect Harm Optimization (IHO), a masked diffusion language model attacker trained via iterative preference optimization against a harmfulness judge, requiring only black-box access to the target. The same method can be used without modification as a strong adaptive attack on individual behaviors, or as an efficient amortized policy that transfers to held-out behaviors and unseen target models without fine-tuning. Even against layered defenses, such as a Circuit Breaker-trained model combined with an auxiliary detector, IHO improves attack success considerably over state-of-the-art approaches, without any defense-specific adaptation. Our results position IHO as a practical step toward the kind of standardized jailbreak evaluation that has improved reliability in the past. Code and models are available on GitHub and Hugging Face.

</details>

### [48] [Human-in-the-Loop Contextual Bandits for Short-Term Rental Dynamic Pricing: Structural Equivalence of Historical Warm-Up and Approval-Gated Live Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02595)
*Oleg Miroshnichenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对短租市场中定价决策的高风险、可解释性需求及反馈稀疏问题，提出Human-in-the-Loop Gated Bandit框架，利用历史定价数据（结构上等价于在线策略预热数据）初始化Bandit后验，将冷启动从约150轮压缩至约30轮，并论证该策略可推广至任何需要人工审批的高风险领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短租市场动态定价面临三大挑战：定价决策财务风险高、运营商需要可解释性、市场反馈稀疏（每间夜仅一个预订结果）。纯在线Bandit学习的冷启动周期长达数周至数月，在实践中不可行。

Method: 提出HITL-GB框架，结合人类审批机制与上下文Bandit算法；证明历史定价数据（来自先验确定性策略）与在线策略预热数据在结构上等价；设计基于正则化岭回归的预热程序，从历史轮次中初始化Bandit后验分布。

Result: 在真实短租生产数据（匿名城市市场，2间房，2022年4月至2026年4月，1461个夜间定价轮次）上验证，预热程序将HF-TS系列智能体的有效冷启动从约150轮压缩至约30轮。

Conclusion: 该结构等价性结论是领域无关的：任何需要人工审批的高风险领域（如临床药物剂量、信贷审批、内容审核、放射诊断）均可受益于相同的预热策略。在受监管行业中，强制性人工监督非但不是部署约束，反而是统计资产。

Abstract: Dynamic pricing in short-term rental (STR) markets presents a distinctive challenge for online learning algorithms: pricing decisions carry significant financial risk, operators require explainability, and market feedback is sparse (one booking outcome per listed night). We introduce the Human-in-the-Loop Gated Bandit (HITL-GB) framework, in which a contextual bandit algorithm generates price recommendations but a human agent retains authority to accept, modify, or reject each recommendation before it is applied. We show that under this approval constraint, historical pricing data -- collected under a prior deterministic policy -- is structurally equivalent to on-policy warm-up data for initialising the bandit's posterior, bypassing the weeks-to-months cold-start period that renders pure online bandit learning impractical in sparse-feedback markets. We formalise the approval-gated reward signal, derive a regularised ridge-regression warm-up procedure from historical episodes, and validate the approach on real STR production data (anonymised urban market, 2 rooms, April 2022 -- April 2026, 1,461 nightly pricing episodes). Our warm-up procedure compresses effective cold-start from ~150 episodes to ~30 episodes when initialising agents from the Hierarchical Factored Thompson Sampling (HF-TS) family. We further argue that the structural equivalence result is domain-agnostic: any high-stakes domain where human approval is legally or operationally required -- including clinical drug dosing, credit origination, content moderation, and radiological diagnosis -- satisfies the same conditions and benefits from the same warm-up strategy. In regulated industries, mandatory human oversight is thus a statistical asset rather than a deployment constraint.

</details>


### [49] [RMPrior: Bridging Propagation Priors and Diffusion Refinement for Efficient Radio Map Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03074)
*Zixuan Guo,Xiucheng Wang,Nan Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种“中启动采样策略”，将传播模型先验注入扩散模型，通过从中间时间步而非纯噪声开始反向去噪，实现无线电台图构建的2.01倍加速，同时提升NMSE、RMSE、SSIM、PSNR等指标。理论分析给出了初始化间隙上界、截断提升重构保真度的充分条件，以及对先验质量的敏感性刻画。实验验证了重构质量可作为传播模型场景级保真度的代理指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型构建高保真电台图需大量采样步数，在动态无线系统中需频繁刷新，计算成本过高。同时，经典传播模型编码了有价值的场景级知识，但标准扩散推理从纯高斯噪声初始化，完全丢弃了这些先验信息。

Method: 提出中启动采样策略：首先用匹配的传播先验（如传播模型输出的电台图）替代纯噪声，将其前向加噪到中间扩散时间步，然后利用预训练的扩散骨干网络仅执行剩余的反向去噪步骤，将计算集中在多径效应感知的精细修正上，而非从头重构。

Result: 在IRT4HighRes数据集上，P_start=0.5时实现2.01倍加速，同时NMSE、RMSE、SSIM、PSNR全面优于全步基线。三种传播模型的先验质量消融实验表明，重构质量随先验质量变化，且敏感性在更短的反向轨迹下放大，与理论预测一致。

Conclusion: 中启动采样策略有效融合传播先验与扩散细化，大幅降低采样成本并提升重构质量。此外，中启动重构质量可作为传播模型场景级保真度的代理排序指标。

Abstract: Diffusion models achieve high-fidelity radio map construction through iterative denoising, yet their sampling cost limits practicality in dynamic wireless systems where radio maps must be refreshed repeatedly. Meanwhile, classical propagation models encode valuable scene-level knowledge that standard diffusion inference discards entirely by initializing from pure Gaussian noise. This paper bridges propagation priors and diffusion refinement through a mid-start sampling strategy. A matched propagation prior is perturbed to an intermediate diffusion timestep, and the pretrained diffusion backbone executes only the remaining reverse steps, focusing computation on multipath-aware refinement rather than full reconstruction from noise. We provide theoretical analysis establishing an upper bound on the initialization gap, a sufficient condition under which truncation improves reconstruction fidelity, and a formal characterization of prior-quality sensitivity under aggressive truncation. Experiments on IRT4HighRes show that, at $P_{\text{start}}=0.5$, the proposed method achieves a $2.01\times$ speedup while simultaneously improving NMSE, RMSE, SSIM, and PSNR over the full-step baseline. A prior-quality ablation across three propagation models of different fidelity confirms that reconstruction quality tracks prior quality, with the sensitivity amplified under shorter reverse trajectories, consistent with the theoretical predictions. These results also suggest that mid-start reconstruction quality can serve as a proxy for ranking the scene-level fidelity of different propagation models.

</details>


### [50] [Spectral Asymptotics of Neural Network Loss Landscapes: An Exact Decomposition of the Curvature Exponent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02596)
*Anherutowa Calvo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了谱对齐分解理论，解释了不同层类型中曲率指数α的变化规律，并推导出光谱传递恒等式s=αγ，通过实验验证了其准确性，最后开发了架构自适应预条件器T(σ;α)并在视觉基准测试中优于AdamW。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解为何不同网络层（卷积、注意力、MLP）的Hessian曲率指数α存在系统性差异（卷积约2，注意力约1，MLP上投影小于1）。

Method: 提出谱对齐分解：α = 2 + d logΦ_k / d logσ_k，将曲率指数变化归因于Kronecker因子特征基与梯度奇异方向之间的对齐程度Φ_k。推导光谱传递恒等式s = αγ，连接曲率指数、有效梯度秩衰减γ和Hessian衰减指数s。

Result: 在93个层、5种架构、3个数据集上，光谱传递恒等式以无参数方式预测s，中位误差约2%。ζ函数界证明曲率集中到每层约一个方向上。基于此开发的谱牛顿方法在α≈2的视觉基准上优于AdamW。

Conclusion: 曲率指数α的变化可通过Kronecker因子特征基与梯度奇异方向的对齐程度解释，光谱传递恒等式提供了跨架构的普适性关系，架构自适应预条件器在实际优化中有效。

Abstract: The curvature exponent $α$ in $h_k \propto σ_k^α$ -- governing how Hessian eigenvalues scale with gradient singular values -- varies systematically across layer types ($α\approx 2$ for convolutions, $\approx 1$ for transformer attention, $< 1$ for MLP up-projections). Why? We prove the Spectral Alignment Decomposition: $α= 2 + d\logΦ_k / d\logσ_k$, where $Φ_k$ measures alignment between Kronecker factor eigenbases and gradient singular directions. This reduces "why does $α$ vary?" to a geometric question we answer for LayerNorm, residual connections, and softmax heads. The decomposition implies a spectral transfer identity $s = αγ$ linking curvature exponent, effective gradient rank-decay $γ$, and Hessian decay exponent $s$. The identity is algebraic; its empirical content is that $α$ and $γ$, fit on independent data (HVPs vs. SVD), recover $s$ to ~2% median error across 93 layers, five architectures, and three datasets -- with no free parameters. A zeta-function bound on participation ratio shows curvature concentrates onto effectively one direction per layer. As a proof of concept, we derive the architecture-adaptive preconditioner $T(σ;α)$ and show that Spectral Newton -- implementing $T$ in the gradient singular basis -- outperforms AdamW on vision benchmarks where $α\approx 2$.

</details>


### [51] [Validation-Gated Multi-Agent Governance for Online Adaptation of Thermal-Hydraulic Surrogate Models under Operating-Regime Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03321)
*Doyeong Lim,Seungyoon Lee,In Cheol Bang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种受防护的持续自适应框架，用于实验热工水力回路数据预测。该框架包含角色分离的多智能体系统（监控、诊断、适配、安全审计、编排），通过确定性冠军-挑战者门控和背景影子学习控制模型替换。在七个代理族中，时间傅里叶神经算子被选为初始冠军。MA-Full模式（多智能体委员会评估每个流步骤）取得了最佳性能，MAE从静态部署的7.06降至5.72，警告超标率从56.8%降至35.8%，性能提升19.0%。验证了从神经算子到Transformer和图神经网络的晋升，表明该框架支持可审计的代理演化，同时确定性门控保留部署权威。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工智能代理离线训练并冻结后，一旦部署到预训练范围之外，可能陷入条件锁定状态，无法适应新工况。需要一种能够在部署后持续安全自适应的方法。

Method: 提出受防护的持续自适应框架：角色分离的多智能体系统（Monitor、Diagnosis、Adaptation、Safety-Auditor、Orchestrator）诊断误差特征、优先候选模型族、审查晋升；确定性冠军-挑战者门控和背景影子学习保留最终模型替换权。七种代理族通过三折交叉验证筛选，时间傅里叶神经算子为初始冠军，进行60秒历史到10秒轨迹预测。

Result: 静态部署MAE=7.06，警告超标率56.8%；规则自适应MAE=6.54；影子刷新接近静态；MA-Full模式MAE=5.72，超标率35.8%，性能提升19.0%。配对bootstrap区间排除零，但自适应模式间区间重叠。验证了从神经算子到Transformer和图神经网络的晋升。

Conclusion: 门控记录下的持续自适应框架支持可审计的代理演化，确定性门控保留部署权威，可在热工水力实验数据中实现安全有效的模型自适应更新。

Abstract: Artificial-intelligence surrogates can support second-by-second thermal-hydraulic forecasting, but models selected and frozen offline may become condition-locked once deployed outside their pretraining envelope. This study develops a guarded continual-adaptation framework for experimental thermal-hydraulic loop data in which role-separated agents - Monitor, Diagnosis, Adaptation, Safety-Auditor, and Orchestrator - diagnose error signatures, prioritize candidate model families, and review promotions, while deterministic champion-challenger gates and background shadow learning retain final authority over model replacement. Seven surrogate families were screened by blocked three-fold cross-validation, and a temporal Fourier neural operator was selected as the initial champion for 60-s-history-to-10-s-trajectory forecasting on two held-out transients, with three seeds per adaptive mode. Static deployment gave a channel-averaged MAE of 7.06 and a 56.8% warning-exceedance ratio; rule-based adaptation reduced MAE to 6.54, whereas shadow refresh alone remained close to Static. The MA-Full mode, in which the role-separated multi-agent council reviews every evaluated stream step, achieved the lowest mean error, 5.72, and 35.8% exceedance, corresponding to a 19.0% improvement over Static. Paired bootstrap intervals against Static excluded zero, although intervals among adaptive modes overlapped and the six paired units limit broad statistical claims. Validated promotions from the neural operator to Transformer and graph neural network indicate that logged, gate-controlled adaptation can support auditable surrogate evolution while deterministic gates retain deployment authority.

</details>


### [52] [Making Brain-Computer Interfaces More Secure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02597)
*Md Fahimul Kabir Chowdhury,Gahangir Hossain*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对EEG脑机接口系统的对抗鲁棒性问题，提出了一种轻量级CNN架构，在两种EEG数据集上对比EEGNet、DeepConvNet和SleepEEGNet三种模型，实验表明所提模型在对抗扰动下具有更好的分类性能和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EEG-BCI研究主要关注分类准确率提升，忽视了安全性与鲁棒性问题，而研究表明EEG-BCI容易受到对抗攻击导致误诊，因此亟需评估模型在对抗扰动下的鲁棒性以确保可靠部署。

Method: 提出一种轻量级自定义CNN架构，在两种EEG数据集上，与EEGNet、DeepConvNet、SleepEEGNet三种针对EEG的CNN模型进行对比，在基于梯度的对抗攻击场景下进行评估。

Result: 所提轻量级模型在对抗扰动下分类性能持续优于基线模型，表现出更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 轻量级架构在对抗条件下能有效提升EEG-BCI系统的可靠性，为开发更安全的BCI系统提供了可行方向。

Abstract: The development of brain-computer interfaces (BCIs) based on electroencephalograms (EEGs) has advanced significantly mainly to machine learning. Although the majority of earlier research has been on increasing classification accuracy, relatively little focus has been placed on security and robustness. According to recent research, EEG-based BCIs are susceptible to adversarial attacks, which can cause misdiagnosis due to minute, well-crafted disturbances. Evaluating model robustness against such perturbations is therefore critical for ensuring reliable deployment. In this study, we propose a lightweight custom Convolutional Neural Network (CNN) architecture to investigate adversarial robustness in EEG-based BCIs. The suggested method is assessed using two EEG datasets and contrasted with three novel CNN models tailored to EEG, namely EEGNet, DeepConvNet, and SleepEEGNet, under gradient-based adversarial attack scenarios. According to experimental findings, the suggested model continuously performs better in classification under adversarial perturbations compared to baseline models, indicating improved robustness. These findings highlight the potential of lightweight architectures for enhancing the reliability of EEG-based BCI systems under adversarial conditions.

</details>


### [53] [Assessing Region-Level EEG Contributions to Cognitive Workload Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02598)
*Jacob Wong,Sohan Singh,Prannaya Gupta,Jin Xing Ang,Kritika Johari,U-Xuan Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于区域级别的EEG认知负荷评估框架，在四个公开数据集上分析发现：额叶电极组在跨数据集和跨被试评估中表现优于全头皮基线（相对排名提升15-20%），额中央区域预测效用最稳定，而后部及枕叶区域贡献不一致。结果表明负荷相关EEG信息主要集中于额叶和额中央区域，支持设计高效、可泛化的EEG负荷监测系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG广泛用于认知负荷评估，但不同任务、数据集和被试间区域级别EEG贡献的一致性尚不清楚。现有研究缺乏跨数据集、跨实验配置的系统性区域重要性分析，限制了通用、高效EEG负荷监测系统的设计。

Method: 提出区域级评估框架：按解剖学头皮区域划分电极组，提取特征训练模型；采用模型无关的基于性能的方法量化区域重要性；在混合被试和被试独立两种评估协议下进行大规模跨数据集分析（4个公开数据集），并使用基于排名的策略聚合结果以保证鲁棒性。

Result: 额叶电极组在跨数据集和被试独立评估中相对排名比全头皮基线提升约15-20%；额中央区域预测效用最稳定；后部和枕叶区域在不同实验条件下贡献不一致。

Conclusion: 负荷相关的EEG信息最一致地保留在额叶和额中央电极组中，支持设计使用更少电极的高效、可泛化的EEG负荷监测系统。

Abstract: Accurate and generalizable estimation of cognitive workload from electroencephalography (EEG) is critical for human-centered and safety-critical systems. Although EEG is widely used for workload assessment, the consistency of region-level EEG contributions across tasks, datasets, and subjects remains unclear. This paper presents a region-level evaluation framework for EEG-based workload prediction in which models are trained and evaluated using features extracted exclusively from electrodes belonging to anatomically defined scalp regions. We perform a large-scale analysis across four publicly available EEG workload datasets spanning diverse task demands, recording hardware, and electrode montages. Region importance is quantified using a model-agnostic, performance-based approach under both mixed-subject and subject-independent evaluation protocols, with results aggregated using a rank-based strategy to ensure robustness across experimental configurations. Across all datasets and subject-independent evaluations, frontal electrode groups outperform the full-scalp baseline by approximately 15-20% in relative rank position while using substantially fewer electrodes. Fronto-central regions exhibit the most stable predictive utility, whereas posterior and occipital regions contribute less consistently across experimental conditions. These findings indicate that workload-relevant EEG information is most consistently retained within frontal and fronto-central electrode groups, supporting the design of efficient and generalizable EEG-based workload monitoring systems.

</details>


### [54] [Testing the Test: Score-Direction Instability in Class-Split Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02601)
*Alejandro Ascarate,Leo Lebrat,Rodrigo Santa Cruz,Clinton Fookes,Olivier Salvado*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了类分割异常检测评估协议在异常类与正常分布在表示空间重叠时失效的问题，提出无须训练的"邻域类泄漏"诊断指标，并在多个数据集与表示空间上验证了其预测能力，主张将类分割基准视为几何依赖性压力测试而非无条件的异常检测能力证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前广泛使用的类分割评估协议（在数据集内划分正常/异常类）作为无条件离群异常检测的代理指标，但该协议在异常类与正常分布在表示空间重叠时可能失效，分数可能趋于随机甚至反转，且最佳分数方向依赖于未知的异常类，因此亟需诊断和重新审视该评估协议的有效性。

Method: 提出一种无须训练的简单诊断指标——邻域类泄漏（neighborhood class leakage），用于检测异常类与正常分布在表示空间中的重叠程度；在Fashion-MNIST、CIFAR-10和Imagenette数据集上，分别在像素空间和VAE潜空间中进行实验验证。

Result: 邻域类泄漏指标能够有效预测异常检测分数方向的不稳定性，即当异常类与正常分布在表示空间中重叠时，分数趋于随机或反转。

Conclusion: 类分割异常检测基准不应被视为无条件的异常检测能力证据，而应视为依赖于数据几何结构的压力测试。

Abstract: Within-dataset class-split evaluation is widely used as a proxy for fully unconditional out-of-distribution anomaly detection. We show that this protocol can become ill-posed when the held-out anomaly class overlaps the normal mixture in representation space. In this regime, anomaly scores may collapse toward chance or even invert, and the preferred score direction can depend on the unknown anomaly class. We introduce a simple training-free diagnostic, neighborhood class leakage, and show that it predicts score-direction instability across Fashion-MNIST, CIFAR-10, and Imagenette, in both pixel and VAE latent spaces. Our results suggest that class-split AD benchmarks should be treated as geometry-dependent stress tests rather than unconditional evidence of anomaly-detection ability.

</details>


### [55] [Training a Predictive Coding Network on ImageNet using Equilibrium Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03584)
*Tugdual Kerjan,Rasmus Høier,Benjamin Scellier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次将基于平衡传播（EP）的训练方法应用于预测编码网络（PCN），并在完整ImageNet数据集上成功训练了一个10层卷积PCN（VGG10），实现了13.23%的top-5测试错误率，接近反向传播基线的12.2%，这是PCN和EP方法首次达到ImageNet规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平衡传播（EP）和预测编码网络（PCN）此前均局限于小规模问题，缺乏在大规模实际任务上的验证。本文旨在将EP应用于PCN，突破二者在规模上的限制。

Method: 将中心化变体平衡传播与针对PCN设计的新型平衡方案相结合，提出了一种基于EP的PCN训练方法。

Result: 在完整ImageNet上训练10层卷积PCN（VGG10），top-5测试错误率为13.23%，接近反向传播基线的12.2%。

Conclusion: 这是PCN和EP训练方法首次在ImageNet规模上得到验证，显著扩展了二者的可扩展性，并暗示EP在其他物理系统中扩展的主要挑战可能来自这些系统的计算特性，而非EP框架本身的固有限制。

Abstract: Equilibrium Propagation (EP) is a physics-based training framework that has primarily been employed in energy-based models, including continuous Hopfield networks, nonlinear resistive networks and coupled phase oscillators. However, EP's practical applications have so far remained limited to relatively small-scale problems. Predictive coding networks (PCNs), another class of energy-based models rooted in computational neuroscience, are typically trained with a specialized algorithm and have likewise not yet been demonstrated at large scale. In this work, we develop an EP-based training method for PCNs which combines the centered variant of EP with a novel equilibration scheme for PCNs. Using this approach, we train a 10-layer convolutional PCN (VGG10) on full-size ImageNet, achieving 13.23\% test error rate on the top-5 classification task, close to the 12.2\% backpropagation baseline. To our knowledge, this is the first demonstration of both PCNs and EP-based training at ImageNet scale. These results significantly extend the scalability of both approaches and suggest that the primary challenges in scaling EP in other physical systems may come more from the computational properties of these systems than from inherent limitations of the EP framework.

</details>


### [56] [Graph Mamba Survival Analysis Based on Topology-Aware ordering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02602)
*Yuanfang Chen,Peiqiang Yan,Yuntao Shou,Qian Zhao,Xiangyong Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TopoMamSurv框架，通过拓扑感知排序、双向Mamba模块和图卷积网络，在保持线性计算复杂度的同时有效建模WSI全局依赖和双向空间结构，在五个TCGA数据集上取得优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WSI生存分析中，Transformer的O(N²)复杂度造成计算瓶颈，而Mamba虽线性复杂度但对输入数据顺序高度敏感，传统节点排序方法未考虑图拓扑连接性，且单向架构无法利用图像双向空间结构。

Method: 提出拓扑感知排序(TAO)策略以适应Mamba的顺序敏感性；设计双向Mamba模块；集成GCN实现"局部聚合-全局捕获"的分层特征学习架构。

Result: 可视化实验证实TAO提取的节点具有更高相似度；在五个TCGA数据集上验证了框架的全面性能优势。

Conclusion: 通过TAO、双向语义建模和分层特征融合的系统设计，有效调和了WSI分析中长程依赖建模、计算效率和空间结构利用之间的矛盾。

Abstract: In computational pathology, Whole Slide Images (WSIs) survival analysis is crucial for patient prognosis assessment, but it faces multiple technical challenges. Although the Transformer captures long-range dependencies through its self-attention mechanism, its $O(N^2)$ time complexity causes a severe computational bottleneck in large-scale WSIs graph structures. The Mamba model breaks through the Transformer's computational bottleneck with linear complexity. But, owing to Mamba's high sensitivity to the order of input data, traditional node sorting methods in Graph Mamba, such as those based on node degree or subgraph size, fail to adequately account for the topological connectivity of graph data. This inadequacy consequently restricts the performance of Mamba's sequential modeling. Moreover, its unidirectional architecture cannot leverage the bidirectional spatial structure of images. To address these challenges, this paper proposes a novel Graph Mamba survival analysis framework based on topology-aware ordering (TopoMamSurv) to adapt to the sequential sensitivity of Mamba. Our visualization experiments further confirmed that the nodes extracted through the topology-aware ordering (TAO) strategy indeed exhibit higher similarity. Furthermore, we designed a bidirectional Mamba module and integrated a Graph Convolutional Network (GCN) to achieve bidirectional spatial context modeling of images, forming a hierarchical feature learning architecture for "local aggregation - global capture." This framework effectively reconciles the contradiction between long-range dependency modeling, computational efficiency, and spatial structure utilization in WSIs analysis through its systematic design of TAO, bidirectional semantic modeling, and hierarchical feature fusion. This framework has been validated for its comprehensive performance advantage on five TCGA datasets.

</details>


### [57] [Auditable Climate Risk Intelligence from Fragmented ESG Data: Deterministic Orchestration and Imbalance-Aware Learning for Scope 1-3 Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02604)
*Karan Sehgal,Khawar Naveed Bhatti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个确定性气候风险智能框架，整合单一数据源编排、时间异常检测、不平衡集成学习和可解释性治理，用于可审计的ESG验证，并在合成基准上评估了其优于传统方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ESG和气候风险数据在Scope 1/2/3报告环境中高度碎片化，传统验证流程缺乏溯源可审计性、隐藏漂移检测和可复现性治理机制。

Method: 框架包含时间漂移分析、SMOTE稀有事件优化、集成学习、溯源感知编排和TreeSHAP可解释性；并构建了基于GHG Protocol、PCAF和ISSB标准的合成ESG验证基准。

Result: 在分类指标（召回率、F1、ROC AUC）、校准指标（ECE、Brier分数）和治理导向的审计溯源完整性指标上，框架优于统计分类器、异常检测方法、时间序列基线和阈值系统。

Conclusion: 该框架将ESG报告重新定义为确定性的气候风险治理基础设施，支持可复现性、可解释性和可操作审计性。

Abstract: ESG and climate risk data remain fragmented across heterogeneous Scope 1, Scope 2, and Scope 3 reporting environments, while conventional validation pipelines lack provenance aware auditability, hidden drift detection, and reproducibility oriented governance. This paper proposes a deterministic climate risk intelligence framework integrating single source of truth orchestration, temporal anomaly detection, imbalance aware ensemble learning, and explainability oriented governance for auditable ESG validation. To support open reproducibility, we construct and release a synthetic ESG validation benchmark calibrated against publicly reported characteristics of the GHG Protocol, PCAF, and ISSB standards. The methodology incorporates temporal drift analysis, SMOTE based rare event optimization, ensemble learning, provenance aware orchestration, and TreeSHAP based interpretability for governance inspection and audit reconstruction. We evaluate the framework against statistical classifiers, anomaly detection methods, temporal forecasting baselines, and a threshold based system using classification metrics (recall, F1, ROC AUC), calibration metrics (ECE, Brier score), and a governance oriented audit trace completeness metric measuring the fraction of flagged anomalies for which a deterministic source to escalation provenance chain can be reconstructed. Results are reported as mean and standard deviation across stratified five fold cross validation with paired significance testing. The framework reframes ESG reporting toward deterministic climate risk governance infrastructure supporting reproducibility, explainability, and operational auditability.

</details>


### [58] [Cross-Modal Contrastive Learning of ECG and Angiography Representations for Severe Stenosis Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02605)
*Nikola Cenikj,Özgün Turgut,Alexander Müller,Alexander Steger,Jan Kehrer,Marcus Brugger,Daniel Rueckert,Philip Müller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出StenCE预训练框架，利用心电图（ECG）特征对冠状动脉狭窄进行风险分层，在无需有创检查的情况下实现早期诊断，并在多个狭窄严重程度阈值和ECG分类任务上取得一致性能提升，首次在严重狭窄分类上实现高性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冠状动脉狭窄是常见心血管疾病，严重未治疗病例有高心肌梗死风险。现有金标准冠脉造影存在有创、耗时、资源密集等局限，仅对高概率患者进行。无症状患者可能漏诊。ECG虽快速、廉价、无创且可常规采集，但缺乏可靠狭窄特异性信号，无法用于狭窄风险分层。

Method: 提出StenCE预训练框架，直接从ECG中提取特征进行患者风险分层，支持不同ECG编码器，可在不同狭窄严重程度阈值下进行评估。

Result: 在不同狭窄严重程度阈值和额外ECG疾病分类任务上，StenCE在不同ECG编码器上均实现一致性能提升，优于先前工作。成功检测到ECG中用于狭窄诊断的信号，首次在严重狭窄分类上达到高性能。

Conclusion: StenCE框架证明ECG可用于冠状动脉狭窄风险分层，支持早期诊断，特别是对无症状患者。代码已开源。

Abstract: Coronary artery stenosis is a common cardiovascular disease, with severe, untreated cases posing significant risks of heart attack. Although coronary (X-ray) angiograms remain the standard for stenosis diagnosis, they are invasive, time- and resource-intensive, and therefore only performed on patients with a high probability of disease based on symptoms and prior clinical tests. However, a subset of patients, especially those without symptoms, may remain undiagnosed. Detecting indications of stenosis from ECGs, which are fast, cheap, non-invasive, and thus routinely acquired even in asymptomatic patients, would support early diagnosis. However, as no reliable stenosis-specific signal has been identified in ECGs, they can not currently be used for stenosis risk stratification. To address this, we introduce StenCE, a pretraining framework, allowing stratification of patients based on features derived directly from ECGs. Evaluations across varying stenosis severity thresholds and additional ECG disease classification tasks demonstrate consistent performance improvements across different ECG encoders, outperforming previous work. The obtained models successfully detect signals for stenosis diagnosis in ECGs and are the first to achieve high performance in severe stenosis classification. The source code is available at https://github.com/NikolaCenic/ecg-stenosis-cls.

</details>


### [59] [ReLoRA: Knowledge-Reusing Adaptation for Fast Rollout of Evolving LLM Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02606)
*Yang Xu,Zihuai Xu,Hongli Xu,Yunming Liao,Zhiwei Yao,Xitong Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ReLoRA框架，通过自适应初始化和调度正则化微调，高效地将旧版LoRA适配器迁移到更新的基座LLM上，避免从头训练，实现8.9倍加速和4.6%精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为持续演化服务频繁更新基座模型，导致先前部署的任务LoRA适配器失效；从头重训所有适配器计算成本过高且延迟服务上线，而直接复用又因适配器-基座不兼容导致性能下降。

Method: 1) 自适应LoRA初始化：利用贝叶斯优化融合旧适配器知识和基座模型演化信息，构建兼容性感知的初始点；2) 带调度正则化的微调：先通过强正则化快速将适配器引导至高质量区域，再放松正则化进行任务特定精调。

Result: 相比基线方法，ReLoRA实现最高8.9倍的就绪时间加速，精度提升最高达4.6%。

Conclusion: ReLoRA有效地解决了基座模型演化场景下LoRA适配器的再适配问题，在保证/提升任务性能的同时显著降低计算开销和服务延迟。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as continuously evolving services, where frequent base-model updates may invalidate previously deployed task-specific Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) adapters. For service providers managing numerous downstream model services, retraining each LoRA adapter from scratch for every updated base model is computationally prohibitive and delays service rollout. Meanwhile, the simpler alternative, i.e., naively applying the original LoRA adapter to the updated base model, often leads to degraded service quality due to adapter-backbone incompatibility. To address this problem, we propose ReLoRA, a knowledge-reusing re-adaptation framework that efficiently restores service-ready LoRA adapters for evolving LLM services while preserving or improving task performance. Specifically, ReLoRA comprises two key optimization steps: 1) Adaptive LoRA initialization leverages Bayesian optimization to construct a compatibility-aware starting point by fusing information from both the previously deployed task adapter and the base model's evolution; 2) Fine-tuning with scheduled regularization first rapidly steers the adapter to a high-quality region via strong regularization, followed by relaxed regularization for task-specific refinement. This design enables rapid service-quality recovery with reduced re-adaptation overhead. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ReLoRA reduces time-to-readiness by up to 8.9$\times$ and improves accuracy by up to 4.6\% compared to baselines.

</details>


### [60] [Geometry-Aware Tabular Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02607)
*David Turtora Zagardo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GATD通过向扩散去噪器注入列间角度和长度作为输入与辅助目标，以显式关系监督提升表格数据合成质量，在10个数据集上以平均3.5倍更少参数取得SOTA，Shape/Trend误差分别降低27%/20%，并证明监督信号本身（而非额外输入或容量）是性能提升的关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格扩散模型依赖隐式机制捕捉列间关系，缺乏对列间几何结构的显式建模，导致合成数据的列相关性不足。

Method: 提出GATD，在扩散去噪器中引入列值差异计算的成对角度和长度作为额外输入和辅助预测目标；在MLP、GNN和Transformer三种架构上验证，并设计了消融实验分离监督信号与输入/容量的影响。

Result: MLP版本以平均3.5x更少参数在10数据集上取得8/10 Shape、7/10 Trend和9/10下游效用最优，Shape/Trend误差降27%/20%；几何监督可迁移至GNN和Transformer（27/30 Shape、25/30 Trend改善）；消融证实监督信号本身驱动增益。

Conclusion: 显式关系监督是一种可迁移的表格扩散归纳偏置，能以更低参数开销显著提升合成质量。

Abstract: Tabular synthesis is critical for privacy-preserving sharing and augmentation, yet diffusion models rely on implicit mechanisms to capture inter-column relationships. We introduce Geometry-Aware Tabular Diffusion (GATD), which augments tabular diffusion denoisers with pairwise angles and lengths computed from column value differences and used as inputs and auxiliary targets. Our MLP instantiation achieves state-of-the-art benchmark performance while using 3.5x fewer parameters on average (up to 25x for classification tasks): on ten datasets, it wins 8/10 Shape, 7/10 Trend, and 9/10 downstream utility (F1/RMSE), reducing Shape and Trend error by 27% and 20%. Default loss weights transfer to GNN and Transformer denoisers, improving Shape on 27/30 and Trend on 25/30 architecture-dataset cells. A matched ablation shows supervision (not extra inputs or capacity) drives the gain. This shows explicit relational supervision is a portable inductive bias for tabular diffusion.

</details>


### [61] [Pruning Deep Neural Networks via the Marchenko--Pastur Distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02608)
*Leonid Berlyand,Theo Bourdais,Houman Owhad,Yitzchak Shmalo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Marchenko-Pastur随机矩阵理论的深度神经网络剪枝方法，能够在极短的微调预算下保持模型精度，并通过理论保证给出确定性的数据路径证书。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有剪枝方法通常需要长时间的后剪枝重优化流程，计算成本高。本文旨在实现低微调预算下仍能保持精度的剪枝方法，并给出理论保障。

Method: 采用Marchenko-Pastur随机矩阵理论进行剪枝分析，提出了零预算剪枝、剪枝-恢复扩展以及加性L2正则化模型。利用MP边界的拟合给出逐层预算信号。实验涵盖ViT和CNN架构，结合2:4稀疏、ToMe等技术。
