This framework provides a representation-theoretic account of feature learning and characterizes a novel low-rank compression phenomenon for matrix-valued group representations. Moreover, for Abelian groups, we provide a complete population-level description: random initialization promotes uniform diversification across nontrivial representations and induces Haar-uniform phases, jointly approximating the indicator via a majority-vote mechanism. We further prove that both phase alignment and representation competition emerge with exponential convergence rates.

</details>


### [96] [CoughSense: Five-Class Respiratory Disease Classification via Whisper Encoder Fine-Tuning and Dual-Encoder Cross-Attention Fusion with Balanced Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02998)
*Nikhil Vincent*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CoughSense是一个基于Whisper编码器的咳嗽分析系统，能够将咳嗽录音分为健康、COVID-19、哮喘、支气管炎和肺炎五类。通过主动帧QKV注意力池化解决音频沉默稀释问题，结合多种训练策略处理类别不平衡和域偏移，在18,301条录音上达到82.3%的平衡准确率（双编码器模型85.4%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有咳嗽分析研究大多局限于二分类COVID-19检测，无法满足实际临床需求。需要一种能在消费级智能手机上区分多种呼吸系统疾病的实用工具。

Method: 使用OpenAI Whisper编码器作为预训练骨干网络，提出主动帧QKV注意力池化（限制注意力仅作用于前200个编码器token），结合WeightedRandomSampler、SpecAugment、Balanced Mixup、监督对比辅助损失、FiLM症状条件化、梯度反转域自适应等训练策略，并设计双编码器模型融合Whisper与OPERA-CT呼吸基础模型。

Result: CoughSense（Whisper-tiny，8.6M参数）五折交叉验证平衡准确率82.3%，宏F1为0.817，AUC为0.941。比EfficientNet-B2高11.1个百分点，比ViT高29.6个百分点。所有五类召回率超过74%，四类超过80%。双编码器模型达到85.4%平衡准确率。主动帧池化在消融实验中贡献最大（5.1个百分点）。

Conclusion: CoughSense展示了在有限音频输入下使用Whisper进行多类呼吸疾病分类的可行性。主动帧池化有效解决了Whisper长输入窗口导致的沉默稀释问题，可推广到其他短音频任务。多类区分能力和轻量级架构（8.6M参数）使其适合部署于消费级智能手机。

Abstract: Automated cough analysis offers a path to low-cost respiratory screening, but most existing work stops at binary COVID-19 detection. A practical tool needs to tell apart several respiratory conditions from one cough recording on a consumer smartphone. We present CoughSense, a system that sorts cough recordings into five classes. These are healthy, COVID-19, asthma or respiratory condition, bronchitis, and pneumonia. We aggregated 18,301 recordings from four public datasets (Coswara, CoughVID, Virufy, and the West China Hospital Pediatric Cough Dataset) and used the OpenAI Whisper encoder as a pretrained backbone for cough disease classification. The main contribution is active-frame QKV attention pooling, which restricts attention to the first 200 of 1500 encoder tokens. This avoids the silence-dilution problem that arises because a 3-second cough fills only 150 tokens of Whisper's 30-second input window. Other training parts handle the 19 to 1 class imbalance and the four-dataset domain shift. These include WeightedRandomSampler, SpecAugment, Balanced Mixup with forced minority pairing, a supervised contrastive auxiliary loss, FiLM symptom conditioning, and gradient-reversal domain adaptation. A dual-encoder model fuses Whisper with the OPERA-CT respiratory foundation model through cross-attention. CoughSense (Whisper-tiny, 8.6M parameters) reached 82.3 percent balanced accuracy on five-fold cross-validation (macro-F1 of 0.817, AUC of 0.941). It beat an ImageNet-pretrained EfficientNet-B2 by 11.1 points and a ViT trained from scratch by 29.6 points. All five classes passed 74 percent recall and four of five passed 80 percent. The dual-encoder model reached 85.4 percent balanced accuracy. Active-frame pooling is the largest single contributor across all ablation components at 5.1 points, which should help any short-audio task using Whisper as a backbone.

</details>


### [97] [How Quantization Changes Interpretable Features: A Sparse Autoencoder Analysis of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03002)
*Evan Duan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究量化对稀疏自编码器（SAE）特征忠实度的影响，发现特征退化是渐进的而非灾难性的，且任务指标（如困惑度）可能掩盖特征层面的损伤，量化与幅度剪枝对特征造成高度重叠的破坏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化模型通常仅通过困惑度或下游准确率来评估，但可解释性特征（如SAE特征）在量化后是否依然可靠未被检验，而安全审计和调控干预正日益依赖这些特征。

Method: 使用冻结的SAE作为固定测量基座，对全精度和RTN量化后的模型在相同token上的激活进行编码，通过Pearson相关系数量化每个特征的生存情况，扫描INT8到INT4位宽，在Pythia-70M和Gemma-2-2B上进行实验。

Result: 特征生存是渐进的：INT6时Pythia-70M上62.4%活跃特征存活，Gemma-2-2B上51.3%存活；非存活特征多为模糊而非完全破坏；生存性可仅从全精度统计量预测（AUC 0.92-0.97），峰值激活为最强预测因子；INT7在改善困惑度的同时破坏了18.7%特征；量化与幅度剪枝损伤的特征集高度重叠（Jaccard 0.79-0.86，Spearman 0.98）。

Conclusion: 行为层面的性能等同不足以说明可解释性发现能够迁移到量化部署中，需要在特征层面进行压缩审计。

Abstract: Quantization is a standard path to deploying large language models, and a quantized model is typically judged acceptable when its perplexity or downstream accuracy stays close to the full-precision original. Whether the model still computes in the same way, or whether the interpretable features identified in the full-precision model survive weight rounding, is rarely tested, even as safety audits and steering interventions increasingly rely on those features. We ask whether sparse autoencoder (SAE) features extracted from a dense full-precision model remain faithful once that model is quantized. Using a frozen SAE as a fixed measurement basis, we encode full-precision and round-to-nearest (RTN) quantized activations on identical tokens and quantify per-feature survival by Pearson correlation, sweeping bit-widths from INT8 to INT4 on Pythia-70M and Gemma-2-2B. We find that feature survival is graded: features degrade systematically rather than failing all at once, with 62.4 percent of active features surviving at INT6 on Pythia-70M and 51.3 percent surviving at INT6 on Gemma-2-2B, and with most non-survivors blurred rather than destroyed. Survival is predictable from full-precision statistics alone, with cross-validated AUCs of 0.92 to 0.97 and peak activation as the strongest marginal predictor. Critically, task metrics can miss this damage: on Gemma-2-2B, INT7 improves perplexity while degrading 18.7 percent of features. Finally, quantization and matched-perplexity magnitude pruning damage strongly overlapping feature sets, with Jaccard overlap of 0.79 to 0.86 and damage-score Spearman correlation of 0.98, suggesting a shared mode of compression-induced vulnerability. These results show that behavioral parity is insufficient evidence that interpretability findings transfer to quantized deployments, motivating feature-level audits of compression.

</details>


### [98] [Exact equivariance, kept through training, buys zero-shot generalisation across the symmetry group](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03003)
*Hongbo Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明，由等变编码器 $E$ 和等变预测器 $f$ 构建的隐空间世界模型，其训练损失天然具有对称性：当动力系统真实地携带群 $G$ 作用时，单步预测 relMSE 在整个群上精确不变，因此仅在部分位姿上拟合动力学即可数学确定整个轨道上的结果。实验在笔记本级别（CPU/MPS，全种子设定）端到端验证：该对称性在真实训练流程（Muon/AdamW + EMA + VICReg）中存活（残差 $\sim 10^{-6}$），单步误差在群上平坦至五位小数，而同类假设的非等变基线在分布外性能差 $13.8\times$（2D）至 $157\times$（SE(3)），且等变模型小 $4.5$-$7.4\times$。闭环控制中，在匹配等变规划器下，控制轨迹在群上精确等变，闭环误差在群上不变（2D/SO(2) 浮点精确，3D/SE(3) 统计平坦）。多步 rollout 等变精确保持（$\times 1.00$），而基线残差随 $H$ 累积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用世界模型中的物理对称性（群作用）来提升样本效率和泛化能力，实现"举一反三"——仅需在部分位姿上学习即可泛化到整个对称群轨道。

Method: 构建等变编码器 $E$ 和等变预测器 $f$ 组成的隐空间世界模型，利用群表示 $\rho(g)$ 保证单步预测损失的群不变性；闭环中匹配等变规划器使控制轨迹自然等变。实验对比非等变基线，验证了优化后对称性存活、分布外泛化优势、闭环不变性和 rollout 稳定性。

Result: 等变模型单步误差群上平坦至五位小数；非等变基线分布外误差放大 $13.8\times$（2D）至 $157\times$（SE(3)），模型大小却大 $4.5$-$7.4\times$；闭环误差在 2D 浮点精确、在 3D 统计平坦；多步 rollout 等变误差恒定（$\le 2\times 10^{-7}$），基线残差随步数累积。

Conclusion: 等变性是世界模型中一种强归纳偏好，能在不依赖数据增强或暴力缩放的情况下实现精确的泛化对称性，且这一特性在优化、闭环控制和多步 rollout 中均稳定保持。

Abstract: A latent world model built from an equivariant encoder $E$ and an equivariant predictor $f$ inherits a provable symmetry of its training loss: when the world's dynamics genuinely carries a group $G$ acting on latents by an orthogonal representation $ρ(g)$, the one-step prediction relMSE is exactly invariant across the whole group, so fitting the dynamics on a restricted slice of orientations mathematically determines it on the entire orbit (jǔ yī fǎn sān). We verify this end-to-end at laptop scale (CPU/MPS, fully seeded). [A] The symmetry survives a real Muon/AdamW + EMA + VICReg run -- composed encode-then-predict residual $\sim 10^{-6}$ after optimisation, not just at initialisation, and under any optimiser. [B] One-step error is flat to five digits across the group, while a same-hypothesis-class non-equivariant baseline fits the slice but breaks out-of-distribution (VN $\times 1.00$ vs baseline $\times 13.8$ in 2D, $\times 17.2$ in 3D, $\times 157$ over the full $\mathrm{SE}(3)$ ladder), with the equivariant model $4.5$-$7.4\times$ smaller. [C] The same isometry argument lifts to closed loop: under a matching equivariant planner the control trajectory at orientation $g$ is exactly $ρ(g)$ applied to the seen one, so closed-loop error is invariant across the group -- float-floor-exact in 2D/$\mathrm{SO}(2)$ on real PushT and statistically flat in 3D/$\mathrm{SE}(3)$ (disjoint 95% CIs). We stress-test the prior against Sutton's Bitter Lesson: augmentation, brute-force scale, and soft-equivariance each close at most the across-group task metric, never the float-floor exactness. Because equivariance is closed under composition, the $H$-fold rollout stays flat ($\times 1.00$, $\le 2\times 10^{-7}$) at every horizon, while the baseline's residual compounds with $H$. Out of scope: task-success sweeps, planner-free invariance, and scaling.

</details>


### [99] [MOSAIC: Efficient Mixture-of-Agent Scheduling via Adaptive Aggregation and Inference Concurrency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03014)
*Saptarshi Mitra,Yifan Zhang,Rachid Karami,Phyo Pyae Moe Aung,Nazmul Takbir,Sreetama Sarkar,Souvik Kundu,Sitao Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MOSAIC是一个针对混合专家系统（MoA）的调度框架，通过ILP优化专家放置与任务分配，以及基于置信度的自适应聚合，在4-GPU系统上实现了最高2.5x专家阶段、4.23x聚合阶段和1.7~2.3x端到端加速，同时精度损失不超过0.1个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoA系统在有限GPU资源上执行时，基于技能的专家路由导致负载不均衡，且指令微调模型与长推理模型的组合造成生成长度极端差异，传统调度策略出现GPU空闲和吞吐量崩溃问题。

Method: 提出MOSAIC框架：1）基于整数线性规划（ILP）的调度器，联合优化专家放置和任务分配，在worker间复制推理专家并固定轻量级专家；2）置信度感知的自适应聚合，利用专家间共识跳过繁重的最终聚合器LLM。

Result: 在4-GPU系统上，MOSAIC相比基线调度器实现专家阶段最高2.5x加速、聚合阶段最高4.23x加速、端到端1.7~2.3x加速，精度匹配在0.1个百分点以内。

Conclusion: MOSAIC有效解决了MoA工作负载中的负载不均衡问题，显著提升吞吐量并减少GPU空闲，同时保持任务精度。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Agents (MoA) systems improve reasoning accuracy by routing each query to multiple expert LLMs and aggregating their outputs. Efficiently executing this workload on limited GPU resources has bottlenecks. Skill-based routing creates skewed expert demand, and combining instruction-tuned LLMs with long-reasoning models results in extreme variability in generation lengths. Consequently, traditional scheduling strategies suffer from significant GPU idling and throughput collapse due to load imbalances. We present MOSAIC, a scheduling framework to accelerate MoA workloads. First, we formulate an Integer Linear Program (ILP) based scheduler that jointly optimizes expert placement and per-worker prompt assignment from offline-profiled costs, replicating reasoning experts across workers while pinning lightweight ones. Second, MOSAIC uses confidence-aware adaptive aggregation, leveraging inter-expert agreement to bypass the heavy final aggregator LLM for consensus queries. In our 4-GPU system, MOSAIC achieves up to 2.5x expert-stage, 4.23x aggregator-stage and 1.7~2.3x end-to-end speedups over the baseline scheduler, while matching accuracy within 0.1pp.

</details>


### [100] [FLIPS: Instance-Fingerprinting for LLMs via Pseudo-random Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03330)
*Gurvan Richardeau,Gohar Dashyan,Erwan Le Merrer,Gilles Tredan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出面向监管的实例级指纹识别方法FLIPS，通过检测LLM在生成二进制随机序列中的偏差，实现对同一模型不同配置（提示词、采样参数、量化方式等）的区分，在237个模型实例上达到96%（封闭集）和90%（开放集）识别准确率，远优于基线方法（35%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM指纹识别技术聚焦知识产权保护，设计上追求对实例级参数（指令提示、采样配置、量化等）变化的鲁棒性，但模型在不同配置下行为差异显著（如安全/有毒输出）。AI监管需要评估实际部署行为而非模型来源，现有方法无法满足这一合规需求。

Method: 提出FLIPS方法，利用LLM在生成二进制随机序列时存在的统计偏差（如对0或1的偏好）来构建实例级指纹，通过分析模型输出序列的偏倚模式区分同一模型的不同配置实例。

Result: 在237个模型实例上，封闭集识别准确率达96%，开放集（存在未知目标）识别准确率达90%，而改编的LLMmap基线仅35%。

Conclusion: 实例级指纹识别对AI监管既必要又可行，FLIPS方法能有效区分同一LLM的不同配置，为合规性评估提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Literature reveals that a Large Language Model's (LLM) behavior is not only conditioned by its original weights but also its instance-level parameters, such as instructional prompt, sampling configuration or quantization. A model that generates safe outputs under one configuration may produce toxic content under another. However, current LLM identification techniques (such as fingerprinting) focus on intellectual property protection, and their design favors robustness to changes in these instance-level parameters. This poses a critical challenge for AI regulation in which compliance assessments target actual deployed behaviors, not model provenance. In this paper, we introduce instance-level fingerprinting, a regulator-oriented paradigm that distinguishes configurations of the same LLM. Our method FLIPS, exploits biases in generated binary random sequences to reach 96% (closed-set) and 90% (open-set, where some targets are unknown) identification accuracy across 237 model instances, versus 35% for the adapted LLMmap baseline. This shows that instance-level fingerprinting is both necessary for regulation and practically feasible. Code available at https://github.com/GurvanR/FLIPS-LLM-Instance-Fingerprinting.

</details>


### [101] [ConTraIRL: Factorized Contrastive Abstractions for Transferable IRL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03017)
*Yikang Gui,Bikramjit Banerjee,Prashant Doshi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ConTraIRL通过分解潜在表征（动态与目标）实现组合奖励转移，在未见过的动态-目标组合场景下提升逆向强化学习的样本效率和奖励恢复能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统IRL在策略需泛化到未见过的环境动态与任务目标组合时，奖励转移不可靠。

Method: 提出ConTraIRL框架，采用双编码器架构（动态编码器+目标编码器），通过双重对比学习目标训练：时间对齐使动态编码器学习目标不变结构，目标编码器捕获动态不变特征，实现因子分解。

Result: 在连续控制基准上，ConTraIRL在未见过的动态-目标配对场景下实现有效的少样本转移，显著提升样本效率和奖励恢复效果。

Conclusion: 分解动态与目标的潜在表征能够支持组合奖励转移，优于现有转移IRL基线方法。

Abstract: Reward transfer in Inverse Reinforcement Learning (IRL) is unreliable when policies must generalize to unseen combinations of environment dynamics and task goals. We propose Factorized Contrastive Abstractions for Transferable IRL (ConTraIRL), a framework that enables compositional reward transfer by learning decoupled latent representations of these two factors. ConTraIRL uses a dual-encoder architecture that maps observations into separate dynamics and goal latent spaces, trained with a dual contrastive objective. Temporal alignment encourages the dynamics encoder to learn goal-invariant structure, while the goal encoder captures dynamics-invariant features. This factorization supports reward inference under recombined dynamics-goal settings. Experiments on continuous control benchmarks demonstrate effective few-shot transfer to unseen dynamics-goal pairings, improving sample efficiency and reward recovery over transfer IRL baselines.

</details>


### [102] [PURGE: Projected Unlearning via Retain-Guided Erasure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03808)
*Vedant Jawandhia,Daksh Ahuja,Ghufran Alam Siddiqui,Prashant Trivedi,Yash Sinha,Pratik Narang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PURGE算法，利用持续学习与机器遗忘的对偶性，通过梯度投影约束保留集损失、多层表示擦除以及保留混淆目标，实现高效且难以被成员推理攻击检测的数据遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续学习（CL）与机器遗忘（MU）本质上是具有相同底层张力但方向相反的对偶问题，现有方法未能充分利用这一观察。

Method: 1) 借鉴A-GEM的梯度投影，约束每一步遗忘不增加保留集损失；2) 多层表示擦除，将遗忘集中间层激活推向保留集分布；3) 使用保留混淆目标（而非均匀分布）作为遗忘目标，使模型难以与从头重训练的模型区分；4) 两个自调节停止准则（保留损失预算和遗忘准确率目标）。

Result: 在5个数据集、22个类级别遗忘任务上，保留准确率保持在96%以上，MIA AUROC接近0.5（理想值），在隐私-效用前沿上优于梯度上升、KL均匀分布及其他基线方法。

Conclusion: PURGE通过利用CL与MU的对偶性以及精心设计的混淆目标，实现了高效、可自终止且隐私保护强的机器遗忘。

Abstract: We propose PURGE, a machine unlearning algorithm built on a simple but an under-exploited observation: continual learning (CL) and machine unlearning (MU) which are fundamentally dual problems. CL tries to learn new tasks without forgetting old ones; MU tries to erase specific data without hurting retained performance representing the same underlying tension in opposite directions. PURGE leverages this duality by adapting gradient projection from A-GEM (Chaudhry et al., 2019) so that every unlearning step is constrained to not increase the retain-set loss. On top of this, it performs multi-layer representation erasure, pushing forget-set activations in intermediate layers towards the retain distribution to remove information from hidden representations rather than just suppressing it at the output. A key design choice is the retain-confusion target: rather than pushing forget outputs toward the uniform distribution, which we found to be surprisingly easy for membership inference attacks to detect, we instead target the model's natural confusion pattern on retain data. This makes the unlearned model hard to distinguish from one retrained from scratch. Two self-regulating stopping criteria (a retain-loss budget and a forget-accuracy target) let the algorithm decide on its own when to stop, removing the need for manual epoch tuning. In experiments on five datasets (CIFAR-10, MNIST, SVHN, STL10, PathMNIST) across 22 class-level forgetting tasks, PURGE consistently keeps retain accuracy above 96% while achieving MIA AUROC close to 0.5 (the ideal), outperforming gradient ascent, KL-uniform, and several published baselines on the privacy-utility frontier.

</details>


### [103] [Will Accurate Fields Mislead Photonic Design? FromGlobal Accuracy to Port Readout](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03038)
*Yitian Zhang,Yonghong chen,Youming Chen,Yiyang Li,Xing Zhe,Renhe Lu,Shaolin Liao,Yuzhe Ma,Zhong Guan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出神经场代理模型在光子设计中的局限性：全局场误差低不代表输出端口读数准确。作者提出传播对齐神经算子PaNO及其增强版PaNO-R2，通过分离场误差、传播剖面误差和输出窗口误差来提升端口功率预测精度。在3×3 MMI分束器基准上，PaNO将端口功率误差从0.2018降至0.0739，PaNO-R2进一步降低72.7%的端口功率误差和72.5%的输出剖面误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统神经场代理模型在光子器件设计（如MMI分束器、耦合器）中，仅关注全局场误差可能导致对候选器件的错误排序，因为最终设计决策依赖于局部输出端口读数（功率、分束比、相位等），而非平均场相似度。现有方法忽略了从场预测到端口读数的传播-聚合链中的误差累积问题。

Method: 提出PaNO（Propagation-aligned Neural Operator）：保留全场预测接口，但将隐态组织为局部边界结构、横向模态内容、轴向传播和跨模态交互。进一步提出PaNO-R2，在端口区域附近引入输出感知反馈来修正残余场分量。采用Field/Mediator/Readout三视图框架，分别量化密集复场误差、传播剖面误差和输出窗口误差。

Result: 在15波长可调3×3 MMI基准（4608个未见过场）上：PaNO将NeurOLight的端口功率误差从0.2018降至0.0739（虽cMAE略高）；PaNO-R2取得最佳cMAE、传播剖面误差、输出剖面误差和端口功率误差，分别降低NeurOLight端口功率误差72.7%和输出剖面误差72.5%。

Conclusion: 全局场精度不足以保障设计相关的输出读数保真度；通过显式建模传播路径和输出窗口聚合误差，传播对齐的神经算子能显著提升端口级预测可靠性。

Abstract: Neural field surrogates can accelerate photonic design loops, but a surrogate that looks accurate in global field error can still mis-rank candidate devices when the final decision depends on localized output-port readouts. This risk is acute in propagation-dominated MMI splitters and couplers, where port power, splitting, phase, and coupling are determined by accumulated modal interference and output-window aggregation rather than by average field similarity alone. We study this field-to-design mismatch through a Field/Mediator/Readout view that separates dense complex-field error from propagation-profile and output-window errors before port aggregation. To align the surrogate with this chain, we propose PaNO, a propagation-aligned neural operator that keeps the full-field prediction interface while organizing latent states around local boundary structure, transverse modal content, axial propagation, and cross-mode interaction. We also evaluate PaNO-R2, an output-aware feedback variant for residual field components near the port region. On a 15-wavelength tunable $3{\times}3$ MMI benchmark with 4608 held-out fields, PaNO lowers NeurOLight's port-power error from 0.2018 to 0.0739 despite slightly higher cMAE, showing that global field accuracy alone is not sufficient for design-relevant readout fidelity. PaNO-R2 attains the best cMAE, propagation-profile error, output-profile error, and port-power error, reducing NeurOLight's port-power and output-profile errors by 72.7\% and 72.5\%.

</details>


### [104] [What Do Students Learn? A Feature-Level Analysis of Dark Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03052)
*Seungu Kang,Songkuk Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过交互张量框架分析知识蒸馏中学生的特征学习机制，发现有效KD相当于一个正则化器，能修剪低频、样本特定的特征。基于此洞察，提出Confusion Distillation (CD)——一种无教师自蒸馏方法，利用模型自身演化中的混淆模式作为动态软目标，在CIFAR-100上优于CS-KD和PS-KD 1.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识蒸馏已被广泛用于模型压缩，但学生模型如何获取特征表示的确切机制尚未被充分探索。现有方法缺乏对蒸馏过程中特征学习本质的理解。

Method: 1) 使用Interaction Tensor框架分析学生特征学习；2) 发现有效的KD会修剪低频、样本特定特征，促进学生依赖紧凑的高复用特征集；3) 观察到数据集级别的混淆矩阵包含类似教师"暗知识"的结构信息；4) 提出Confusion Distillation (CD)，利用模型自身演化中的混淆模式作为动态软目标进行无教师自蒸馏。

Result: 在CIFAR-100上使用ResNet-34和ResNet-50进行实验，CD方法优于CS-KD和PS-KD等现有自蒸馏方法1.2%，同时提供比标准KD更高效的计算方案。

Conclusion: CD作为一种无教师的自蒸馏方法，通过利用模型自身的混淆模式作为动态软目标，在性能上超越现有自蒸馏方法，且计算成本更低，为知识蒸馏提供了新的理解和高效替代方案。

Abstract: Knowledge Distillation (KD) is a powerful tool for model compression, yet the precise mechanisms by which student models acquire feature representations remain underexplored. In this work, we analyze student feature learning using the Interaction Tensor framework. Our analysis reveals that effective KD acts as a regularizer that prunes low-frequency, sample-specific features, encouraging the student to rely on a compact set of highly reusable features. Crucially, we observe that the dataset-level confusion matrix contains structural information analogous to the teacher's "Dark Knowledge." Leveraging this insight, we propose Confusion Distillation (CD), a teacher-free self-distillation method that utilizes the model's own evolving confusion patterns as dynamic soft targets. CD achieves competitive performance on ResNet-34 and ResNet-50 for CIFAR-100, outperforming existing self-distillation methods like CS-KD and PS-KD by 1.2% while offering a computationally efficient alternative to standard KD.

</details>


### [105] [Rethinking Molecular Text Representations for LLMs: An Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03057)
*Arun Raja,Garrett M. Morris,Kian Ming A. Chai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了16个LLM在9种分子表示和8项化学任务上的表现，发现性能高度依赖于表示方式：结构化文本（CML、MolJSON）在结构任务上最优，IUPAC在语义任务上最优，SMILES虽在预训练中常见但很少是最优选择。化学专用LLM在SMILES上表现好但在结构化文本上大幅退化。建议采用任务感知的表示路由策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地被用于分子任务，但使用哪种分子表示方式效果最佳尚不明确，缺乏系统性的基准评估。

Method: 构建系统基准，涵盖9种分子表示、8项化学任务，评估16个LLM（含推理/非推理变体、化学专用LLM及闭源前沿模型），并通过token化审计、线性探针和注意力机制进行机制分析。

Result: 性能高度依赖表示方式，无单一表示在所有任务上最优；CML整体最佳，其次是MolJSON、InChI和规范SMILES。结构化文本在结构任务上占优，IUPAC在语义任务上全面领先，SMILES变体很少最优。化学专用LLM在SMILES上表现好但在结构化文本上退化严重。

Conclusion: 分子表示方式对LLM性能影响显著，不应假设表示方式无关；建议采用任务感知的表示路由策略来优化LLM在化学任务中的应用。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for molecular tasks, but it remains unclear which molecular representation to use. We present a systematic benchmark evaluating LLM molecular competence across nine representations and eight chemical tasks. We benchmark 16 LLMs across five model families, including reasoning and non-reasoning variants, chemistry-specialized LLMs, and closed frontier models. Performance is strongly representation-dependent and no single representation wins across tasks, though CML is the best, followed by MolJSON, InChI, and then canonical SMILES. Explicit structured text representations (CML and MolJSON) dominate structural tasks; IUPAC dominates semantic tasks, winning molecule retrieval for all 16 LLMs; and SMILES variants are rarely optimal despite their prevalence in pretraining. Chemistry-specialized models perform well with SMILES at the cost of large degradations with structured text representations, suggesting SMILES-only evaluation rewards specialization that does not generalize. Using LLM-as-a-judge, we find that IUPAC produces the highest fraction of correct molecule generations. A mechanistic study via tokenization audits, linear probes and attention shows that representations are encoded differently inside the model; for example, structured representations require higher attention across the molecular span. Our results argue against representation-invariant evaluation and motivate task-aware representation routing for LLM-based chemistry.

</details>


### [106] [Learn When and Where to Connect: Adaptive Virtual Nodes for Dynamic Message Passing on Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03068)
*Jaejun Lee,Joyce Jiyoung Whang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MAVN框架，在消息传递神经网络中通过端到端可微的方式自适应地动态引入虚拟节点，允许节点与虚拟节点之间无约束连接，并通过双视角评分机制在每个层选择必要的虚拟节点，理论上可模拟任意节点-虚拟节点连接模式，在9个数据集上最高提升46.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于虚拟节点的方法存在限制：所有节点连接到相同数量的虚拟节点、连接在应用MPNN前固定、节点连接虚拟节点时独立于其他节点，无法适应节点表示的演化。

Method: 提出MAVN框架：1) 端到端可微的节点-虚拟节点无约束连接；2) 根据节点表示的演化动态决定何时（哪一层）和何处（哪些节点）引入虚拟节点；3) 双视角评分机制同时捕捉节点对虚拟节点的偏好和虚拟节点对节点的偏好；4) 从候选池中每层选择必要虚拟节点。

Result: 在9个真实数据集上，MAVN持续提升骨干MPNN的性能，最高提升46.5%，并超越所有基线方法。

Conclusion: MAVN通过动态自适应地引入虚拟节点连接，克服了现有VN方法的局限性，理论上能模拟任意节点-VN连接模式，实验证明了其有效性和泛化能力。

Abstract: While Virtual Nodes (VNs) are often utilized in Message Passing Neural Networks (MPNNs) to facilitate effective message passing, existing VN-based methods have limitations, such as constraining all nodes to connect to the same number of VNs, fixing the connections before applying MPNNs, and connecting a node to a VN independently of the other nodes that connect to the same VN. We propose MAVN, an end-to-end differentiable MPNN framework that allows non-constrained connections between nodes and VNs and dynamically introduces VNs on demand in response to evolving node representations across layers. Specifically, MAVN learns to adaptively determine when (at which layer) and where (to which nodes) to introduce and connect VNs based on the relative importance of connections. From a pool of candidate VNs, MAVN selects the necessary VNs in each layer, where each selected VN is connected to a nonempty subset of nodes, guided by a dual-perspective scoring mechanism that jointly captures the nodes' preferences for VNs and the VNs' preferences for nodes. We theoretically prove that for any node-VN connectivity pattern, there exists a set of MAVN's parameters that can simulate the pattern. Experiments on nine real-world datasets demonstrate that MAVN consistently improves the performance of backbone MPNNs, achieving up to 46.5% improvement over the backbones and outperforms the baselines.

</details>


### [107] [ASymPO: Asymmetric-Scale Policy Optimization for Asynchronous LLM Post-Training Without Behavior Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03070)
*Zehua Liu,Yuxuan Yao,Xiaojin Fu,Tao Zhong,Mingxuan Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Asymmetric-Scale Policy Optimization (ASymPO)，通过当前策略的token负对数概率归一化，解决异步强化学习中策略漂移导致的尺度不平衡问题，无需行为策略概率即可稳定训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异步强化学习能提升语言模型后训练吞吐量，但陈旧响应会导致分布漂移。现有行为校正方法依赖行为策略概率（需令牌对齐、版本一致），成本高昂。作者探索是否仅用当前策略概率即可稳定异步组相对强化学习。

Method: 1. 识别尺度不平衡失效模式：陈旧响应当前策略评估时，正负损失项处于不同负对数概率尺度，零和优势无法平衡损失。2. 提出ASymPO：每个响应的token损失除以其当前平均token负对数概率。3. 提出SPO基线（固定负缩放）。4. 在异步数学推理后训练中评估。

Result: ASymPO无需行为策略概率，恢复响应级零和平衡，保留非零学习信号。在异步数学推理后训练中验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 仅用当前策略概率即可稳定异步组相对强化学习，ASymPO通过归一化机制解决尺度不平衡问题，为异步RLHF提供了更简洁高效的方案。

Abstract: Asynchronous reinforcement learning can improve language-model post-training throughput by decoupling response generation from policy optimization, but stale responses introduce distribution drift. Standard behavior-corrected methods control this drift with behavior-policy probabilities, importance ratios, or clipping, which requires token-aligned, versioned, and numerically consistent behavior log-probabilities across rollout and learner systems. We ask whether asynchronous group-relative RL can instead be stabilized using only current-policy probabilities. We identify a scale-imbalance failure mode: when stale responses are evaluated under the current policy, positive and negative loss terms can appear at different negative log-probability scales, so zero-sum advantages no longer imply balanced loss contributions. We propose Asymmetric-Scale Policy Optimization (ASymPO), which normalizes each response's token loss by its current average token negative log-probability. ASymPO requires no behavior-policy probabilities, restores response-level zero-sum balance, and preserves a nonzero learning signal. We also introduce Scaled Policy Optimization (SPO), a fixed negative-scaling baseline, and evaluate both current-policy-only objectives in asynchronous mathematical reasoning post-training.

</details>


### [108] [Efficient Hyperparameter Optimization for LLM Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03073)
*Minping Chen,Bowen Xiao,Du Liang,Chuxuan Zeng,Zeyi Wen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出联合保真度超参数优化（JF-HPO），通过同时调整模型大小和训练预算作为保真度，显著提升LLM强化学习中的超参数优化效率，在相同时间预算下实现高达14.9倍的计算效率提升和5.8%-111.6%的性能改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的强化学习对超参数配置高度敏感，现有超参数优化方法因模型规模庞大和训练周期长而效率低下。

Method: 提出JF-HPO方法，核心创新包括：(i)使用目标LLM的小型代理模型进行高效训练和评估；(ii)基于训练动态设计早停策略；(iii)引入高效检查点机制消除冗余计算。核心思路是同时将模型大小和训练预算作为联合保真度进行自适应调整。

Result: 每轮HPO试验计算效率提升高达14.9倍；相同时间预算下取得更优或相当的预测精度；相比VeRL Recipe，性能提升5.8%-111.6%。

Conclusion: JF-HPO通过创新性地联合调整模型规模和训练预算两种保真度维度，有效解决了LLM强化学习场景下超参数优化的计算瓶颈问题。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) for large language models (LLMs) is highly sensitive to hyperparameter configurations, making hyperparameter optimization (HPO) essential yet computationally expensive. Existing multi-fidelity HPO methods remain inefficient for LLM RL due to the massive model scale and resource-intensive training cycles. In this paper, we propose Joint Fidelity Hyperparameter Optimization (JF-HPO), which simultaneously adapts both model size and training budget as fidelity. JF-HPO is empowered by: (i) it leverages a small proxy model of the target LLM for efficient training and evaluation in each HPO trial; (ii) it integrates carefully designed early-stopping strategies based on training dynamics; (iii) it introduces an efficient checkpointing mechanism to eliminate redundant computations. Compared with existing HPO methods, JF-HPO significantly improves the computational efficiency of each trial (up to 14.9 times), while achieving better or competitive predictive accuracy under the same time budget. Notably, compared with utilizing hyperparameter configurations from the VeRL Recipe, JF-HPO delivers performance improvements ranging from 5.8% to 111.6%.

</details>


### [109] [Libra: Efficient Resource Management for Agentic RL Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03077)
*Kaiwen Chen,Xin Tan,Jingzong Li,Hong Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Libra提出了一种针对大语言模型智能体强化学习中长尾、非平稳工作负载的弹性资源调度系统，通过周期性全局资源规划器和因果驱动的多级反馈队列，在48张A800 GPU上实现了最高3.0倍的吞吐量提升和2.5倍的收敛加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在智能体强化学习中，rollout阶段因工具调用产生长尾分布和非平稳的工作负载，导致：1) 少数长轨迹主导rollout完成时间；2) rollout与训练在计算模式、内存需求和序列长度敏感性上存在严重不对称；3) 随着RL策略更新，轨迹长度分布发生漂移，静态资源分配持续退化。

Method: Libra包含两个核心机制：1) 周期性全局资源规划器，在rollout和训练集群间联合优化GPU分配，利用弹性混合池实现轻量级、非阻塞的worker重分配；2) 因果驱动的多级反馈队列调度器，基于工具返回结果的因果信号（而非脆弱的长尾预测）将请求路由到异构rollout桶中。

Result: 在48张A800 GPU上的实验表明，Libra相比基线方法实现了最高3.0倍的吞吐量提升，并在奖励收敛速度上加速高达2.5倍。

Conclusion: 通过弹性资源池和因果驱动的调度策略，Libra有效解决了智能体RL中长尾分布和动态漂移带来的资源管理挑战，显著提升了系统效率和训练收敛速度。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a standard post-training paradigm for large language models (LLMs), extending beyond preference alignment to complex reasoning and multi-turn agentic behaviors. In agentic RL, the rollout stage generates trajectories while invoking tools, producing long-tailed and non-stationary workloads that challenge conventional resource-management assumptions. Three fundamental challenges arise. First, due to the long-tail distribution, a small fraction of trajectories dominates rollout makespan. Second, rollout and training exhibit strong asymmetry in compute patterns, memory demands, and sensitivity to sequence length. Third, as the RL policy evolves, the trajectory-length distribution drifts over time, rendering any static resource split progressively suboptimal.
  We present Libra, which introduces two core mechanisms. The first is a periodic global resource planner that jointly optimizes GPU allocation across rollout and training clusters. It leverages an elastic hybrid pool to enable lightweight, non-blocking worker reallocation between stages. The second is a causality-driven multi-level feedback queue (C-MLFQ) scheduler, which routes requests to heterogeneous rollout buckets based on causal signals derived from tool-return outcomes, rather than relying on fragile length predictions. Evaluated on 48 A800 GPUs, Libra achieves up to 3.0$\times$ higher throughput and converges up to 2.5$\times$ faster in reward compared to the baselines.

</details>


### [110] [Multi-component Causal Tracing in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03085)
*Zirui Yan,Dennis Wei,Dmitriy A. Katz,Prasanna Sattigeri,Ali Tajer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一框架，通过软干预和度量转换，高效地对大语言模型中的多个组件（如注意力头和MLP神经元）同时进行因果追踪，以识别对目标性能指标最关键的子集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往的因果追踪研究局限于单一组件或单层分析，缺乏同时对多个组件进行系统性因果归因的统一方法，难以应对多组件组合带来的组合爆炸问题。

Method: 提出一个统一的多组件因果追踪框架，利用软干预和精心设计的度量转换，将组合搜索问题转化为可在适当约束下高效求解的连续优化问题，从而生成组件选择的二元决策。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提方法能够高效识别对目标指标影响最大的组件子集，性能优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: 多组件因果追踪框架有效解决了组合复杂度问题，为理解大语言模型内部因果机制提供了更全面的分析工具。

Abstract: Causal tracing systematically intervenes on a large language model's (LLM's) internal representations to uncover and quantify the causal pathways linking specific inputs or computations to specific metrics of interest, quantifying the LLM's behavior. Building on previous single-component or single-layer studies, this paper presents a unified framework for causally tracing multiple components simultaneously. This framework systematically identifies the subsets of components (e.g., attention heads and multi-layer perceptron neurons) most critical to a desired target performance metric (e.g., accuracy and fairness). This is achieved by incorporating flexible interventions applied to a wide range of desired metrics. To address the combinatorial complexity of the multi-component problem, an efficient algorithm is designed that leverages soft interventions and a carefully designed metric transformation, converting the combinatorial search problem into a continuous one that can be solved efficiently under proper constraints, thereby generating proper binary decisions for selecting components. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed method efficiently identifies subsets of the model's components that have a high impact on the target metric, outperforming existing baseline approaches. Our code is available at https://github.com/ZiruiYan/multi-component-causal-tracing.

</details>


### [111] [Learning to Solve, Forgetting to Retain: Correct-Set Turnover in RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03087)
*Chuanyu Qin,Chenxu Yang,Qingyi Si,Naibin Gu,Peng Fu,Zheng Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现 RLVR 训练大语言模型时存在“正确集翻转”现象（已掌握问题随训练进程重新变得不可解），提出 retention-aware 的 Remind 机制，通过零额外开销的预回滚批次替换定期重温已掌握提示，在 20 个多模态与纯文本基准上显著优于 GRPO、DAPO 和 replay 基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RLVR 方法在提升大模型能力时，已解决问题会随着训练进度悄悄变得不可解，这种正确集翻转带来掌握集上“获取”与“遗忘”的耦合动态，而标准 RLVR 流水线未能利用问题修复的低成本窗口期。

Method: 提出 Remind 机制，一种保留感知的回顾机制：追踪模型已掌握的 prompt，利用预回滚批次替换（pre-rollout batch replacement）定期将其重新引入训练，在零额外 rollout 开销下提醒模型回忆之前的解决方案。

Result: 在 20 个基准测试（涵盖图像-文本、视频、纯文本任务；使用 Qwen3-VL 和 Qwen2.5-Math）上，Remind 一致优于 GRPO、DAPO 和 replay 基线，展现出跨模态和算法的鲁棒泛化能力。

Conclusion: 正确集翻转是 RLVR 训练中的关键隐性问题，修复延迟的成本呈急剧增长（repair-window principle），通过有策略地重温已掌握样本可以有效缓解遗忘，且 Remind 机制在不增加计算开销的前提下实现了显著的性能提升。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) improves the ability of large language model, yet headline accuracy gains often conceal a hidden cost: previously solved problems quietly become unsolvable as training proceeds. We frame this phenomenon as \emph{correct-set turnover}, representing the coupled dynamics of solution acquisition and regression over the mastered set. Under this view, retention becomes an explicit optimization target alongside acquisition. We analytically and empirically establish the \emph{repair-window principle}: the cost of restoring a regressed prompt grows sharply with review delay, defining a low-cost window that standard RLVR pipelines fail to exploit. To address this, we propose \textbf{\method{}}, a retention-aware review mechanism that tracks mastered prompts and periodically reintroduces them to \textbf{remind} the model of previous solutions. By utilizing pre-rollout batch replacement, \method{} incurs zero additional rollout overhead. Evaluated across 20 benchmarks spanning image-text, video, and text-only tasks with Qwen3-VL and Qwen2.5-Math, \method{} consistently improves performance over GRPO, DAPO, and replay baselines, demonstrating robust generalizability across modalities and algorithms.

</details>


### [112] [When RLHF Fails: A Mechanistic Taxonomy of Reward Hacking, Collapse, and Evaluator Gaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03238)
*Zelalem Abahana*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对RLHF的奖励破解（reward hacking）进行了细粒度分类研究，发现PPO的局部奖励破解率最高（14.45%），而UP-PPO可降低该风险；通过训练动态可在奖励崩溃发生前进行预测（ROC-AUC 0.821），并指出RLHF失败不仅是最终模型问题，更是可分类、可定位、可部分预测的训练动态过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLHF用可学习的奖励代理替代人类目标，但这一替代引入了一个结构化的失败面：优化可能提升学习到的奖励值而外部质量下降。现有研究通常将reward hacking视为单一终点事件，缺乏对其训练动态过程的细粒度分类与早期预警机制。

Method: 使用紧凑型RLHF管线（PPO、DPO、UP-PPO），引入奖励模型不确定性、近似策略漂移、多样性与重复性诊断及两个外部LLM评判器；基于学习奖励、评判器得分和平均评判器得分的变动方向对检查点间的过渡进行分类，共分析61个检查点行和1920个行级过渡。

Result: PPO的局部奖励破解率最高（14.45%，95% CI: 10.16-18.75），UP-PPO在相同激进训练下降至11.33-10.94%；基于过渡前的逻辑回归模型可预测未来行级奖励破解（ROC-AUC 0.821）；在12个设置中有3个发现行级分析能捕捉到检查点平均被忽略的局部奖励破解。

Conclusion: RLHF失败不仅是最终模型病理，更是可分类、可定位、可部分预测的训练动态过程。方法论启示：应关注训练过程中的动态信号而非仅评估最终模型。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) makes large-scale post-training possible by replacing an underspecified human objective with learned and scalable proxies. The same substitution creates a structured failure surface: optimization can raise the learned reward while external quality falls, degrade both proxy and judge scores, reveal proxy under-alignment, or produce evaluator-specific disagreement. We present an empirical failure-mode study of a compact RLHF pipeline with proximal policy optimization (PPO), direct preference optimization (DPO), uncertainty-penalized PPO (UP-PPO), reward-model uncertainty, approximate policy drift, diversity and repetition diagnostics, and two external LLM judges. Rather than treating reward hacking as a single terminal event, we classify matched transitions between checkpoints using the directions of the learned reward, judge scores, and average judge score. Across 61 checkpoint rows and 1920 row-level transitions, aggressive PPO has the highest localized reward-hacking rate (14.45%; bootstrap 95% CI: 10.16-18.75), while UP-PPO yields lower rates in the same aggressive regime (11.33-10.94%). A pre-transition logistic model predicts future row-level reward hacking with ROC-AUC 0.821, and row-level analysis finds localized reward hacking that checkpoint averages miss in 3 of 12 settings. The central conclusion is methodological: RLHF failures are not only final-model pathologies, but training dynamics that can be classified, localized, and partially anticipated.

</details>


### [113] [Message Tuning Outshines Graph Prompt Tuning: A Prismatic Space Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03290)
*Yancheng Chen,Dun Ma,Shuai Zhang,Yang Liu,Xixun Lin,Xiangyu Zhao,Wenguo Yang,Wei Chen,Chuan Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出棱镜空间理论（PS-Theory）来量化图提示调优的适应能力上界，并引入消息调优（MTG）方法，通过注入可学习消息原型超越该上界，实验验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法虽然解释了图提示调优为何有效，但缺乏严格量化其适应能力的手段，理解这一能力上限对于揭示图提示调优的局限性以及开发更强大的适应方法至关重要。

Method: 提出PS-Theory数学框架用于量化适应方法的容量，并推导图提示调优的适应能力上界；在此基础上提出MTG，在GNN骨干每层注入少量可学习消息原型，自适应引导消息融合，无需更新预训练权重。

Result: 通过PS-Theory证明MTG的适应能力可超越图提示调优的理论上界；在多个基准数据集上，MTG一致优于图提示基线方法。

Conclusion: PS-Theory为量化图提示调优的适应能力提供了理论工具，MTG作为一种轻量级方法成功突破了该上界，实验结果为理论发现提供了有力支持。

Abstract: Graph Foundation Models (GFMs), built upon the Pre-training and Adaptation paradigm, have emerged as a research hotspot in graph learning. For GNN-based GFMs, graph prompt tuning has become the prevailing adaptation method for downstream tasks. Although recent methods explain why graph prompt tuning works, how to rigorously measure its adaptation capacity remains an open problem. Addressing this problem is critical for understanding the capability limits of graph prompt tuning and for developing more powerful adaptation methods. In this paper, we propose Prismatic Space Theory (PS-Theory), a novel mathematical framework to quantify the capacity of adaptation methods, while focusing on establishing the upper bound for the adaptation capacity of graph prompt tuning. Building upon the proposed PS-Theory, we further introduce Message Tuning for GFMs (MTG), a lightweight approach that injects a small set of learnable message prototypes into each layer of the GNN backbone to adaptively guide message fusion without updating pre-trained weights. Through our PS-Theory, we prove that the adaptation capacity of MTG can exceed the theoretical upper bound of graph prompt tuning. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MTG consistently outperforms graph prompt baselines across diverse benchmark datasets, providing strong empirical support for our theoretical findings.

</details>


### [114] [Learning Multi-Scale Hypergraph for High-Order Brain Connectivity Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03310)
*Jaeyoon Sim,Soojin Hwang,Seunghun Baek,Guorong Wu,Won Hwa Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出自适应多尺度超边学习框架MuHL，通过构建层次化节点特征并在多分辨率图信号上动态学习高阶交互，有效提升神经退行性疾病（AD/PD）分类性能，并能识别与疾病进展相关的关键脑区及其群体交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图模型主要关注节点间的成对交互，难以捕捉跨多个脑区的高阶依赖关系；而超图方法多依赖预定义超边或仅学习超边权重，灵活性不足，无法捕获多分辨率结构模式。

Method: 提出自适应多尺度超边学习框架MuHL：构建层次化节点特征，在多分辨率图信号上动态连续地构建超边，从而学习高阶交互。

Result: 在多个脑网络基准数据集上，MuHL在不同疾病阶段均持续提升了疾病分类性能；从学到的超边中识别出与疾病进展相关的关键ROI及其群体交互。

Conclusion: MuHL作为脑网络分析工具在神经退行性疾病研究中具有潜力，能有效建模多分辨率高阶结构模式并辅助疾病机制理解。

Abstract: Understanding complex interactions between brain regions is critical for early neurodegenerative disease classification such as Alzheimer's Disease (AD) and Parkinson's Disease (PD). While graph-based models are widely used to analyze brain networks, most existing approaches primarily focus on pairwise interactions between directly connected nodes, limiting their ability to capture higher-order dependencies across multiple regions. Although hypergraph-based methods have been proposed to model higher-order relations, many rely on predefined hyperedges or restrict learning to hyperedge weights, reducing flexibility and limiting their capacity to capture multi-resolution structural patterns. In this regard, we introduce an adaptive multi-scale hyperedge learning framework, i.e., MuHL, which constructs hierarchical node features and dynamically learns high-order interactions through continuous hyperedge construction over multi-resolution graph signals. Extensive experiments on multiple brain network benchmarks demonstrate that MuHL consistently improves disease classification performance across different stages, and further identifies key regions of interest (ROIs) and their group-wise interactions from the learned hyperedges that are associated with disease progression, highlighting its potential as a powerful tool for brain network analysis in neurodegenerative disorders.

</details>


### [115] [HiSE: A Lightweight Hierarchical Semantic Explainer for Heterogeneous Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03495)
*Zongrui Li,Yuhang Zhao,Ying Zhao,Yuanzhao Guo,Qiang Huang,Yuan Tian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HiSE提出了一种面向异构图神经网络(HGNN)的轻量级特征级可解释方法，通过分层语义建模（语义层使用LASSO学习稀疏特征表示，跨语义层使用KL散度自适应融合）实现高保真、鲁棒且高效的语义感知特征解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HGNN解释方法存在两大局限：一是生成的解释无法反映HGNN固有的语义层次，导致对模型内部决策机制的保真度不足；二是特征解释依赖复杂的搜索或扰动机制，计算复杂度高、效率低下。

Method: HiSE采用分层语义建模框架：语义层利用基于LASSO的局部替代模型学习各语义视图下的稀疏特征表示；跨语义层通过KL散度自适应刻画不同语义视图的贡献，生成统一解释。

Result: 大量实验表明，HiSE在保真度、鲁棒性和跨语义解释能力上优于现有方法，且轻量级框架计算开销低，可高效应用于大规模复杂真实异构图。

Conclusion: HiSE通过分层语义建模有效解决了现有HGNN解释方法保真度低和计算效率差的问题，为高敏感场景下HGNN的可信应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Heterogeneous graph neural networks (HGNNs) have demonstrated remarkable performance in modeling complex relational data, however their interpretability in high-stakes applications remains a critical challenge. Existing explanation methods suffer from two major limitations: on the one hand, the generated explanations fail to reflect the inherent semantic hierarchy of HGNNs, resulting in a lack of fidelity to the model's internal decision-making mechanism; on the other hand, feature explanations often rely on complex search or perturbation mechanisms, leading to excessive computational complexity and poor efficiency. To address these issues, we propose HiSE, a lightweight feature-oriented interpretable model for HGNNs. HiSE achieves semantically aware feature explanations through hierarchical semantic modeling: at the semantic level, local surrogate models based on the Least Absolute Shrinkage and Selection Operator (LASSO) are employed to learn sparse feature representations under each semantic view; at the cross-semantic level, the contributions of different semantic views are adaptively characterized via KL divergence to produce a unified explanation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that HiSE outperforms existing methods in terms of fidelity, robustness, and cross-semantic explanation capability, while its lightweight framework incurs low computational overhead, enabling efficient application to large-scale, complex real-world heterogeneous graphs.

</details>


### [116] [CauTion: Knowing When to Trust LLMs for Ensemble Causal Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03602)
*Bo Peng,Kaiwen Wu,Sirui Chen,Zhiheng Wang,Yu Qiao,Chaochao Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CauTion是一个通过共识过滤和LLM可靠性估计，将LLM领域知识可靠集成到统计因果发现算法集成中的框架，在三阶段流程（共识投票、信任校准仲裁、环修复）后，在六个数据集上 consistently 优于纯数据驱动和LLM增强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯统计因果发现方法存在统计不可区分性（等价类内）和有限样本敏感性等固有限制；现有LLM增强方法易受LLM错误影响且token成本高；依赖单一数据驱动算法会使结果对算法特定偏差敏感。

Method: 三阶段框架：(1) 算法集成通过共识投票解决算法间一致边（高达96%），在过滤的共识边上达到近乎完美精度；(2) 信任校准仲裁机制通过无标注信任校准过程估计LLM与算法的相对可靠性，并用于控制信任加权投票，将LLM仲裁限制在算法证据不可靠的边上；(3) 环修复步骤确保最终因果图是有效的无环图。

Result: 在六个数据集上，CauTion一致优于纯数据驱动和LLM增强基线，在更大图上增益更大，并对LLM错误具有强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: CauTion通过共识过滤和信任校准机制有效结合了多算法集成与LLM领域知识的优势，在提升因果发现准确性的同时降低了对LLM错误的敏感性。

Abstract: Causal discovery from observational data remains challenging due to the fundamental limitations of purely statistical methods, such as statistical distinguishability within equivalence classes and sensitivity to finite sample sizes. While large language models (LLMs) offer a promising source of domain knowledge to complement statistical inference, existing LLM-augmented methods are vulnerable to LLM errors and incur high token costs. Moreover, reliance on a single data-centric algorithm can make results sensitive to algorithm-specific biases. To address these limitations, we propose CauTion, a framework that reliably integrates LLM domain knowledge into an ensemble of statistical causal discovery algorithms through consensus filtering and LLM reliability estimation. CauTion proceeds in three stages. First, an algorithm ensemble utilizes a consensus voting to resolve up to 96% of edges on which algorithms agree, achieving near-perfect accuracy on the filtered consensus edges. Second, a trust-calibrated arbitration mechanism estimates the relative reliability of the LLM and the algorithms via an annotation-free trust calibration procedure, which is then utilized to govern a trust-weighted voting process that restricts LLM arbitration exclusively to edges with unreliable algorithmic evidence. Third, a cycle repair step is applied to guarantee the final causal graph is validly acyclic. Experiments on six datasets demonstrate that CauTion consistently outperforms both data-centric and LLM-augmented baselines, with larger gains on larger graphs and strong robustness to LLM errors. Code is available at https://github.com/OpenCausaLab/CauTion.

</details>


### [117] [Exploiting Verification-Generation Gap: Test-Time Reinforcement Learning with Confidence-Conditioned Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03608)
*Jiahui Li,Jianfeng Shan,Wenpei Chen,Shunyu Wu,Jian Lou,Wenjie Feng,Dan Li,See-Kiong Ng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TTRL-CoCoV框架，通过置信度条件验证机制解决无标签设置下Pass@k优化难题。针对低置信度样本伪标签不准确和高置信度样本多样性坍塌问题，采用自适应策略：高置信度样本引导验证器并施加探索增强奖励，低置信度样本由验证器筛选伪标签，中等置信度样本跳过验证。在6个基准上，Pass@1平均提升+9.8%，Pass@16提升+18.7%，甚至超越全监督RL方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时强化学习研究聚焦于Pass@1性能，但在无标签设置下优化Pass@k（衡量生成覆盖率和持续探索能力）仍未被充分探索。直接应用针对RLVR设计的Pass@k优势函数效果不佳，需要专门的方法来提升Pass@k覆盖率和Pass@1性能。

Method: 提出TTRL-CoCoV框架，基于置信度条件机制：(1) 高置信度样本：自举验证器并采用探索增强奖励防止多样性坍塌；(2) 低置信度样本：将伪标签选择权委托给验证器以过滤错误标签；(3) 中等置信度样本：跳过验证环节。核心洞察是验证能力通常引领生成能力。

Result: 在6个广泛认可的基准上，TTRL-CoCoV优于所有对比方法：相比TTRL，Pass@1平均绝对提升+9.8%，Pass@16平均绝对提升+18.7%；甚至在多个推理基准上，相比全监督RL方法也实现了高达+5.0%的Pass@1绝对提升。

Conclusion: 置信度条件验证机制有效解决了无标签测试时强化学习中低置信度样本伪标签错误和高置信度样本多样性坍塌两大核心问题，不仅显著提升了Pass@k覆盖范围，也改进了Pass@1性能，甚至可媲美全监督RL方法。

Abstract: Test-time reinforcement learning has emerged as a promising paradigm for enhancing the complex reasoning abilities of large language models in a completely label-free manner. Despite existing studies focusing on Pass@1 performance, optimizing Pass@k remains under-explored yet critical in label-free settings, which measures generation coverage for sustained exploration. Optimizing Pass@k in label-free setting is highly non-trivial, as directly applying the Pass@k advantage designs effective for RLVR yields unsatisfactory performance. Through in-depth empirical analysis, we discover the root causes hindering performance: pseudo-label estimations for low-confidence samples have a high probability of being incorrect, while candidate answers for high-confidence samples suffer from severe diversity collapse. To overcome these hurdles, we propose TTRL-CoCoV (Test-Time Reinforcement Learning with Confidence-Conditioned Verification), a novel confidence-adaptive framework that expands Pass@k coverage and improves Pass@1 performance. Based on our key insight that verification capability generally leads generation capability, TTRL-CoCoV employs a confidence-conditioned mechanism: for high-confidence samples, it bootstraps verifier and applies an exploration-enhancing reward to prevent diversity collapse; for low-confidence samples, it delegates pseudo-label selection to the verifier to filter incorrect pseudo-labels; and for medium-confidence samples, it bypasses verification entirely. Extensive experiments demonstrate that TTRL-CoCoV outperforms the best competing methods across 6 widely-recognized benchmarks, achieves average absolute gains of +9.8% in Pass@1 and +18.7% in Pass@16 over TTRL, and even achieves absolute Pass@1 improvements of up to +5.0% across multiple reasoning benchmarks when compared against fully supervised RL methods. Our code repository: https://github.com/shanjf666/CoCoV.

</details>


### [118] [Spatial Transcriptomics-Guided Alignment Enhances Molecular Profiling in Pathology Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03644)
*Fengtao Zhou,Yingxue Xu,Zhengyu Zhang,Yihui Wang,Zhengrui Guo,Ling Liang,Jiabo Ma,Cheng Jin,Ziyi Liu,Huajun Zhou,Hongyi Wang,Du Cai,Chenglong Zhao,Xi Wang,Can Yang,Yu Wang,Wenbin Li,Feng Gao,Zhe Wang,Zhenhui Li,Xiuming Zhang,Li Liang,Hao Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出STAMP框架，利用空间转录组学数据引导病理基础模型学习分子感知表征，通过构建HumanST-1k数据集和通路引导的对齐策略，在H&E全切片图像上实现分子表型推断，显著提升临床分子 profiling 能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精准肿瘤学亟需全面分子分析，但现有方法成本高、耗时长且消耗样本；现有病理基础模型主要依赖视觉自监督学习或视觉-语言对齐，缺乏空间分辨的分子监督信号，难以将细微形态特征与潜在基因组改变关联起来。

Method: STAMP框架包含三个关键要素：(1) 构建HumanST-1k人类空间转录组数据集，包含180万对H&E斑块与对应转录组图谱；(2) 通道路径引导的对齐策略，将原始转录组数据聚合为生物功能通路，降低技术噪声；(3) 参数高效微调将通路信息整合入病理基础模型，增强其分子感知表征能力。

Result: 通过多层级评估框架验证了增强表征的临床实用性，表明STAMP能够解锁亚视觉分子特征的分辨能力，实现从常规H&E图像推断分子表型。

Conclusion: STAMP通过空间转录组学引导的对齐框架，成功赋予病理基础模型内在分子感知能力，为克服精准肿瘤学中成本、样本和时间瓶颈提供了新范式。

Abstract: Comprehensive molecular profiling is essential for modern precision oncology but remains hindered by prohibitive costs, specimen exhaustion, and protracted turnaround times. While pathology foundation models (PFMs) have demonstrated potential for inferring molecular phenotypes from routine hematoxylin and eosin (H&E) whole-slide images (WSIs), current architectures primarily rely on vision-centric self-supervised learning or vision-language alignment, lacking the spatially resolved molecular supervision required to connect subtle morphological features with underlying genomic alterations. Spatial transcriptomics (ST) emerges as a transformative technology that enables transcriptomic quantification within intact tissue sections, thereby preserving the precise spatial link between histology and molecular profiles. In this study, we present a Spatial Transcriptomics-guided Alignment framework for Molecular Profiling (STAMP), which endows PFMs with intrinsic molecular awareness. To support this paradigm, we curated HumanST-1k, a human ST dataset spanning diverse anatomical organs and sequencing platforms. This atlas yields 1.8 million pairs of H&E patches and corresponding transcriptomic profiles, providing a corpus that links histological structures with their molecular states. To mitigate the technical noise inherent to raw transcriptomics, STAMP applies a pathway-informed alignment strategy that aggregates transcriptomic data into biologically functional pathways, which are subsequently integrated into PFMs via parameter-efficient fine-tuning. This alignment enriches the representation space of PFMs and unlocks their capacity to resolve sub-visual molecular signatures. The clinical utility of these augmented representations was validated through a multi-tier evaluation framework.

</details>


### [119] [The Shape of Addition: Geometric Structures of Arithmetic in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03645)
*Liuyuan Wen,Xun Zhu,Lihao Huang,Wenbin Li,Yang Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过分析残差流几何结构，发现了多操作数加法中的等原和轨迹（IRST），并提出噪声量化模型（Noisy Quantization Model）将算术错误解释为由内部神经噪声引起的几何滑移（Geometric Slippages），进而开发出几何一致性检验方法用于检测和纠正推理中的量化失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在基础算术任务中表现出矛盾性的脆弱性，暗示其内部计算与离散输出之间存在脱节，作者希望从几何角度揭示这一现象背后的机制。

Method: 1) 分析残差流几何结构，识别等原和轨迹（IRST）；2) 提出噪声量化模型，将算术错误建模为连续潜进位势跨过量化阈值时的几何滑移；3) 提出几何一致性检验方法用于检测和纠正量化失败。

Result: 发现IRST结构——表示由语义数字锚定并由连续进位纤维调制；通过噪声量化模型解释了算术错误的几何本质；展示了探针通用性（Probe Versatility）原理；验证了几何一致性检验方法能有效检测和纠正推理错误。

Conclusion: 算术错误本质上是内部神经噪声导致的几何滑移，可以通过几何一致性检验进行检测和纠正，为理解LLM内部计算与输出之间的脱节提供了统一的几何框架。

Abstract: Large Language Models exhibit paradoxical fragility in fundamental arithmetic, implying a disconnect between internal computation and discrete output. By analyzing the residual stream geometry during multi-operand addition, we identify the Iso-Raw-Sum Trajectory (IRST), a geometric structure where representations are anchored by semantic digits and modulated by continuous carry fibers. We propose the Noisy Quantization Model to explain this geometry, framing arithmetic errors as Geometric Slippages caused by internal neural noise pushing a continuous, latent Carry Potential across quantization thresholds. This geometric framework further elucidates Probe Versatility, explaining how lightweight probes can disentangle coexisting latent signals (such as ground truth versus hallucination) from a single activation vector. Finally, we validate these insights through a geometric consistency check method that effectively detects and corrects these quantization failures during inference. Our code is available at https://github.com/RL-MIND/Shape-of-Addition.

</details>


### [120] [Speedrunning Tabular Foundation Model Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03681)
*Salih Bora Ozturk,Alexander Pfefferle,Frank Hutter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为nanoTabPFN的表格基础模型预训练加速竞赛（speedrun），参赛者通过修改单文件训练脚本，在单张NVIDIA L40S GPU上以固定下游ROC AUC目标进行竞争。当前最佳记录达到目标仅需0.92分钟，相比基线74.32分钟实现了81倍加速，同时使用的合成数据集数量减少了22倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练成本是表格基础模型研究的主要瓶颈，拖慢了新架构、先验知识和优化思路的迭代周期，而社区缺乏简单的方法来比较和累积预训练加速效果。

Method: 设计了一个社区speedrun竞赛，参与者修改单文件训练脚本，在统一硬件（单张NVIDIA L40S GPU）上，以子采样TabArena数据集的固定下游ROC AUC目标为基准，进行预训练加速比拼。

Result: 当前最佳记录在0.92分钟内达到目标，相比基线74.32分钟实现81倍加速，且合成数据集使用量减少22倍。

Conclusion: 该speedrun形式为社区提供了一种简单、可验证、可叠加的预训练改进协议，加速了表格基础模型的研究迭代，排行榜向社区开放。

Abstract: Pretraining cost is a major bottleneck for research on tabular foundation models, slowing the iteration cycle for new architectures, priors, and optimization ideas. Yet the community lacks a simple way to compare and accumulate pretraining speedups. We introduce a community speedrun for nanoTabPFN: contributors modify a single-file training script and compete to reach a fixed downstream ROC AUC target on subsampled TabArena using one NVIDIA L40S GPU. The current best record reaches the target in 0.92 minutes, an 81x speedup over the 74.32 minute baseline while using 22x fewer synthetic datasets. The speedrun format provides a simple protocol for the community to add, verify, and stack pretraining improvements, with the leaderboard open to contributions. Code and records are available at https://github.com/borawhocodess/modded-nanotabpfn.

</details>


### [121] [A Close Look At World Model Recovery In Supervised Fine-Tuned LLM Planners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03685)
*Patrick Emami,Nan Qiang,Peter Graf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过可解释性实验探究了经过监督微调（SFT）的LLM在经典规划任务中是否真正恢复了底层世界模型（即学会表示和推理规划问题）。实验发现：SFT使LLM能够线性编码动作有效性和部分状态谓词；即使模型无法通过输出概率正确分类动作有效性，其内部表示仍能区分有效与无效动作；更广的状态空间覆盖（如随机游走数据）有助于更准确地恢复世界模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索监督微调后的LLM在经典规划任务中是否真正学会了表示和推理规划问题（即恢复世界模型），而不仅仅是生成有效动作序列。

Method: 设计一系列可解释性实验，从内部表示和生成能力两个维度综合检验LLM的世界模型恢复情况。具体包括：分析线性编码能力（动作有效性、状态谓词）、比较输出概率与内部表示的分类能力、评估不同训练数据（如随机游走数据）对状态空间覆盖的影响。

Result: 1) SFT使LLM能线性编码动作有效性和部分状态谓词；2) 即使输出概率分类失败，内部表示仍能区分有效/无效动作；3) 更广的状态空间覆盖（随机游走数据）可提升世界模型恢复准确度。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一套将可解释性技术应用于规划LLM的通用方法，并揭示了LLM中知识表示的关键洞见，有助于理解LLM如何内部表征规划问题。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) improves end-to-end classical planning in large language models (LLMs), but do these models also learn to represent and reason about the planning problems they are solving? Due to the relative complexity of classical planning problems and the challenge that end-to-end plan generation poses for LLMs, it has been difficult to explore this question. In our work, we devise and perform a series of interpretability experiments that holistically interrogate world model recovery by examining both internal representations and generative capabilities of fine-tuned LLMs. We find that: a) Supervised fine-tuning on valid action sequences enables LLMs to linearly encode action validity and some state predicates. b) Models that struggle to use output probabilities for classifying action validity may still learn internal representations that separate valid from invalid actions. c) Broader state space coverage during fine-tuning, such as from random walk data, yields more accurate recovery of the underlying world model. In summary, this work contributes a recipe for applying interpretability techniques to planning LLMs and generates insights that shed light on open questions about how knowledge is represented in LLMs.

</details>


### [122] [Staying Alive: Uncensored Survival Analysis with Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03689)
*Mariana Vargas Vieyra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于表格基础模型（TFM）的无训练生存回归方法，通过迭代插补右删失数据构建加速失效时间（AFT）模型，在标准生存分析基准上取得了与需训练的Cox回归和参数化AFT模型相竞争的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生存分析中的右删失问题使得传统方法需要针对特定数据集进行参数拟合，而表格基础模型虽在预测任务中表现优异却难以直接应用于时间-事件数据。作者旨在利用TFM的零样本能力解决右删失带来的挑战，实现无需训练的生存回归。

Method: 提出一种无训练方法：(1) 利用表格基础模型构建加速失效时间（AFT）模型，仅需拟合一个标量参数；(2) 基于Buckley-James估计器，引入非参数化的上下文内估计器对右删失数据进行迭代插补，从而完成生存回归。

Result: 在标准生存分析基准上，该方法与需要训练的多种参数化和半参数化生存回归模型（包括Cox回归和参数化AFT模型）具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 证明了表格基础模型可以在无需训练的情况下有效应用于右删失时间-事件数据的生存回归，为生存分析提供了新的无训练范式。

Abstract: Survival Analysis (SA) is a statistical framework that models the time span until some event of interest occurs. Widely used in several domains, including healthcare and churn prediction, a central challenge in its applicability stems from the time of the event being partially observed or \emph{right-censoring}.
  Tabular Foundation Models (TFM) have attracted significant interest in recent years due to their ability to perform prediction tasks in a single forward pass, requiring no dataset-specific parameter fitting. Despite their success, their application to prediction tasks on time-to-event data remains difficult due to right censoring. In this work, we present a training-free method to survival regression by leveraging TFMs to both predict the time of the event and iteratively impute right-censored data.
  Our method uses a TFM to construct an Accelerated Failure Time (AFT) model requiring no training beyond fitting a single scalar parameter. Subsequently, by building on the Buckley-James estimator, we introduce a non-parametric in-context estimator for right-censored data. Our experiments on standard survival analysis benchmarks show that our method is competitive with several parametric and semi-parametric survival regression models that require training, including Cox regression and parametric AFT models.

</details>


### [123] [Multi$^2$: Hierarchical Multi-Agent Decision-Making with LLM-Based Agents in Interactive Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03698)
*Sangeun Park,Minhae Kwon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Multi²，一个分层多智能体决策框架，将高层智能体（System 1）通过监督微调进行上下文感知的子目标生成，与低层智能体（System 2）通过离线到在线强化学习执行原子动作相结合，有效缓解了LLM智能体在长时域决策中的目标漂移问题，并发布了三个分层基准数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体在长时域交互中容易出现目标漂移（objective drift），即目标和计划随着交互时间延长而逐渐偏离原始方向，导致长期决策不稳定。

Method: 提出Multi²分层框架：高层智能体（System 1）使用监督微调生成上下文感知的子目标，低层智能体（System 2）采用离线到在线强化学习在交互环境中执行原子动作，通过角色分离实现稳定的长时域控制。

Result: 在多种交互环境中，Multi²持续优于强基线智能体，展现出更好的鲁棒性和多轮交互协调能力。同时发布了三个分层基准数据集以弥补该领域训练和评估数据的缺失。

Conclusion: 分层多智能体框架通过明确的角色分解（目标规划与动作执行）能有效缓解目标漂移，提升LLM智能体在长时域决策任务中的稳定性和适应性。

Abstract: A central goal of large language model (LLM) research is to build agentic systems that can plan, act, and adapt through sustained interaction with dynamic environments. While recent LLM-based agents exhibit impressive contextual reasoning, their long-horizon decision-making remains fragile, often suffering from objective drift, where goals and plans drift over extended interactions. We introduce Multi$^2$, a hierarchical multi-agent decision-making framework that explicitly decomposes agent behavior into complementary roles. A high-level agent (System 1) focuses on context-aware sub-goal generation using supervised fine-tuning (SFT), while a low-level agent (System 2) executes atomic actions through offline-to-online reinforcement learning (RL) in interactive environments. This separation enables stable long-horizon control, mitigates objective drift, and allows efficient adaptation. Across diverse interactive environments, Multi$^2$ consistently outperforms strong agentic baselines, demonstrating improved robustness and coordination in multi-turn interaction. Beyond performance, we introduce and release three hierarchical benchmark datasets, filling a long-standing gap in training and evaluating hierarchical decision-making for LLM-based agents.

</details>


### [124] [When Graph Tokens Sink: A Mechanistic Analysis of Graph Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03712)
*Ding Zhang,Runtao Zhou,Wenqing Zheng,Rizal Fathony,Bayan Bruss,Chirag Agarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究分析了图语言模型（GLMs）中图token的内部机制，发现图sink token虽然表现出激活层面的异常值，但并不等同于图信息的有效利用，图token的激活显著性与图语义效用之间存在解耦现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索LLMs如何在内部解释图token，以及图token是否真正作为图结构的有意义载体，揭示现有GLMs中图token构造、放置和对齐机制的局限性。

Method: 通过分析代表性GLM架构中图token的内部显著性、注意力权重分布，并采用剪枝、重新定位和交换等干预实验来评估图token的实际语义和结构重要性。

Result: 图sink token在激活层面表现为离群值（沿少数隐藏状态维度有巨大激活值，偏向早期位置），但并未吸引最大的注意力权重，且通过干预实验证明它们并非下游预测中最重要的语义或结构token。

Conclusion: 当前GLMs将图结构映射到LLM token空间后，生成的图token表示并未自然形成可用的拓扑感知内部表示，而是表现出激活显著性与图语义效用之间的解耦，提示现有图token构造机制存在局限性。

Abstract: Graph Language Models (GLMs) have become a promising direction for adapting Large Language Models (LLMs) to graph learning tasks. By transforming graph topology and node information into graph tokens, GLMs allow LLMs to jointly process structured graph inputs and textual instructions. Yet, it remains unclear how LLMs internally interpret these graph tokens and whether graph tokens act as meaningful carriers of graph structure. In this work, we analyze how LLMs process graph information through graph-token behavior in representative GLM architectures.
  Findings. We find that the internal saliency of graph tokens in GLMs is not equivalent to graph information utilization. Graph sink tokens consistently emerge as activation-level outliers: they can be identified by massive activation values along a small set of hidden-state dimensions and are biased toward early graph-token positions. However, this activation-level saliency does not imply that these tokens are the main carriers of graph information. Unlike classical attention sinks in language and vision-language models, graph sink tokens do not necessarily attract the largest attention weights from query tokens. Through pruning, repositioning, and swapping interventions, we show that graph sink tokens are not the most important semantic or structural tokens for downstream prediction.
  Implications. Together, these results suggest that after current GLMs map graph structure into the LLM token space, the resulting graph-token representations do not naturally form a fully usable topology-aware internal representation; instead, they exhibit a decoupling between activation-level saliency and graph-semantic utility. This decoupling points to limitations in existing graph-token construction, placement, and alignment mechanisms.

</details>


### [125] [Compress then Merge: From Multiple LoRAs into One Low-Rank Adapter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03723)
*Zhengbao He,Ruiqi Ding,Zhehao Huang,Ruikai Yang,Tao Li,Xiaolin Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Compress-then-Merge (CtM)方法，通过先压缩后合并的流程，在低秩空间中对多个LoRA适配器进行合并，避免了传统Merge-then-Compress方法中全参数合并破坏低秩结构的问题，保证输出为严格的rank-r LoRA，并在多项任务中优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Merge-then-Compress方法先在全参数空间合并适配器再压缩，这一过程可能破坏低秩结构，导致后续SVD截断难以恢复有效的rank-r LoRA。因此需要一种能保证合并后仍保持低秩结构的方法。

Method: CtM采用"先压缩后合并"的逆序流程：首先利用所有LoRA权重计算共享的r维子空间以捕获跨适配器的共同结构，然后将每个适配器投影到该共享子空间得到r×r坐标，最后在该低维空间应用标准合并规则。该方法通过构造保证输出为rank-r LoRA。

Result: 在多个模型和任务上的实验表明，CtM在单一LoRA输出场景下一致优于现有基线方法，同时缩小了与全参数合并方法的性能差距。

Conclusion: CtM通过逆转合并与压缩的顺序，有效解决了多LoRA合并中的低秩结构破坏问题，在保证秩约束的前提下实现了更好的性能，为LoRA适配器的复用与部署提供了更优方案。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) enables parameter-efficient specialization of foundation models, but the proliferation of task-specific adapters fragments capabilities across many adapters, complicating reuse and deployment. We study the problem of merging $T$ LoRAs into a single rank-$r$ LoRA, thereby preserving the benefits of low-rank structure. Existing Merge-then-Compress pipelines treat the rank constraint as an afterthought: they merge adapters in the full parameter space, then compress the merged result to rank $r$ via truncated SVD. However, full-parameter merging may destroy the low-rank structure, making it difficult for subsequent compression to recover an effective rank-$r$ LoRA. We propose Compress-then-Merge (CtM), a reversed pipeline that enforces the rank-$r$ bottleneck before merging: CtM computes shared $r$-dimensional subspaces using only the LoRA weights to capture cross-adapter common structure, projects each adapter into the shared subspaces to obtain $r\times r$ coordinates, and then applies standard merging rules in this reduced space. CtM guarantees a rank-$r$ LoRA by construction, avoiding post-hoc truncation, and enables efficient computation in the core space spanned by concatenated LoRA factors. Experiments across multiple models and tasks show that CtM consistently outperforms existing single-LoRA-output baselines while narrowing the performance gap to full-parameter merging methods.

</details>


### [126] [Conformal Language Modeling via Posterior Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03731)
*Nicolas Emmenegger,Theo X. Olausson,Armando Solar-Lezama,Chara Podimata*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对大语言模型幻觉问题，提出从近似LLM后验分布中采样（以校准的高分区域为条件）的方法，替代传统的后处理过滤方式，在保持统计保证的同时提升生成结果的实用性和一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于共形预测的幻觉抑制方法采用后处理方式，将采样过程视为原子操作然后对样本进行手术式修改，导致：(1) 生成样本不连贯、不一致或模型本身不太可能生成；(2) 无法将概率质量转移到更有用、更符合需求的响应上。

Method: 提出从LLM后验分布的近似中进行采样，以校准的高分区域作为条件事件；针对条件顺序生成场景开发了专门的校准流程，有效识别该区域并实现目标风险控制。

Result: 在开放式传记生成和数学问题求解两个案例研究中，该方法在获得相同统计保证的前提下，实现了更高的下游效用。

Conclusion: 通过将校准与生成过程结合而非后处理，可以在保证统计风险控制的同时提升生成质量与实用性。

Abstract: Large Language Models remain plagued by hallucinations. Recent work has sought to tame their prevalence using statistical techniques based on conformal prediction, with both theoretical and empirical success. However, these methods operate in a post-hoc fashion, treating the sampling procedure itself as atomic and then surgically altering samples to remove hallucinated claims. This disconnect between filtering and generation can result in samples that are incoherent, inconsistent, or simply unlikely under the model itself. Moreover, post-hoc surgery is unable to shift probability mass towards more useful and helpful responses. To address these issues, we propose to instead sample from approximations to an LLM posterior, where the conditioning event corresponds to a calibrated, high-scoring region. We develop a calibration procedure tailored to the setting of conditional sequential generation that effectively identifies this region and achieves target risk control. Empirically, we apply our method to case studies focused on open-ended biography generation and mathematical problem solving; compared to prior work, we obtain the same statistical guarantees, with higher downstream utility.

</details>


### [127] [Tool-Aware Optimization with Entropy Guidance for Efficient Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03762)
*Hongye Cao,Nuo Yan,Haoyuan Deng,Ziwei Wang,Tianpei Yang,Jing Huo,Yuyao Zhang,Yang Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TAO-RL是一个统一框架，通过工具感知轨迹过滤和熵引导探索，解决了LLM在工具增强强化学习中的训练不稳定问题，在7个推理基准上取得了优异表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在LLM中集成外部工具时存在训练不稳定问题：过度依赖工具导致输入分布偏移，而过于保守的工具使用限制了有效探索。

Method: 提出TAO-RL框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 数据层面：工具感知轨迹过滤，剔除所有工具调用失败、所有rollout全正确或全错误的轨迹，保留既具备工具能力又信息丰富的训练数据；(2) 算法层面：引入工具感知熵引导奖励，在工具调用后的token重塑优势函数，鼓励策略在关键决策点探索更多样化的推理路径。

Result: 在7个具有挑战性的推理基准、3种模型规模上的大量实验表明，TAO-RL优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 轨迹过滤和熵引导探索两个组件相互强化：前者建立干净信息丰富的训练基础，后者在关键工具交互节点驱动更强的推理行为，共同提升了工具增强RL的训练效果和推理性能。

Abstract: Agentic reinforcement learning (RL) equips large language models (LLMs) with tool-use capabilities that substantially improve reasoning on complex tasks. However, integrating external tools often destabilizes training: over-reliance on tools can induce input distribution shift, while overly conservative tool use limits effective exploration. To address this issue, we propose a unified framework TAO-RL that couples tool-aware trajectory filtering with entropy-guided exploration for efficient policy optimization. Specifically, at the data level, TAO-RL filters rollout trajectories along two criteria: discarding those where all tool invocations fail to execute, and removing those where all rollouts are either correct or incorrect, as both cases yield degenerate advantage estimates that contribute no discriminative learning signal. This joint filtering retains data that are both tool-capable and informative, establishing a high-quality training distribution. At the algorithmic level, we introduce a tool-aware entropy-guided bonus that reshapes the advantage function at post-tool-call tokens, encouraging the policy to explore more diverse reasoning paths at critical decision points. These two components are mutually reinforcing: trajectory filtering establishes a clean and informative training foundation, while entropy-guided exploration drives stronger reasoning behaviors at critical tool-interaction junctures. Extensive experiments on 7 challenging reasoning benchmarks across 3 model scales demonstrate the superiority of TAO-RL over existing methods.

</details>


### [128] [Limit Analysis of Graph Neural Networks with Wireless Conflict Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03794)
*Romina Garcia Camargo,Zhiyang Wang,Alejandro Ribeiro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于稀疏随机几何图（RGG）的图神经网络（GNN）在无线资源分配中的可迁移性，通过建立RGG与确定性网格图（DGG）之间的理论界限，证明了GNN跨尺度迁移时的性能损失上界，并在链路调度问题中验证了所提方法优于现有基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线网络规模不断增长，需要模型能够从小规模图训练后直接迁移到大规模部署中。现有GNN的可迁移性研究多基于一般图论，缺乏针对无线网络稀疏特性的理论分析。本文旨在建立稀疏随机几何图场景下GNN跨尺度迁移的理论保证。

Method: 聚焦于稀疏RGG的冲突图（用于建模链路间干扰），利用RGG与确定性网格图（DGG）之间的近似关系，推导跨尺度迁移时性能损失的理论界限。通过链路调度问题验证理论结果，并分析理论假设对实证性能的影响。

Result: 建立了稀疏RGG下GNN跨尺度迁移的性能损失上界；链路调度实验表明学习到的策略在规模扩展时持续优于现有基准。

Conclusion: 稀疏RGG场景下GNN具有良好的跨尺度可迁移性，理论界限与实际性能一致，为无线网络中的GNN应用提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have emerged as a powerful tool for wireless resource allocation that leverages the underlying graph structure of communication networks. Their transferability property enables models trained on small-scale graphs to generalize to large-scale deployments with little performance deterioration, a desirable property for currently growing networks. Wireless networks are sparse regimes, where a single node is connected to a small number of other users. This work establishes theoretical results for transferability of GNNs over graphs derived from sparse Random Geometric Graphs (RGGs). In particular, we focus on conflict graphs of RGGs used to model interference among links. Our approach considers the closeness between RGGs and Deterministic Grid Graphs (DGG) to establish bounds in the performance loss when a model is transferred across scales. We validate our theoretical findings through the problem of link scheduling, demonstrating that our learned policies consistently outperform existing benchmarks at scale. Finally, we examine the impact of our theoretical assumptions on empirical performance.

</details>


### [129] [Trading Human Curation for Synthetic Augmentation in RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03800)
*Akshansh <last>,Leonardo Rosa Rodrigues,Michael Korostelev,Youssef Hassan,Mark E. Whiting*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探讨在基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）中，使用预定义、门控过滤的增强任务替代人工策划任务的经济可行性。通过定义成本调整的交换率 ρ_cost 并进行受控消融实验，发现增强内容可在十项基准测试中保持与人工任务相当的泛化性能，且交换率在1.4倍到11.6倍之间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量训练任务的供给是基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）在智能体语言模型上的核心瓶颈。每个任务都需要沙盒环境、提示和手工编写的奖励函数，且只有通过质量门槛的任务才能产生有效训练信号。手工筛选在规模上不经济，且自动生成变体与人工任务之间的替代率尚未确定。

Method: 使用预定义、门控过滤的增强方法对少量手工基座任务进行扩充，作为RLVR中额外人工筛选的替代方案。形式化定义了成本调整的交换率 ρ_cost（增强任务与人工任务之间的经济替换比率），通过跨训练语料库（不同增强比例）的受控消融实验进行测量，并评估端到端经济性。

Result: 在涵盖代码、指令遵循、推理和多轮智能体函数调用的十项基准测试中，用增强内容替代额外人工任务保持了聚合的留出泛化性能。成本调整的交换率 ρ_cost 在合理的人工/增强成本比范围内保持在1.4倍到11.6倍之间。

Conclusion: 通过门控过滤的增强任务可以有效替代部分人工策划任务，在保持模型泛化性能的同时具有经济优势，为RLVR训练任务的规模化提供了可行路径。

Abstract: The supply of high-quality training tasks is a central bottleneck for reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR) on agentic language models. Each task requires a sandboxed setup, a prompt, and a hand-authored reward function, and only tasks that pass a quality bar produce useful training signal. Hand-curation at this quality bar does not scale economically to the task counts effective RL training requires, and the substitution rate between automatically generated task variants and human-authored ones is not yet established. We investigate using pre-specified, gate-filtered augmentations of a small hand-authored base as a substitute for additional human curation during RLVR. We formalize the cost-adjusted trade rate $ρ_{\text{cost}}$ between augmented and human-authored tasks, measure it through a controlled ablation across training corpora with varying augmentation share, and characterize the end-to-end economics of the augmentation pipeline. Substituting augmented content for additional human-authored tasks retains aggregate held-out generalization on a ten-benchmark suite spanning code, instruction following, reasoning, and multi-turn agentic function-calling. The cost-adjusted trade rate $ρ_{\text{cost}}$ between gated synthetic and human-authored RLVR tasks stays in $[1.4\times, 11.6\times]$ across the plausible $c_{\text{human}}/c_{\text{aug}}$ range.

</details>


### [130] [Easy-to-Use Shielding for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03804)
*Stefan Pranger,Bettina Könighofer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过开发tempestpy库，将形式化盾牌综合工具Tempest集成到Gymnasium API中，使RL practitioners能够在现有RL流程中直接使用盾牌技术进行安全探索，并扩展了随机多人博弈的盾牌计算支持，同时提出了MiniGridSafe环境集以促进可重复实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 盾牌技术虽能保证RL探索安全，但需要形式化方法专业知识且工程成本高，难以被RL社区采纳。缺乏将形式化盾牌综合与标准RL框架连接的可访问端到端基础设施。

Method: 1) 开发tempestpy库，将盾牌综合工具Tempest集成到Gymnasium API；2) 扩展Tempest算法以支持随机多人博弈的可靠盾牌计算；3) 提出MiniGridSafe环境集，包含概率转移和多智能体场景的安全探索测试床。

Result: 实现了从盾牌综合到RL部署的端到端工作流，在多个环境中对比了有/无盾牌的RL性能，提供了符号化MiniGrid模型和MiniGridSafe环境用于实验验证。

Conclusion: tempestpy降低了盾牌技术的使用门槛，使形式化安全探索方法成为RL从业者可用的实用组件，同时扩展了盾牌在随机多人博弈场景中的适用范围。

Abstract: Safe exploration is a key challenge in Reinforcement Learning (RL) that aims to prevent agents from making harmful decisions while exploring their environment. Safe exploration is a key challenge in Reinforcement Learning (RL) that aims to prevent agents from making harmful decisions while exploring their environment. Shielding is one such technique that assumes domain knowledge in the form of an environment model to decide upon action safety. Although well-established, shielding has seen limited adoption in RL due to the lack of accessible end-to-end infrastructure connecting formal shield synthesis with standard RL frameworks. Applying shielding typically requires expertise in formal methods and substantial engineering effort, keeping it outside the typical RL workflow. We address this by extending our shield synthesis tool Tempest into a practical backend for safe RL. Our core contribution is tempestpy, a Python library that integrates Tempest-based shield synthesis directly into the Gymnasium API, allowing shields to be synthesized and deployed within existing RL pipelines. This lowers the barrier to entry for shielding and turns formal safe-exploration methods into a usable component for RL practitioners. We also extend Tempest's algorithmic support to compute sound shields for stochastic multiplayer games, preserving formal safety guarantees. We demonstrate the resulting workflow end to end and evaluate shielded and unshielded RL across multiple environments. To facilitate modeling, we provide symbolic models for MiniGrid and introduce MiniGridSafe, a collection of playground environments designed to make shielding easily accessible and experimentally transparent. MiniGridSafe extends MiniGrid with safety-oriented scenarios featuring probabilistic transitions and additional agents, enabling the study of challenging safety aspects in a simple and intuitive setting.

</details>


### [131] [TreeFlash: Parallel AR-Approximation for Faster Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03819)
*Peer Rheinboldt,Frédéric Berdoz,Roger Wattenhofer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TreeFlash提出了一种两阶段近似机制，通过在单次前向传播的草稿生成器中引入MLP层，利用隐藏状态和前一个token来近似自回归分布，解决了非自回归条件化导致的分布偏移问题，在树状推测解码中实现了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单次草稿生成器（one-shot block drafters）在推测解码中虽然消除了顺序token生成，但其非自回归条件化导致草稿分布与验证器的真实自回归分布随着草稿深度增加而产生差异，在树状草稿生成中这一问题更加严重。

Method: TreeFlash在单次草稿生成器中引入一个MLP层，以草稿生成器的隐藏状态和前一个token为条件来近似自回归分布，并通过两阶段近似机制保持O(1)的解码时间复杂度。

Result: 在多种任务和模型上取得SOTA性能，相比边际树草稿（marginal tree drafting），块效率提升12%，加速比提升9%。

Conclusion: TreeFlash通过轻量级MLP层有效缓解了非自回归条件化带来的分布偏移问题，在不牺牲单次草稿生成器效率的前提下显著提升了推测解码的性能。
