Abstract: One-shot block drafters for speculative decoding generate the full draft in a single forward pass, achieving strong throughput by eliminating sequential token generation. However, they predict each draft token conditioned only on the prefix context, with no dependence on previously drafted tokens. This non-autoregressive conditioning causes the drafter's distribution to diverge from the verifier's true autoregressive distribution as draft depth grows. This problem becomes more severe in tree-based drafting, where distinct branches are forced to share the same marginal distribution for subsequent tokens. We propose TreeFlash, which addresses this by incorporating an MLP layer conditioned on the drafter's hidden state and the previous token to approximate an autoregressive distribution. TreeFlash retains the $\mathcal{O}(1)$ decoding time complexity of one-shot drafters by employing a two-stage approximation mechanism. TreeFlash achieves state-of-the-art performance across a variety of tasks and models, improving over marginal tree drafting by $12\%$ higher block efficiency and $9\%$ higher speedup.

</details>


### [132] [Finding Needles in the Haystack: Transductive Active Labeling in Ecology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03821)
*Rupa Kurinchi-Vendhan,Sara Beery*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出当前生态学中主动学习的归纳式评估与实际生态任务（归纳式标注整个数据池）不匹配，尤其是在长尾分布中，目标应从预测转向发现稀有类。作者提出了基于生态稀有化曲线的混合停止准则，以减少过早停止，提高稀有类别的发现率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动学习评估方式（基于测试集的归纳预测性能）与大多数生态学任务的实际目标（归纳式地标注完整数据池）不一致，且忽略了人类标注者的作用，低估了持续标注的重要性，特别是对生态学中重要的稀有类别。

Method: 1. 对比归纳式与归纳式评估在长尾生态数据上的差异；2. 提出一种衡量采样难度的新指标，量化稀有类嵌入在密集常见类潜在几何中的程度；3. 借鉴生态学稀有化曲线，提出一种保守的混合停止准则，结合预测性能与发现标准。

Result: 归纳式目标下，长尾类别的关键挑战从预测转变为发现；混合停止准则能减少在长尾数据池上的过早停止，在发现（而非分类）成为限制因素时显著改善稀有类别的恢复。

Conclusion: 生态学主动学习应转向以发现为导向的归纳式评估框架，采用结合预测与发现标准的停止准则，以更高效地标注稀有但重要的生态类别。

Abstract: Active learning is now standard practice in labeling ecological data, enabling ecologists to quickly process large volumes of field data to understand and monitor natural environments. Current practices evaluate active learning inductively, estimating predictive performance on a held-out test set. We argue that this evaluation is misaligned with most ecological tasks, where the goal is to transductively label an entire pool of data as efficiently as possible. We demonstrate that ignoring the human-in-the-loop underestimates the importance of continuing to label, particularly for classes in the long tail which may be of disproportionate ecological importance (rare species, uncommon behaviors, etc.). Our analysis shows that, for this long tail, the transductive objective shifts importance from prediction to discovery: the true challenge becomes finding "needles in the haystack," examples of rare classes that are embedded within dense regions of abundant classes in the latent geometry, which we quantify with a novel metric of sampling difficulty. Finally, to translate these insights to practical ecological workflows, we propose a conservative hybrid stopping criterion inspired by ecological rarefaction curves, and show that combining predictive performance with discovery criteria reduces premature stopping on long-tailed pools, improving rare-class recovery when discovery, not classification, is the limiting factor.

</details>


### [133] [Dynamic Short Convolutions Improve Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03825)
*Oliver Sieberling,Bharat Runwal,Rameswar Panda,Yoon Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出动态短卷积作为改进Transformer的新原语，通过在键、查询和值表示上应用输入相关的卷积滤波器，在1.3B到2B参数的语言模型中相比标准Transformer取得1.33倍到1.60倍的计算优势，并在线性RNN和混合专家架构上也有改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Transformer在关联记忆等任务上存在不足，静态卷积虽然能提供局部性偏差但表达能力有限，因此需要一种既保持卷积局部性又具有高表达力的新原语来提升Transformer性能。

Method: 引入动态短卷积（dynamic short convolutions），其滤波器根据输入动态生成，而非静态固定。将其应用于Transformer的key、query和value表示上，并与标准Transformer及静态短卷积变体进行对比。同时设计了定制的Triton内核以实现高效训练。

Result: 在150M到2B参数的语言模型实验中，动态卷积一致优于标准Transformer和静态短卷积增强的Transformer。对K/Q/V应用时获得1.33倍计算优势，在每个线性层后添加时获得1.60倍优势。在线性RNN（Mamba-2/Gated DeltaNet）和混合专家架构上也观察到改进。

Conclusion: 动态短卷积是一种可扩展、硬件高效且富有表达力的原语，能够有效推进基于Transformer的语言模型的发展。

Abstract: Transformers have become the dominant architecture for large language models, largely due to the scalability and flexibility of attention, feed-forward layers, residual connections, and normalization. This paper introduces dynamic short convolutions as an additional neural network primitive for improving Transformers. Unlike static short convolutions, dynamic convolutions use input-dependent filters, which preserves the locality bias of convolution while increasing expressivity. Motivating experiments show that applying dynamic short convolutions to key, query, and value representations improves performance on challenging associative recall tasks compared with static convolutional variants. Across language-modeling experiments ranging from 150M to 2B parameters, dynamic convolutions consistently outperform standard Transformers and Transformers augmented with static short convolutions. Fitting scaling laws indicates a 1.33$\times$ compute advantage over compute-matched Transformers when dynamic convolutions are applied to the key, query, and value vectors, and a 1.60$\times$ advantage when adding dynamic convolutions after every linear layer. Dynamic convolutions also offer improvements on linear RNNs (Mamba-2/Gated DeltaNet) and mixture-of-experts architectures. We make these gains practical with custom Triton kernels that enable efficient training with a manageable end-to-end slowdown. These results suggest that dynamic short convolutions are a scalable, hardware-efficient, and expressive primitive for advancing Transformer-based language models.

</details>


### [134] [Online Learning with Gradient-Variation Interval Regret](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03831)
*Yan-Feng Xie,Shuche Wang,Peng Zhao,Zhi-Hua Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对非平稳在线学习问题，提出首个基于梯度变化量实现区间遗憾界的两层在线集成算法，该算法能自适应多种问题依赖量并保持极小极大最优率，同时引入Lipschitz自适应元算法实现超参数自动调节，在区间动态遗憾和随机扩展对抗优化等更广泛场景也具有理论优势，实验验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线学习算法在处理非平稳环境时，难以在每一个时间区间内都保持良好的性能。本文旨在设计一种算法，使其在区间遗憾度量下能够自适应地处理梯度变化等多种问题依赖量，同时避免超参数手动调节的困难。

Method: 提出一个两层在线集成结构：底层集成多个子学习器，顶层通过元算法动态组合子学习器的输出。此外，设计了一个新颖的Lipschitz自适应元算法，使得算法无需预先知道Lipschitz常数和光滑度参数，即可自动适应这些未知常数。

Result: 算法在区间遗憾上达到了与梯度变化量成比例的上界，同时保持了最坏情况下的极小极大最优率；在区间动态遗憾和随机扩展对抗优化等更广泛的设定下也获得了理论保证，实验验证了方法有效性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的算法在非平稳在线学习的区间遗憾度量下取得了突破性进展，其自适应性质和广泛的理论适用性为在线学习领域提供了新的分析工具和实用方法。

Abstract: This paper investigates non-stationary online learning using the metric of interval regret, which requires an online algorithm to perform well over every time interval. We propose the first online learning algorithm that achieves an interval regret bound scaling with gradient variation, a fundamental measure of the cumulative change in online function gradients, which relates to various problem-dependent quantities and is closely connected to stochastic optimization and other problems. Our method employs a simple and efficient two-layer online ensemble structure that achieves strong theoretical guarantees. Specifically, it enjoys a regret bound that simultaneously adapts to various problem-dependent quantities while also preserving the minimax-optimal rate in the worst case. Moreover, recognizing the challenge of hyperparameter tuning, we introduce a Lipschitz- and smoothness-agnostic variant that automatically adapts to these potentially unknown constants. This is primarily enabled by a novel Lipschitz-adaptive meta algorithm, which may be of independent interest. Beyond interval regret, our method also yields broader implications: it provides versatile bounds for interval dynamic regret, a stronger measure that competes with changing comparators over any interval, and yields the first piecewise characterization for stochastic extended adversarial optimization. Theoretical findings are validated by experiments.

</details>


### [135] [Text-attributed Graph Condensation via Text Selection and Attribute Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03839)
*Haowei Han,Yuxiang Wang,Guojia Wan,Hao Wang,Shanshan Feng,Hao Huang,Jiawei Jiang,Xiao Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TAGSAM是一种针对文本属性图（TAG）的图压缩方法，通过子图文本选择和属性相似度匹配两大设计，在压缩文本描述和图拓扑的同时保持训练精度。在1%压缩率下仍保持竞争性准确率，相比最优基线平均提升4.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的TAG模型通常联合训练图神经网络和语言模型，导致在大规模数据集上空间和时间消耗过高，需要一种高效的图压缩方法来解决这一问题。

Method: TAGSAM包含两个关键设计：(1) 子图文本选择——通过最大化互信息，从多个相关文本描述中选择并合并代表性文本块；(2) 属性相似度匹配——通过对齐稳定的相似度矩阵，缓解基于匹配训练轨迹（MTT）的压缩方法中存在的高方差问题。

Result: 在六个最先进基线方法上的评估显示，TAGSAM在相同压缩规模下，准确率平均提升4.9%；即使压缩到原始规模的1%，仍能保持具有竞争力的训练准确率。

Conclusion: TAGSAM通过有效的文本和图拓扑压缩策略，实现了在大幅压缩TAG的同时保持高精度，验证了其方法的优越性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Text-Attributed Graph (TAG) is an important type of graph structured data, where each node has a text description. TAG models usually train a Graph Neural Network (GNN) and language model jointly, which leads to high space and time consumption, especially on large datasets. To mitigate this, we propose TAGSAM, a condensation method that compresses TAGs while preserving training accuracy. TAGSAM comes with two key designs, i.e., subgraph text Selection and Attribute similarity Matching, which compress the text description and graph topology of TAG, respectively. For the texts, subgraph text selection selects and merges representative text chunks from multiple related text descriptions by maximizing mutual information. For the graph topology, popular condensation methods based on Matching Training Trajectories (MTT) suffer from high variance, which hinders accuracy. Our attribute similarity matching mitigates this issue by aligning stable similarity matrices. We evaluate TAGSAM against six state-of-the-art baselines, where it showcases superior performance. For the same compressed size, TAGSAM improves upon the best-performing baseline by an average of 4.9% in accuracy. Furthermore, it maintains competitive training accuracy even when the TAG is condensed to just 1% size. Our code is available at https://github.com/SundayVHan/TAGSAM

</details>


### [136] [Re-Evaluating Continual Learning with Few-Shot Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03843)
*Amogh Inamdar,Matthew So,Vici Milenia,Richard Zemel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出用少样本评估替代零样本评估，更全面地衡量持续学习系统的稳定性和可塑性，并通过新指标“每样本可塑性”发现元学习引入“前瞻性”能诱导任务序列上的学会学习行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的零样本评估无法完全衡量模型或方法在持续学习中保留已学信息或快速适应新信息的能力，因为它需要在多个任务上实现完美回忆。因此需要一种更全面的评估范式。

Method: 提出少样本评估作为持续学习系统稳定性和可塑性的更全面评估方法；引入新指标“每样本可塑性”（per-shot plasticity）；在持续图像分类任务序列上进行细粒度评估；通过元学习未来任务的短序列为持续学习方法添加“前瞻性”。

Result: 少样本评估范式对流行持续学习策略的性能产生了新的见解；通过每样本可塑性指标证明，元学习引入“前瞻性”能诱导任务序列上的学会学习行为。

Conclusion: 少样本评估比零样本评估更能全面衡量持续学习系统的稳定性和可塑性；引入“前瞻性”的元学习方法能提升持续学习性能。

Abstract: Continual learning methods aim to maximize the stability and plasticity of machine learning models that are trained on a sequence of tasks. The standard measure of stability (i.e., forgetting) is the 0-shot performance of a model on previously learned tasks, and plasticity, the performance on the most recently learned task. However, 0-shot evaluation does not fully measure a model or method's ability to retain learned information or adapt quickly to new information, as it requires perfect recall across multiple tasks. In this paper, we propose few-shot evaluation as a more comprehensive assessment of the stability and plasticity of a continual learning system. We conduct a fine-grained assessment on task sequences for continual image classification and find that this paradigm produces novel insights into the performance of popular continual learning strategies. Through few-shot evaluation with a novel metric -- per-shot plasticity -- we show that adding `foresight' to continual learning methods via the meta-learning of a short sequence of future tasks induces learning-to-learn behavior over the task sequence.

</details>


### [137] [Two-Action Apple Tasting with Switching Costs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03851)
*Tommaso Cesari,Roberto Colomboni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了带切换代价的双动作苹果品尝问题，证明了其最小最大遗憾为Θ(√T)，推翻了此前认为该问题可能构成Ω(T^{2/3})下界的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带切换代价的反馈图分类问题中，两动作苹果品尝图被认为是可能的Ω(T^{2/3})下界障碍，若成立将影响一大类仍未分类的反馈图。本文旨在验证这一障碍是否真实存在。

Method: 将问题规范化为揭示动作和盲动作的交替选择，学习者在两者间切换需付出单位代价，遗憾对标最优固定动作。通过对抗性遗忘对手设定，推导出上下界。

Result: 证明该问题的对抗性最小最大期望遗憾满足：1/(2√3)·√T ≤ R_T^* ≤ 2√3·√T，即Θ(√T)量级。

Conclusion: 苹果品尝问题并不构成Ω(T^{2/3})的缺失下界障碍，带切换代价的反馈图分类需要寻找其他结构作为下界来源。

Abstract: We study the two-action apple-tasting problem with switching costs against an oblivious adversary. In an equivalent normalized formulation, at each round the learner chooses between a revealing action and a blind action: the revealing action gives reward $0$ and reveals the hidden value $x_t\in[-1,1]$ of the blind action; the blind action gives reward $x_t$ but reveals nothing. The learner pays one unit whenever they switches actions, and regret is measured against the best fixed action in hindsight.
  General feedback-graph algorithms with switching costs give $\widetilde O(T^{2/3})$ regret guarantees for this problem. The two-action apple-tasting graph was the natural candidate for the missing $Ω(T^{2/3})$ obstruction in the switching-cost classification: such a lower bound would have transferred to a large family of still-unclassified feedback graphs. We prove that this obstruction is not there: the oblivious minimax expected regret for this problem satisfies \[
  \frac{1}{2\sqrt3}\cdot\sqrt T
  \le
  R_T^\star
  \le
  2\sqrt{3}\cdot \sqrt{T}. \]

</details>


### [138] [Attribution via Distributional Paths for Information Revelation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03885)
*Kieran A. Murphy,Shameen Shrestha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Reveal-IG方法，将路径归因从输入空间提升到结构化探针分布空间，通过逐步揭示输入信息来解释模型预测，避免了输入空间路径方法的伪影，并在图像分类和表格回归任务上取得竞争性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于输入空间路径的特征归因方法（如Integrated Gradients）虽然满足完备性，但路径早期（靠近基线）部分与输入本身对解释的贡献权重相同，无法控制特征被查询的分辨率，导致路径伪影问题。

Method: 将路径归因从输入空间迁移到围绕样本的结构化探针分布空间（Reveal-IG），通过逐步揭示输入信息来遍历分布路径，计算模型期望输出的变化归因。

Result: Reveal-IG避免了输入空间路径伪影，在ImageNet分类和表格回归任务上产生稳定的有符号归因，在使用归因符号的指标上领先，其余指标上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: Reveal-IG保留了期望模型响应的完备性，自然适用于多尺度图像探针和表格数据中特征级不确定性，为路径归因提供了更灵活且无伪影的框架。

Abstract: Feature attribution methods explain predictions by assigning importance scores to input features. Path-based methods such as Integrated Gradients are especially appealing because they satisfy \textit{completeness}: attributions sum to the change in model output between a reference state and the input. Yet most path methods define this trajectory in input space, explaining a model through pointwise perturbed inputs along a chosen path. An input-space path integrates the model's raw response at each point it passes through, with no control over the resolution at which a feature is queried; the early, baseline-adjacent part of the trajectory contributes to the explanation on equal footing with the input itself. Here, we lift path attribution from input space to a space of structured probe distributions around the example of interest, and call our method Reveal-IG. Rather than traversing raw input values, Reveal-IG progressively reveals information about the input and attributes changes in the model's expected output along this distributional path. The result is a path-attribution framework that retains completeness with respect to the expected model response, and naturally accommodates multiscale image probes and feature-wise uncertainty in tabular data. Synthetic diagnostics show that Reveal-IG avoids path artifacts that affect input-space methods, and across ImageNet classification and tabular regression it produces stable, signed attributions -- leading on metrics that use attribution sign while remaining competitive on the rest.

</details>


### [139] [Denoise First, Orthogonalize Later: Understanding Momentum in Muon via Spectral Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03899)
*Xianliang Li,Zihan Zhang,Weiyang Liu,Han Bao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了Muon优化器中动量充当频谱过滤器的作用：在信号加扰动的梯度模型下，动量抑制扰动并放大频谱间隙，从而稳定正交化步骤的奇异子空间，提升优化效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon在大语言模型训练中表现优异，但动量在其中的作用理论解释尚不明确。现有研究要么移除动量单独分析频谱更新，要么保留动量但无法解释其为何提升性能。

Method: 提出结构化信号加扰动梯度模型，从理论上证明动量在Muon中作为频谱过滤器，通过抑制扰动、保留主导信号来扩大频谱间隙，进而稳定正交化步骤的奇异子空间。同时证明了先动量后正交化比反向顺序或移除动量能实现更强的信号对齐。

Result: 实验验证了理论分析，在大语言模型预训练等多种任务上支持了动量作为频谱过滤器的观点。

Conclusion: 该理论为理解动量在其他基于矩阵的优化器中的作用提供了起点。

Abstract: Muon has recently demonstrated strong empirical performance in large language model training, but the theoretical role of momentum in Muon remains unclear. Existing analyses of Muon either remove momentum to study spectral updates in isolation, or retain momentum without explaining why it improves empirical performance. Our work bridges this gap by showing momentum in Muon acts as a spectral filter. Under a structured signal-plus-perturbation gradient model, we prove that momentum suppresses perturbations while preserving the dominant signal, thereby enlarging the spectral gap between them. This enlarged gap stabilizes the singular subspaces of the matrix passed to Muon's orthogonalization step, making the resulting update more reliable. We further show that applying momentum before orthogonalization achieves provably stronger alignment with the signal component of the gradient than either reversing this order or simply removing momentum. Experiments across diverse tasks, including LLM pretraining, support our theoretical analysis. More broadly, our theory offers a starting point for understanding the benefits of momentum in other matrix-based optimizers.

</details>


### [140] [MAdam: Metric-Aware Multi-Objective Adam](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03904)
*Fengbei Liu,Rachit Saluja,Sunwoo Kwak,Ruibo Wang,Ruining Deng,Heejong Kim,Johannes C. Paetzold,Mert R. Sabuncu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 现有多目标优化求解器普遍使用Adam作为优化器，但Adam与MOO求解器之间存在权重不匹配和几何不匹配两个系统性缺陷。本文提出MAdam，通过偏好条件曲率预处理方向，使Adam的二阶矩退化为单位矩阵，从而消除上述不匹配，在多个任务上一致提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前MOO求解器（损失均衡、梯度均衡、帕累托类）几乎都使用Adam优化器，但Adam的设计与MOO求解器的意图之间存在系统性偏差，导致优化效果受损。

Method: 提出MAdam，一种即插即用的包装器。它通过偏好条件的标量化目标曲率对协调方向进行预处理（白化），使得输入Adam的梯度协方差矩阵退化为单位矩阵，从而使实际更新由偏好条件度量主导。

Result: 在多任务学习、帕累托前沿恢复、物理信息神经网络和医学成像等多个任务上，MAdam在所有求解器族上均一致优于Adam。

Conclusion: MAdam通过消除Adam与MOO求解器之间的权重不匹配和几何不匹配，有效提升多目标优化的性能，且无需修改求解器或优化器本身。

Abstract: Multi-objective optimization (MOO) underlies many machine learning problems, yet MOO solvers across the loss-balancing, gradient-balancing, and Pareto-based families almost universally hand their reconciled directions to Adam~\cite{kingma2015adam}. We show this coupling introduces two systematic gaps between the solver's intent and the optimizer's execution. The first is a \emph{weighting mismatch}: Adam's second-moment denominator entangles the time-varying preference vector with gradient statistics, marginalizing the preference into a history average and collapsing distinct Pareto trade-offs toward a near-uniform mixture. The second is a \emph{geometric mismatch}: Adam's adaptive metric distorts the Euclidean geometry MOO solvers assume, turning aligned objectives into apparent conflicts. To resolve both jointly, we introduce \textbf{MAdam} (Metric-Aware Multi-Objective Adam), a drop-in wrapper that leaves both solver and optimizer unchanged. MAdam preconditions the reconciled direction by the preference-conditioned curvature of the scalarized objective; on this whitened input, Adam's second moment collapses to identity, so the realized update is governed by the preference-conditioned metric. Across multi-task learning, Pareto-front recovery, physics-informed neural networks, and medical imaging, MAdam consistently improves over Adam for every solver family.

</details>


### [141] [Contrastive Neural Algorithmic Reasoning for Graph Coloring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03923)
*Thien Le,Tianyu Zhao,Melanie Weber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种对比学习框架，用于学习可迁移的图着色几何结构，使同色节点嵌入对齐、相邻节点嵌入正交，从而在未见过的图上实现有效的近似k-着色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无监督GNN方法逐实例优化，无法跨图大小和分布进行泛化；本文旨在学习可迁移的着色几何表征，实现跨图泛化。

Method: 采用对比学习框架，使同色节点嵌入对齐到同一子空间，相邻节点嵌入推向正交方向；通过单位范数嵌入下的线-原型结构分析和未归一化变体下的最大边际偏差分析，建立理论保证。

Result: 在合成图和真实图上，对比GNN编码器能有效泛化，产生低冲突着色，性能匹配甚至超越贪心方法。

Conclusion: 对比学习框架能学习可迁移的着色几何结构，实现跨图大小的泛化，为图着色问题提供了一种高效的监督或无监督学习方案。

Abstract: Graph coloring seeks to assigns colors to a graph's nodes so that adjacent nodes receive different colors, using as few colors as possible. Here, we study approximate $k$-coloring, where the goal is to use at most $k$ colors while minimizing the number of monochromatic edges. This problem is central to graph theory and has applications in areas such as scheduling and resource allocation. Recent unsupervised GNN approaches optimize each instance directly, precluding generalization across graph sizes and distributions. We instead propose a contrastive learning framework that learns transferable coloring geometry where the embeddings of same-color nodes align, while adjacent nodes' representations are pushed toward distinct directions. We analyze the resulting population objective over bounded-size graphs. For unit-norm embeddings, we show that its optima have a line-prototype structure: Representations of nodes of the same color collapse to a shared one-dimensional subspace, and edges connect orthogonal subspaces. This geometry yields stationarity conditions in the supervised setting and is preserved by projected subgradient dynamics under a balanced-coloring assumption. In an unnormalized variant, gradient descent has a max-margin bias governed by a quotient-graph hard-margin problem. Experiments on synthetic and real-world graphs show that contrastive GNN encoders generalize effectively and produce low-conflict colorings, matching and sometimes improving on greedy approaches.

</details>


### [142] [FFR: Forward-Forward Learning for Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03927)
*Xinyang Liu,Xuanyu Liang,Shiqi Ding,Boyang Li,Zhiqiang Que,Jiayang Li,Guosheng Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FFR算法，首次将前向-前向(FF)算法扩展到回归任务，通过序数竞争性 goodness 函数、分层阶梯架构和多尺度预测等创新，在五个真实回归基准上平均恢复BP算法98.6%的精度，同时将峰值训练内存降至BP的27%（深度8）和8%（深度32）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FF算法虽提供计算高效且生物合理的BP替代方案，但本质设计为通过对比正负样本对进行分类，难以扩展到回归任务：连续目标空间缺乏天然的"对立面"，标准goodness函数不携带目标量值或排序信息。

Method: (1) 序数竞争性goodness函数：用分区神经元组间的竞争学习替代对比对，引入距离感知的序数监督；(2) 分层阶梯架构：浅层学习粗粒度序数判别，深层细化为细粒度回归，多尺度特征聚合促进层间协作；(3) 层次化预测与不确定性估计：多尺度预测器联合提供鲁棒预测及预测置信度。

Result: 五个真实回归基准平均恢复BP 98.6%精度；峰值训练内存深度8时仅BP的27%，深度32时仅8%；每迭代时间约BP的72%；显著优于所有无BP的竞争者。

Conclusion: 提出FFR框架，首次将FF算法成功扩展到回归任务并取得与BP竞争的性能，同时大幅降低内存消耗，为生物合理且高效的学习提供了新方向。

Abstract: The Forward-Forward (FF) algorithm offers a computationally efficient and biologically plausible alternative to backpropagation (BP) by training neural networks through purely local, layer-wise optimization. However, FF is inherently designed for classification via contrastive positive-negative sample pairs, and extending it to regression poses fundamental challenges: continuous target space lack natural "opposites" for contrastive learning, and the standard goodness function carries no information about target magnitude or ordering. We propose FFR (Forward-Forward for Regression), to our knowledge, the first framework to extend FF to real-world regression and demonstrate competitive performance across diverse real-world datasets. FFR introduces three key innovations: (1) an ordinal competitive goodness function that replaces contrastive pairs with competitive learning between partitioned neuron groups under distance-aware ordinal supervision; (2) a stratified ladder architecture where shallow layers learn coarse ordinal discrimination and deeper layers refine into fine-grained regression, with multi-scale feature aggregation for inter-layer collaboration; and (3) hierarchical prediction with uncertainty estimation, where multi-scale predictors jointly provide robust predictions and prediction confidence as a free-lunch. Extensive experimental results show FFR recovers on average 98.6% of BP's accuracy across five real-world regression benchmarks while reducing peak training memory to only 27% of BP's at depth 8 and 8% at depth 32, with per-iteration time around 72% of BP's, and substantially outperforms all BP-free competitors.

</details>


### [143] [Value-Aware Stochastic KV Cache Eviction for Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03928)
*Ting-Yun Chang,Harvey Yiyun Fu,Deqing Fu,Chenghao Yang,Jesse Thomason,Robin Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了KV缓存淘汰中两个关键因素——大数值状态导致模型陷入重复推理循环，以及引入随机性可提升缓存多样性——并据此提出VaSE方法，在4倍压缩下比最强淘汰方法准确率高4%以上，且支持FlashAttention2和静态内存占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理模型通过长链式思考提升准确率，但长输出导致显存和计算瓶颈。现有KV缓存淘汰方法虽然能降低开销，但准确率不如保留完整缓存的稀疏注意力选择方法，亟需弥合效率与精度之间的差距。

Method: 提出VaSE（Value-aware Stochastic KV Cache Eviction），一种无需训练的淘汰策略：1）保护具有异常大幅度的值状态不被淘汰，避免模型陷入重复推理循环；2）在淘汰过程中引入随机性，增加缓存多样性，提升准确率。

Result: 在6个推理任务上，Qwen3模型使用VaSE在4倍KV缓存压缩下，平均准确率超过当前最先进的稀疏选择方法，并且比最强淘汰方法高出4%以上。

Conclusion: VaSE有效弥合了KV缓存淘汰方法的效率与精度差距，支持FlashAttention2，并能实现推理模型的静态内存占用，为长推理模型的高效部署提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Reasoning models improve accuracy through extended chains of thought, but their long outputs create a memory and compute bottleneck. KV cache eviction methods reduce this cost by evicting unimportant key-value pairs from the cache, yet they often yield worse accuracy than selection-based sparse attention alternatives, which keep the full KV cache. We identify key factors crucial to KV cache eviction accuracy. First, a small fraction of value states have abnormally large magnitudes, and evicting them causes catastrophic failure where models enter repetitive reasoning loops. Second, introducing stochasticity during eviction improves accuracy by increasing cache diversity. Based on these findings, we propose Value-aware Stochastic KV Cache Eviction (VaSE), a training-free recipe that protects large-magnitude value states and promotes diverse eviction decisions. Across six reasoning tasks, Qwen3 models using VaSE with 4x KV cache compression yield higher average accuracies than SOTA selection method at the same sparsity, while outperforming the strongest eviction method by more than 4%. Overall, VaSE bridges the gap between efficiency and accuracy, supporting FlashAttention2 and enabling a static memory footprint for reasoning models.

</details>


### [144] [Correcting Neural Operator Spectral Bias via Diffusion Posterior Sampling with Sparse Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03936)
*Niccolò Perrone,Fanny Lehmann,Stefania Fresca,Filippo Gatti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FreqNO-DPS框架，融合神经算子代理与扩散后验采样，通过频谱整形引导校正高频偏差，在稀疏传感器条件下实现近零频谱偏差的PDE解预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经算子代理虽计算快但存在频谱偏差（高频信息被系统性衰减），而稀疏传感器测量虽点精确但覆盖不全。两者互补后，直接融合会重新引入代理的频谱偏差，因此需要一种频谱校准的融合方法。

Method: 将NO预测作为扩散后验采样中的辅助观测，引入闭式频谱整形引导分数，该分数按频率依赖精度加权代理输出，无需去噪器反向传播；提供一致性诊断工具验证代理残差的近似频谱对角性。

Result: 在3D弹性波场预测中，5%和2%传感器覆盖率下各频段近零频谱偏差；各向同性引导基线虽提升点精度但保留偏差，证实频谱依赖校准的必要性。

Conclusion: 频谱依赖的引导校准是融合代理与后验采样的关键，而非仅有益；框架仅需配对代理/参考数据，无问题特定结构依赖。

Abstract: Neural operator surrogates (NO) approximate PDE solutions orders of magnitude faster than numerical solvers, but suffer from spectral bias: high-frequency content is systematically attenuated, limiting reliability where fine-scale structure matters. Sparse sensor measurements of the field are often available too, offering pointwise accuracy without spectral distortion but covering only a small fraction of the domain. We address this by treating NO predictions as auxiliary observations in a diffusion posterior sampling framework. Our method, FreqNO-DPS (https://github.com/niccoloperrone/FreqNO-DPS), combines an unconditional score-based diffusion prior, trained on high-fidelity simulations, with diffusion posterior sampling (DPS) conditioned on sparse observations and guided by a frozen neural operator. Naive integration reintroduces the surrogate's spectral bias; we resolve this with a closed-form, spectrally shaped guidance score that weights the surrogate by its frequency-dependent accuracy and needs no denoiser backpropagation. A distribution-free analysis bounds the approximation error across the frequency-diffusion-time plane and shows the guidance's frequency dependence is preserved regardless of distributional assumptions. On 3D elastic wavefield prediction at 5% and 2% sensor coverage, the method reaches near-zero spectral bias across all bands, where both the surrogate and sensor-only DPS show systematic high-frequency attenuation. Isotropic guidance, the natural baseline, improves pointwise accuracy but carries the bias into the posterior nearly intact, confirming that frequency-dependent calibration is essential, not merely beneficial. The framework needs only paired surrogate/reference data and exploits no problem-specific structure beyond the residual's approximate spectral diagonality, verifiable for new surrogates via the coherence diagnostic we provide.

</details>


### [145] [q0: Primitives for Hyper-Epoch Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03938)
*Bishwas Mandal,Shmuel Berman,Akshay Vegesna,Samip Dahal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出超纪元预训练（q0），通过将多轮训练预算转化为多样化模型群体并聚合其预测，相比单模型精炼能实现更低验证损失和更高数据效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前计算资源增长速度快于高质量文本供给，多轮训练成为标准，但单模型在数轮后便饱和，无法充分利用计算预算。作者认为需要从训练单一模型转向探索模型群体并聚合预测。

Method: q0包含三个核心原语：（1）使用反相关学习率和权重衰减的循环调度，从少数并行轨迹收集多样化模型；（2）链式蒸馏，每个模型向其前驱学习，使模型质量在群体中累积；（3）基于留出集拟合的学习先验，为任意推理预算选择和加权成员。

Result: 在1.8B参数模型、100M FineWeb数据上，q0以约56轮（约4.6倍更少）匹配256轮强集成基线，或以约67轮（约3.8倍更少）匹配基线集成规模，且持续改进。在Slowrun设置下实现累计约12.9倍数据效率提升，并迁移到下游基准。

Conclusion: 最优分配随预算变化，作者给出了在给定轮次预算下最大化泛化的处方性建议，覆盖从单轮到最大预算的不同场景。

Abstract: Multi-epoch training is becoming the standard now that compute is growing faster than the supply of high-quality text. But pretraining a single model saturates within a few passes, long before the compute budget is exhausted. We argue this calls for a conceptual shift from training a single model toward exploring a population of models and aggregating their predictions. We introduce hyper-epoch pretraining (q0), which turns a multi-epoch budget into a population of diverse models whose combined predictions reach a lower validation loss than a single refined model. q0 reduces to three core primitives. A cyclic schedule with anti-correlated learning rate and weight decay collects diverse models from a few parallel trajectories. Chain distillation trains each model against its predecessor so that model quality compounds across the population. A learned prior, fit on a held out set, selects and weights members for any inference budget. On a 1.8B-parameter model trained on 100M FineWeb tokens, q0 matches a strong 256-epoch ensemble baseline using only ${\sim}56$ epochs (${\sim}4.6\times$ fewer), or ${\sim}67$ epochs (${\sim}3.8\times$ fewer) when matched to the baseline's ensemble size, and continues to improve beyond it. These gains reach cumulative ${\sim}12.9\times$ data efficiency under the Slowrun setting and transfer to downstream benchmarks. Crucially, the optimal allocation shifts with the budget, so we give prescriptive recipes for how to spend a given epoch budget to maximize generalization, from a single epoch up to the largest budgets.

</details>


### [146] [Language Models Need Sleep: Learning to Self-Modify and Consolidate Memories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03979)
*Ali Behrouz,Farnoosh Hashemi,Vahab Mirrokni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种受人类睡眠过程启发的“Sleep”范式，通过Memory Consolidation（知识播种+泛化蒸馏）和Dreaming（基于RL的自我改进）两个阶段，使机器学习模型能够持续学习、将短期记忆转化为长期知识，并在无需人工监督的情况下自我提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习模型（包括LLMs）缺乏持续学习能力，无法将时间上下文中的短期知识有效迁移到长期参数中，而人类能够通过睡眠过程巩固记忆并自我改进。

Method: 提出Sleep范式，包含两个阶段：(1) Memory Consolidation：通过Knowledge Seeding（结合on-policy蒸馏与基于RL的模仿学习的泛化蒸馏过程）将小模型的记忆向上蒸馏到大模型中；(2) Dreaming：模型使用RL生成合成数据课程，在无人监督条件下排练新知识并改进现有能力。

Result: 在长程任务、持续学习、知识融合和少样本泛化等实验任务中验证了睡眠阶段的重要性，表明该方法能有效提升模型性能。

Conclusion: 受人类睡眠启发的Sleep范式（记忆巩固+梦境自我改进）为机器学习模型实现持续学习和知识迁移提供了有效途径。

Abstract: The past few decades have witnessed significant advances in the design of machine learning algorithms, from early studies on task-specific shallow models to more general deep Large Language Models (LLMs). Despite showing promising results in tasks that require instant prediction or in-context learning, existing models lack the ability to continually learn and effectively transfer their temporal in-context knowledge to their long-term parameters. Inspired by human learning process, we introduce a ''Sleep'' paradigm that allows the models to continually learn, distill their short-term fragile memories into stable long-term knowledge with replay, and recursively improve themselves with ''Dreaming'' process. In more detail, sleep consists of two stages: (1) Memory Consolidation: an upward distillation process, called Knowledge Seeding, where the memories of a smaller-self are distilled into a larger network to provide more capacity while preserving the knowledge. As a proof of concept, we present a new Generalized Distillation process for {Knowledge Seeding} (i.e., the combination of on-policy distillation with Reinforcement Learning (RL)-based imitation learning); (2) Dreaming: a self-improvement phase, where the model uses RL to generate a curriculum of synthetic data to rehearse new knowledge and refine existing capabilities without human supervision. Our experiments on long-horizon, continual learning, knowledge incorporation, and few-shot generalization tasks support the importance of the sleep stage.

</details>


### [147] [Skill-RM: Unifying Heterogeneous Evaluation Criteria via Agent Skill](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03980)
*Tao Chen,Gangwei Jiang,Pengyu Cheng,Siyuan Huang,Yihao Liu,Jingwei Ni,Jiaqi Guo,Mengyu Zhou,Kai Tang,Junling Liu,Qinliang Su,Xiaoxi Jiang,Guanjun Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Skill-RM，一种将奖励建模重构为可复用"奖励评估技能"执行的统一框架，通过将奖励计算视为结构化智能体任务，动态选择和聚合异质证据，在奖励基准测试和下游应用（Best-of-N选择、强化学习）中一致优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前奖励评估依赖基于规则的验证器、真实参考、程序化检查表和复杂评分标准等异质标准，缺乏统一机制来整合所有类型的证据。

Method: Skill-RM将奖励建模重构为可复用"奖励评估技能"的执行，将奖励计算视为结构化智能体任务，提供一致接口来编排异质资源，动态选择并聚合适合每个输入特定要求的证据。

Result: 在奖励基准测试及下游应用（Best-of-N选择、强化学习）中，Skill-RM一致优于传统评判基线。

Conclusion: Skill-RM不仅为奖励建模提供了统一解决方案，而且通过战略性和动态化的证据编排实现了更优性能。

Abstract: Reward models (RMs) provide critical feedback signals for LLM post-training, notably in reinforced fine-tuning (RFT) and reinforcement learning (RL) pipelines. However, current reward evaluation relies on heterogeneous criteria such as rule-based verifiers, ground-truth references, procedural checklists, and complex rubrics, where a unified mechanism to integrate all types of evidence remains unexplored. To this end, we propose Skill Reward Model (Skill-RM), a unified framework that reformulates reward modeling as the execution of a reusable Reward-Evaluation Skill. By treating reward computation as a structured agentic task, Skill-RM provides a consistent interface to orchestrate heterogeneous resources, dynamically selecting and aggregating evidence tailored to the specific requirements of each input. This approach enables the reward model to move beyond static evaluation, ensuring consistency and transparency across diverse tasks. Extensive experiments on reward benchmarks and downstream applications, including best-of-N selection and reinforcement learning, demonstrate that Skill-RM consistently outperforms traditional judge baselines. Our findings suggest that Skill-RM not only provides a unified solution for reward modeling but also achieves superior performance through the strategic and dynamic orchestration of evidence. The code is at https://github.com/Qwen-Applications/Skill-RM.

</details>


### [148] [Neuron Populations Exhibit Divergent Selectivity with Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03990)
*Amil Dravid,Yasaman Bahri,Alexei A. Efros,Yossi Gandelsman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究神经网络中神经元群体是否随模型规模可预测地演化，发现Rosetta神经元数量遵循次线性幂律增长，并观察到神经元极化效应：Rosetta神经元随规模增大变得更选择性和单语义化，而非Rosetta神经元则保持较低选择性。一个分析性模型解释了这种次线性幂律缩放和极化效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究神经元群体结构是否像宏观可观测指标（如损失）一样随模型规模呈现可预测的缩放规律，从而将缩放定律从宏观层面扩展到神经元层面的可解释结构。

Method: 分别分析语言模型（最高30B参数）和视觉模型（最高5B参数）中Rosetta神经元的演化规律，构建分析性模型平衡特征效用与有限神经元容量来解释观测现象，并通过数据过滤案例研究展示其选择性。

Result: Rosetta神经元绝对数量随规模增长但占总神经元比例呈次线性幂律下降；出现神经元极化效应，Rosetta神经元变得更选择性和单语义化；Rosetta神经元随规模增大变得更领域专化。

Conclusion: 存在关于可解释、共享神经元层级结构的缩放定律，将模型规模与神经元普遍性、选择性和专化性的系统性变化联系起来。

Abstract: We investigate whether neuron populations within neural networks evolve predictably with scale, extending scaling laws beyond macroscopic observables such as loss. To probe this question, we study Rosetta Neurons, a previously characterized class of neurons whose activation patterns are similar across independently trained models (Dravid et al., 2023). In separate analyses of language models up to 30B parameters and vision models up to 5B parameters, we observe that the population of Rosetta Neurons follows a sublinear power law in model size, growing in absolute number but occupying a shrinking fraction of the total neuron count. We further observe a Neuron Polarization Effect: Rosetta Neurons become more selective and increasingly monosemantic with scale, separating from a growing non-Rosetta population that remains less selective. An analytical model balancing feature utility against limited neuron capacity explains the sublinear power-law scaling and this polarization effect. Finally, we find that Rosetta Neurons become more domain-specialized with scale and illustrate their selectivity through a targeted data-filtering case study for continued pretraining. Our results point to a scaling law for interpretable, shared neuron-level structure, linking model size to systematic changes in neuron universality, selectivity, and specialization.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [149] [InquiryBits: Sharing AI Conversation Traces to Support Collaboration Within Trust Boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02763)
*Caitlin Morris,Pattie Maes*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: AI聊天工具正在将问题解决和头脑风暴从同事间对话转向私密AI交互，减少了支持团队协调的共享意识。InquiryBits系统可在可配置的信任边界内共享AI对话的简洁摘要。研究发现80名专业人士愿意在有限群体内共享这些痕迹以支持协作，但舒适度随受众扩大而急剧下降，信任边界比信息粒度更重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI聊天工具使人们转向私密AI交互，导致团队共享意识减弱，影响协作协调。

Method: 设计了InquiryBits系统，在可配置信任边界内共享AI对话的最小摘要；通过80名专业人士实验评估共享意愿。

Result: 人们愿意在紧密团队内共享AI对话痕迹以避免重复工作，但舒适度随受众扩大而下降；详细信息量影响小于受众范围；信任边界内偏好更多细节。

Conclusion: 信任边界比信息粒度是更关键的设计参数。

Abstract: AI chat tools are shifting problem-solving and brainstorming conversations away from colleagues and into private AI interactions, reducing the shared awareness that supports team coordination. We introduce InquiryBits, a system that shares minimal summaries of AI conversations within configurable trust boundaries, separating AI-only analysis from human-visible sharing. In a study with 80 professionals, we find that people are broadly willing to share these traces to support collaboration and avoid duplicating work - but only within bounded groups. Comfort drops sharply as audience expands beyond close teams; the level of detail shared matters less than who can see it, with a preference for more detail over less within trusted groups. These findings suggest that trust boundaries, more than information granularity, may be the most impactful design parameter.

</details>


### [150] [LLM-Assisted Reranking to Operationalize Nuanced Objectives in Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02883)
*Amir Ghasemian,Homa Hosseinmardi,Upasana Dutta,Duncan J. Watts*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM辅助重排序在新闻推荐中是否会在提升个性化程度的同时，无意中增加用户接触意识形态极端或阴谋论政治内容的风险。实验表明，无约束的LLM重排序会强化个性化但加剧极端内容暴露，而轻量级的提示词约束（保持主题相关性、扩大意识形态多样性）可显著减少极端内容推广并提升意识形态多样性，仅带来轻微相关性损失。合成实验揭示LLM基于语言统计规律而非语义理解进行重排序，并强调提示词设计是价值承载而非中立的默认行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推荐系统大多优化参与度或准确率指标，很少关注更广泛的社会影响（如个性化如何重塑用户在具有社会后果的领域中的信息接触）。作者希望实证考察LLM辅助重排序在提升个性化程度时，是否会无意中加剧用户暴露于意识形态极端或阴谋论政治内容的风险，这一风险虽被理论预测但缺乏经验性特征刻画。

Method: 使用真实的新闻消费历史数据，对YouTube侧边栏候选内容进行基于零样本、指令提示的LLM重排序。对比基线提示词与一个受约束变体（保持主题相关性、拓宽意识形态暴露面、减少阴谋论/极端内容）。同时进行合成实验探究LLM重排序的内在机制。

Result: 无约束重排序增强了个性化，但增加了曾消费过极端/阴谋论内容的用户接触此类材料的风险。轻量级提示词正则化可在仅带来轻微相关性损失的情况下，减少极端内容推广并提升意识形态多样性。合成实验表明LLM基于语言统计规律而非语义理解进行重排序。

Conclusion: LLM有能力在高风险推荐中操作化上下文细微差别，但需要超越准确率指标评估LLM辅助个性化，并将提示词设计视为价值承载而非中立的默认选择。

Abstract: Recommender systems have grown from content-organization tools into sophisticated systems that shape daily behavior. By controlling what we see, they shape what we perceive, raising concerns about filter bubbles, radicalization, polarization, and social inequality. Large language models (LLMs) enable more powerful personalization, intensifying these dynamics. Yet most recommenders are tuned for engagement or limited accuracy metrics, with little attention to broader social implications, e.g. how personalization reshapes exposure in socially consequential domains. We investigate whether LLM-assisted reranking, while improving personalization, inadvertently amplifies exposure to ideologically extreme or conspiratorial political content, a risk theorized but not empirically characterized in news recommendation. Using real news-consumption histories, we rerank YouTube's sidebar candidates through zero-shot, instruction-based prompting. We compare a baseline prompt with a constrained variant that preserves topical relevance and broadens ideological exposure while reducing conspiratorial or extreme content. Without constraints, reranking strengthened personalization but increased exposure to conspiratorial and extremist material for users whose histories contained such content. Lightweight prompt-level regularization reduced promotion of extreme content and increased ideological diversity, with modest relevance loss. Synthetic experiments suggest that LLMs rerank via statistical regularities in language rather than semantic understanding of ideology, clarifying why naive prompts amplify these patterns and why regularization can reshape them. Together, our results highlight the power of LLMs to operationalize contextual nuance in high-stakes recommendation, and the need to evaluate LLM-assisted personalization beyond accuracy and treat prompt design as a value-laden rather than neutral default.

</details>


### [151] [Characterization and Effects of CS2 Learning with GenAI, Visualization, and Human Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02933)
*Quinton Yong,Anthony Estey,Miguel Nacenta*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 在高级算法课程中，对比生成式AI、算法可视化和人工实时辅导三种学习支持方式，发现GenAI虽提升自我效能感，但学习效果最差；人工辅导效果最佳，而算法可视化未被有效利用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究多关注GenAI在入门编程课中的负面影响（过度依赖、元认知问题），但缺乏对高级计算机科学概念学习的机制理解及泛化性验证。

Method: 混合方法研究：12名学生在二年级算法课程中参与3次90分钟学习（排序、树、图算法），记录眼动和交互数据，每次结束后进行概念理解测试，分析求助时机和交互模式。

Result: GenAI显著提升自我效能感，但学习效果明显低于人工辅导；算法可视化未被有效使用；GenAI在高级主题学习中存在使用障碍；人工辅导学习效果最高。

Conclusion: 不同学习支持方式对高级算法学习效果差异显著，GenAI的自我效能提升与学习效果下降并存，需谨慎使用；算法可视化需改进使用指导；人工辅导仍是最高效方式。

Abstract: Generative AI (GenAI) is becoming a widely adopted learning support tool for both students and instructors, as it offers benefits such as personalized tutoring and scaffolded learning. However, recent research highlights potential drawbacks such as overreliance and metacognitive issues, especially in novice programmers. Most prior work focuses on introductory programming courses, and important questions remain about the underlying mechanisms behind the negative effects of GenAI and if findings can be generalized when students learn more advanced computer science concepts. To address this gap, we conducted a mixed-methods study comparing student interactions with GenAI to two traditional learning supports in a second-year algorithms course: algorithm visualization (AV) and human live tutoring (LT). Twelve students participated in three 90-minute study sessions focusing on sorting, tree, and graph algorithms. We recorded gaze and interaction data, and each session concluded with a test assessing their conceptual understanding of the topic. Our analysis classifies when during the problem-solving process participants sought help, and compares the interaction patterns across the three learning supports. Although GenAI produced a larger increase in self-efficacy compared to live tutoring, it was associated with noticeably lower results in learning outcomes. We found that participants did not use algorithm visualizations effectively, faced usage barriers when using GenAI to learn advanced topics, and that live tutoring yielded the highest learning outcomes.

</details>


### [152] [From Explanation to Diagnosis: Next Generation Interactive Video Coach with Misstep Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02970)
*Xiao Jin,Rahul K. Dass,Ashok K. Goel*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Ivy（一种神经符号AI教练）的增强功能，通过双模型架构（TMK模型+教学模型PM）实现错误感知辅导。PM将教师的诊断知识显式编码为机器可读形式，包括每个错误答案背后的学习者信念、TMK定位、误解类型和针对性支架。实验证明该流水线能检测和分类学习者错误，生成基于诊断的支架反馈，推动AI辅导系统从知识检索向诊断性错误感知方向发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能辅导系统擅长生成解释，但缺乏对学习者错误位置和原因的原则性诊断能力。研究者希望填补这一空白，让AI教练不仅能提供知识，还能像人类教师一样诊断错误根源。

Method: 采用双模型架构：在Task-Method-Knowledge (TMK)模型基础上增加Pedagogical Model (PM)。PM将教师的Q&A答案编码为机器可读的诊断知识，包含：学习者潜在信念、TMK定位（误解来源）、误解类型和针对性支架。通过在线研究生AI课程中的测验问题，构建了端到端流水线进行错误检测、分类和诊断驱动支架生成。

Result: 成功验证了概念验证流水线，能够检测和分类学习者错误，并生成基于诊断的支架反馈。将Ivy从知识检索系统提升为具备诊断性错误感知能力的辅导系统。

Conclusion: 该方法使AI辅导系统能够提供更精确、可操作的反馈，支持概念转变，推进了AI教育领域自适应学习系统的发展方向。

Abstract: Intelligent tutoring systems excel at generating explanations but rarely provide principled diagnosis of where and why a learner is wrong. We introduce a misstep-aware coaching capability for Ivy, a neurosymbolic AI coach, built on a two-model architecture that augments a Task-Method-Knowledge (TMK) model with a new Pedagogical Model (PM) in the context of an online graduate AI course at Georgia Tech. The PM makes instructor diagnostic knowledge explicit and machine-readable by encoding, for each quiz question and incorrect response, the learner's underlying belief(a brief statement of the incorrect idea or missing knowledge), a TMK locus(the source of the misunderstanding), a misconception type and targeted scaffolding derived from the instructor's Q\&A key. Using quiz questions from the course, we demonstrate a proof-of-concept pipeline that detects and classifies learner errors and generates diagnosis-grounded scaffolding, moving Ivy beyond knowledge retrieval toward diagnostic misstep awareness, and enabling more precise, actionable feedback that supports conceptual change and advances adaptive learning systems in AI in education and the learning sciences.

</details>


### [153] [A Benchmarking Framework for Multimodal User Interface Toolkits: Comparing Modality Coverage, Developer Workflow, and Experimental Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02977)
*Ariton Verush*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个针对多模态用户界面工具包的基准测试框架，从模态覆盖、开发者体验和实验支持三个维度对Geno、MSP、ReactGenie、WAMI和EmoSync五个工具包进行结构化比较，提供了一个可复用的评估模板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态用户界面工具包日益增多，但缺乏系统性、可复用的比较方法来评估这些工具包的功能支持、开发工作量和评价策略，导致开发者难以选择合适的工具。

Method: 基于文档分析、技术比较和未来开发者评估的结构化基准框架，从模态覆盖与交互抽象、开发者体验与工作流、实验与集成支持三个维度进行分析，并通过五个代表性工具包进行框架示例说明。

Result: 提出了一个可复用的基准测试模板，涵盖三个评估维度，并用Geno、MSP、ReactGenie、WAMI和EmoSync五个工具包进行了框架示例展示，但尚未报告完整的实证结果。

Conclusion: 该基准框架可为未来研究者提供可实例化的模板，用于开展经验测量、开发者研究和更多多模态工具包的比较评估。

Abstract: Multimodal user interfaces increasingly combine speech, gesture, vision, gaze, touch, biosignals, and other sensor data. Recent toolkits from the past five years, such as Geno, Multisensor-Pipeline (MSP), ReactGenie, and EmoSync, aim to make it easier for developers to prototype such interfaces, while older work such as WAMI shows how early web-based multimodal systems were conceived. Yet the field still lacks a systematic and reusable way to compare what these toolkits actually support, how much implementation work they offload from developers, and which evaluation strategies are appropriate for them. This paper reframes an HCI seminar draft into a benchmarking framework paper for multimodal user interface toolkits. Rather than reporting completed empirical results, it proposes a structured benchmark based on document analysis, technical comparison, and a future developer-based evaluation. The framework is organized around three dimensions: modality coverage and interaction abstraction, developer experience and workflow, and experimental and integration support. The paper illustrates the framework through five representative toolkits: Geno, MSP, ReactGenie, WAMI, and EmoSync. The contribution is a reusable benchmark template that future researchers can instantiate with empirical measurements, developer studies, and additional multimodal toolkits.

</details>


### [154] [Hanger Reflex Based Driving Assistance for Drivers with Peripheral Visual Field Defects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03020)
*Hailong Liu,Junya Wada,Toshihiro Hiraoka,Junpei Kuwana,Makoto Itoh,Takahiro Wada*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究探索了衣架反射提示（HRC）作为一种针对周边视野缺损驾驶员的辅助驾驶方法，通过施加机械压力于头部特定区域，促进驾驶员提前朝向潜在危险行人。驾驶模拟器实验（15名参与者）表明，HRC显著增加了驾驶员头部旋转角度和注视时长，并降低了碰撞发生率，结构方程模型分析进一步揭示了从头部旋转到注视分配再到碰撞减少的序列路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 周边视野缺损驾驶员可能无法注意到周边视野中的行人，导致危险意识延迟和碰撞风险增加，现有辅助方法尚不足，因此需要探索新的驾驶辅助手段。

Method: 采用驾驶模拟器实验方法，招募15名参与者，在模拟周边视野缺损条件下对比有无HRC的行人遭遇场景，采集头部旋转角度、注视时长、碰撞发生等行为数据，并采用分段结构方程模型进行分析。

Result: HRC显著改变了驾驶员朝向危险行人的头部旋转角度模态值，显著增加了对该行人的注视时长；碰撞发生率在HRC条件下低于无HRC条件，但直接效应仅呈边际显著性；分段结构方程模型显示HRC可能通过"头部旋转→注视分配→碰撞减少"的序列路径降低碰撞。

Conclusion: HRC能够支持对周边危险的预期性注意力分配，为周边视野缺损驾驶员提供了一种有前景的驾驶辅助方法。

Abstract: Drivers with peripheral visual field defects may fail to notice pedestrians in their peripheral visual field, leading to delayed hazard awareness and increased collision risk. This study explores hanger reflex cue (HRC) as a driving assistance method for drivers with peripheral visual field defects, in which mechanical pressure is applied to specific regions of the head to facilitate anticipatory orientation toward potentially risky pedestrians and support safer driving. In a driving simulator experiment with 15 participants, we compared driving behavior with and without HRC during pedestrian encounters under simulated peripheral visual field defect. The results showed that HRC significantly shifted drivers' modal head rotation angle toward the risky pedestrian and significantly increased gaze duration toward that pedestrian. Collision occurrence was lower in the w/ HRC condition than in the w/o HRC condition, although the direct effect of HRC on collision occurrence showed only a marginal trend. A piecewise structural equation modeling analysis further suggested that HRC may contribute to collision reduction through a sequential pathway from head rotation to gaze allocation and then to collision occurrence. These findings provide preliminary evidence that HRC can support anticipatory attention allocation toward peripheral hazards and may offer a promising driving assistance method for drivers with visual field impairment.

</details>


### [155] [AI Assistance for Discretionary Work: Increasing Feedback Provision in Higher Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03095)
*Romina Mahinpei,Victoria Dean,Ruth Fong,Lydia T. Liu,Manoel Horta Ribeiro*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过随机田野实验和定性访谈，发现在高等教育中，AI辅助反馈草稿可以显著提高助教提供个性化反馈的参与率（+10.8个百分点）和反馈长度（+39.8字符），且不影响学生的有用性评分或降低单位时间效率。AI草稿作为可编辑的支架，降低了启动反馈的障碍，而非减少整体工作量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究主要关注AI如何提升必需任务的效率和准确性，但对于AI能否增加人们本意想做但经常跳过的可自由选择但有益的工作（discretionary but beneficial work）的参与度，尚不清楚。本研究在高等教育个性化反馈这一教学上有价值但通常可选的实践中探究该问题。

Method: 采用混合方法研究：在300-level机器学习课程中开展随机田野实验（n=11名助教，n=88名学生），学生作业随机分配至（1）处理组：助教在批改后收到AI辅助的反馈草稿；（2）对照组：无AI草稿。助教完全自主决定是否使用、编辑或忽略草稿。同时结合定性访谈。

Result: AI辅助反馈显著提高了反馈提供率（+10.8个百分点，SE=1.1，p<0.001）和反馈长度（+39.8字符，SE=3.45，p<0.001），且未对学生有用性评分或每字符花费时间产生负面影响。

Conclusion: AI辅助草稿作为可编辑的支架（editable scaffolds），降低了启动反馈的障碍，而非减少总体努力。AI在可自由选择但有益的任务中具有潜力：能增加本可能被遗漏的工作量，同时保留人类对最终结果的自主控制。

Abstract: AI systems increasingly shape human workflows by generating intermediate artifacts that users can adopt, revise, or ignore. While prior work has shown that AI assistance can improve the efficiency and accuracy of required tasks, less is known about whether it can increase participation in discretionary but beneficial work that users often intend to perform but frequently skip. We study this question in the context of personalized feedback provision in higher education, a pedagogically valuable but often optional practice. We conduct a mixed-methods study combining a randomized field experiment and qualitative interviews in a 300-level machine learning course with n=11 teaching assistants (TAs) and n=88 students. Student submissions were randomly assigned to either (1) a treatment condition where TAs received AI-assisted feedback drafts after grading or (2) a control condition without drafts. TAs remained fully in control and could use, edit, or ignore drafts at their discretion. We find that AI-assisted feedback significantly increases feedback provision (+10.8 percentage points, SE=1.1, p<0.001) and feedback length (+39.8 chars, SE=3.45, p<0.001) without negatively affecting student usefulness ratings or reducing time per character. Qualitative findings suggest that AI-assisted drafts function as editable scaffolds that lower barriers to initiating feedback rather than reducing overall effort. Our findings highlight AI's promise for discretionary but beneficial tasks: increasing work that might otherwise go undone while preserving human control over final outcomes.

</details>


### [156] [Pulse Focus: Validation of the Focus Performance Score as a Behavioral Signal for Human Attentional State Modeling Toward Attention-Aware AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03164)
*Yisak Debele,Israel Goytom,Anwar Misbah*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过行为学（N=466）、神经影像学（DMCC55B数据集，N=55）和公式验证三方面分析，证明Focus Performance Score (FPS) 是一种行为有效、可靠且具有神经基础的选择性注意控制测量指标。FPS来源于移动端Stroop任务，能够捕捉Stroop干扰效应、追踪个体差异，且其核心成分（不一致试次平均反应时）与冲突监测的关键神经基质——前扣带回皮层（ACC）的激活显著相关。公式验证还识别并解决了评分框架中的结构冗余问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人工智能系统需要可靠的认知状态测量指标来建模和支持人类认知，但目前缺乏经过行为、神经和公式多层面验证的注意力控制量化工具。

Method: 采用三种验证方法：(1) 行为验证（N=466, 111,133试次）：分析FPS对Stroop干扰效应的敏感性、个体差异追踪能力及重测信度；(2) 神经验证（DMCC55B fMRI数据集，N=55）：检验FPS核心成分（不一致试次平均反应时）与前扣带回皮层（ACC）激活的关系；(3) 公式验证：识别评分框架中的结构冗余并提供收敛性支持。

Result: 行为结果表明FPS能有效捕捉Stroop干扰效应、追踪注意力控制的个体差异，并具有强重测信度；神经结果发现FPS核心成分与ACC激活显著相关；公式验证识别并解决了评分框架的结构冗余问题，支持权重设计的合理性。

Conclusion: FPS是一种行为有效、可靠且具有神经基础的注意力控制测量工具，可作为评估人类注意力状态的可辩护行为信号，支持未来注意力感知的人机交互和生理状态建模研究。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence systems that model and support human cognition require reliable measures of cognitive state. We present the Focus Performance Score (FPS) from the Pulse Focus mobile Stroop application and evaluate whether it measures attentional control during color-word conflict resolution. We conduct behavioral, neural, and formula validation analyses. Behavioral results (N=466, 111,133 trials) show that FPS captures the Stroop interference effect, tracks individual differences in attentional control, and demonstrates strong test-retest reliability. Neural validation using the DMCC55B fMRI dataset (N=55) shows that the primary FPS component, mean incongruent reaction time, is significantly associated with anterior cingulate cortex activation, a key neural substrate of conflict monitoring. Formula validation identifies and resolves structural redundancy within the scoring framework and provides convergent support for the weighting design. Together, these findings establish FPS as a behaviorally valid, reliable, and neurally grounded measure of attentional control. FPS provides a defensible behavioral signal for evaluating human attentional state and supports future work on attention-aware human-AI interaction and physiological state modeling.

</details>


### [157] [Focused on the User, Overlooking the Risks: Security and Privacy Understandings, Practices and Challenges of Independent Chinese AI Agent Developers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03190)
*Shuning Zhang,Mingyao Xu,Zhixin Huang,Yutong Jiang,Rongjun Ma,Yuting Yang,Xin Yi,Kanye Ye Wang,Hewu Li*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 通过对28名中国独立AI智能体开发者的访谈研究，发现他们常从用户视角思考，聚焦于有害内容等用户端安全风险，但对安全漏洞认知不足，缺乏正式工具和流程，主要依赖临时性手动防护和非正式沟通，原因在于缺乏正规培训、可用的安全工具和平台指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体的普及使独立开发者能够创建复杂自主系统，但引入了超越传统企业架构的新型安全与隐私挑战，而这一群体的S&P认知与实践尚未被充分研究。

Method: 对28名中国开发者进行访谈研究，这些开发者因广泛使用全球大语言模型服务而具有代表性。

Result: 独立开发者倾向于从用户视角思考，关注有害内容等用户端安全风险，对安全漏洞认知薄弱；几乎完全依赖临时性手工防护和非正式沟通，缺乏正式工具或流程；主要障碍包括缺乏正规S&P培训、可用的安全工具及平台的可操作指导。

Conclusion: 该研究首次探索了独立AI智能体开发者对安全与隐私的认知，并为针对性的安全工具设计提供了机会。

Abstract: The proliferation of AI agents empowers independent developers, defined as individual or small groups who self-initiate projects rather than fulfill client-based contracts, to create sophisticated autonomous systems, but also introduces novel security and privacy (S&P) challenges beyond traditional corporate structures. We conducted an interview study (N=28) with Chinese developers, whose extensive use of global LLM services offer valuable insights into this population. We investigate their understandings, practices and challenges of S&P challenges in their developed AI agent products. We revealed that independent developers frequently think and act from their users' perspective. They focused on user-facing safety risks such as harmful content while exhibiting low awareness of security vulnerabilities. Consequently, developers rely almost exclusively on ad-hoc, manually crafted safeguards and informal communication, with an absence of formal tools or processes for S&P practices. We found these actions are driven by various inhibitors, primarily a lack of formal training on S&P related skills, accessible security tools and actionable guidance from platforms. Our work contributed the first exploration of independent AI agent developers' S&P understanding, outlining opportunities for tailored security tooling.

</details>


### [158] [Investigating Novice Researchers' Perceptions of Research Privacy Within LLM-Assisted Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03248)
*Shuning Zhang,Changxi Wen,Eve He,Ying Ma,Robert Xiao,Xin Yi,Hewu Li*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 对44名研究者的访谈揭示，初级研究者因发表压力和对机构支持缺失，在依赖LLM辅助学术工作时常面临隐私与知识产权风险。有趣的是，对创意泄露的恐惧反而加速了他们对LLM的使用（以求快速发表），而非阻止。研究者普遍存在两种错误认知：认为自己的创意不值得被攻击、认为输入数据会安全地淹没在海量数据集中。参与者尝试了输入分段、对抗性探测等五种缓解措施，但大多认为效果不佳。研究建议实施机构级沙盒隔离、场景化隐私教学和可验证数据删除审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM辅助学术工作流引入严重的隐私和知识产权风险，而初级研究者作为特别脆弱的群体，因发表压力和缺乏机构支持而高度依赖公开LLM，面临高风险的隐私-发表权衡，但这一问题尚未得到充分研究。

Method: 对44名来自不同学科的研究者进行半结构化访谈。

Result: 发现对创意泄漏的恐惧反而加速了对LLM的依赖；研究者存在错误认知（认为创意价值低不值得攻击、输入数据会被安全稀释）；识别出五种缓解措施（如输入分段、对抗性探测），但参与者普遍认为这些措施无效。

Conclusion: 需实施机构级沙盒隔离、场景化隐私教学和可验证数据删除审计以提升透明度。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLMs)-assisted scholarly workflows introduce critical privacy and intellectual property risks. As a uniquely vulnerable cohort driven by publication pressure and a lack of institutional support, novice researchers rely heavily on public LLMs, compelling them to navigate high-stakes privacy-publication trade-offs. To investigate these concerns, we conducted semi-structured interviews with 44 researchers across diverse disciplines. Our findings reveal that the fear of idea leakage paradoxically accelerates, rather than deters, reliance on LLMs, as researchers utilize them to expedite publication. They also held misconceptions that their ideas lacked the unique value to attract targeted attacks, and that their inputs would be safely diluted within massive datasets, preventing reconstruction. From interviews, we identified five types of mitigations including input fragmentation and adversarial probing, though we found that participants largely perceived these measures as ineffective. We outline implications including implementing institution-level sandboxed isolation, scenario-based privacy pedagogy, and verifiable data-deletion audits for transparency.

</details>


### [159] [ReforMe: Re-Shaping Documents with Contextual Prompting and Layout-Aware Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03266)
*Nabin Khanal,Tongyan Wang,Jui-Cheng Chiu,Ningning Nicole Kong,Hannah Yanhua Zong,Yingjie Victor Chen*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出一个交互式文档数字化系统，结合布局感知解析、OCR和LLM重建，并引入用户驱动修正和布局感知传播机制，通过用户研究证明其能提升修正效率、减少重复劳动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统OCR难以处理手写内容、不规则表格和异构布局，而基于LLM的方法缺乏精确、可扩展的修正机制，因此需要更高效可控的文档数字化方案。

Method: 通过形成性研究识别关键挑战与交互需求；系统整合布局感知解析、OCR和LLM重建；支持直接编辑和自然语言指令；引入布局感知传播机制，将用户修正泛化到结构相似区域，实现文档重构成结构化表示。

Result: 基于12名被试的真实文档用户研究表明，系统提升了修正效率，减少了重复性工作。

Conclusion: 该系统实现了更高效、可控的文档数字化流程。

Abstract: Digitizing complex documents with handwritten content, irregular tables, and heterogeneous layouts remains challenging, as traditional Optical Character Recognition (OCR) systems fail to capture writing nuances, author-specific conventions, and document structure, and recent LLM-based approaches lack mechanisms for precise, scalable correction. We present an interactive document digitization system that integrates layout-aware parsing, OCR, and LLM-based reconstruction with user-driven refinement. The system is informed by a formative study that identifies key challenges and interaction needs in real-world digitization workflows. It supports both direct edits and natural-language instructions, and introduces a layout-aware propagation mechanism that generalizes user corrections across structurally similar regions. This enables not only efficient error correction but also document re-shaping into structured, analyzable representations. We evaluate the system through a within-subjects user study (n=12) on real-world documents. Results show improved correction efficiency and reduced repetitive effort, demonstrating more effective and controllable document digitization procedure.

</details>


### [160] [Agentic Relationship Harm: Benchmarking and Gating Relational Manipulation in AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03271)
*Pei-Sze Tan,Tasuku Igarashi,Isao Echizen*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于大语言模型的AI代理在人际关系中可能造成的“代理关系伤害”风险，并为此构建了包含110个提示的基准测试、关系特定标注框架以及轻量级策略门控机制。实验表明，该关系感知门控在自动评判下优于通用安全提示，能有效识别并防范关系操纵行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全评估和通用防护措施通常将“有害性”视为孤立输出的属性，忽略了角色敏感的人际互动模式，无法应对AI代理在关系层面（如维持欺骗性身份、强化情感依赖、隔离目标等）的操纵行为。

Method: 提出“代理关系伤害”概念；构建110个提示的基准测试（攻防双方平衡）；设计关系特定的标注框架；开发轻量级后生成策略门控机制用于本地代理部署。

Result: 在自动评判下，关系特定门控优于通用安全提示；在主基准测试和多轮压力测试中均未检测到有害合规案例；同时保留了受害者侧的保护性干预能力。

Conclusion: 关系伤害是一个独特的社会技术风险面，角色敏感评估加上轻量级策略门控为超越通用拒绝提示提供了一条实用路径。

Abstract: AI agents built on large language models can assist not only legitimate tasks but also relational manipulation. AI agents can be used to help a user maintain a deceptive identity, intensify emotional dependency, isolate a target, or prepare for later extraction. We conceptualise this risk as agentic relationship harm: workflow-level assistance that can exploit recipient vulnerability, persuasive influence, and relational power asymmetry. Existing safety evaluations and generic guardrails often treat harmfulness as a property of isolated outputs, missing role-sensitive interaction patterns. To study this, we introduce a 110-prompt benchmark with balanced attacker- and victim-side cases, a relationship-specific labelling framework, and a lightweight post-generation policy gate for local agent deployments. In our evaluation, the relationship-specific gate outperforms generic safety prompting under automated judging, with no judge-identified harmful-compliance cases on the main benchmark or multi-turn stress test while preserving victim-side protective intervention. These results suggest that relationship harm is a distinct sociotechnical risk surface and that role-sensitive evaluation plus lightweight policy gating offers a practical path beyond generic refusal prompting.

</details>


### [161] [Intellectual Humility as a Cognitive Filter for AI-Generated Health Misinformation. An Evolutionary Perspective on Epistemic Vigilance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03377)
*Marcin Rządeczka,Maciej Wodziński,Kacper Zacharski,Marcin Moskalewicz*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究(N=99)表明，智力谦逊(IH)作为选择性认知过滤器，能有效降低对伪科学AI健康对话的可信度评价，但不影响对准确内容的判断；IH不帮助识别AI生成来源，表明认知警惕机制作用于内容质量而非来源归因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究智力谦逊这一元认知特质如何影响人们对不同科学严谨程度的AI生成健康对话的评价，并检验其是否涉及来源识别机制。

Method: 99名参与者随机分配评估三段关于运动与心理健康的对话（科学准确/中度伪科学/高度伪科学），测量智力谦逊得分与可信度评价、AI来源识别能力之间的关联。

Result: 高IH者显著降低对伪科学内容的可信度评价，与准确内容评价无相关；IH不能预测参与者识别AI来源的能力。

Conclusion: 智力谦逊是一种进化适应的认知防御机制，能有效检测AI生成内容中的操纵企图，但对识别AI来源无效；研究对基础模型如何影响人类认知防御具有启示意义。

Abstract: We present experimental findings from a study (N=99) examining how intellectual humility (IH), i.e., the metacognitive awareness of epistemic limitations, affects the evaluation of AI-generated health dialogues varying in scientific rigor. Participants were randomly assigned to evaluate one of three dialogues about exercise and mental health: scientifically accurate, moderately pseudoscientific, or strongly pseudoscientific. Results reveal that IH functions as a selective cognitive filter. Individuals with higher humility scores rated pseudoscientific content as significantly less credible, while showing no correlation with credibility assessments of accurate content. Crucially, humility did not predict the ability to identify AI as the source of dialogues, suggesting that epistemic vigilance operates on content quality rather than source attribution. We interpret these findings through an evolutionary lens, proposing that IH represents an ancestral adaptation for navigating informationally uncertain environments. It remains effective at detecting exploitation attempts in AI-generated content, despite humans lacking evolved mechanisms for detecting AI sources. The study contributes to understanding how foundation models might improve or undermine human epistemic defenses, especially in health communication contexts.

</details>


### [162] [Analyzing Visual Attention Patterns During Band Rehearsal with Mobile Eye Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03485)
*Arvind Srinivasan,Tobias Rau,Michael Sedlmair*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过移动眼动追踪技术，考察了一个四人乐队在自然排练中的视觉注意分配模式，发现以乐队领队为中心的“轮毂-辐条”式注意拓扑结构，并在多次练习中注视转移显著减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉注意对合奏协调至关重要，但音乐家在自然排练过程中如何分配目光这一问题尚不清楚，现有研究缺少对真实场景中多成员交互的量化分析。

Method: 采用Pupil Labs Neon移动眼动仪对四人乐队进行三首歌曲各两次排练的跟踪，利用YOLOv8辅助场景标注，将注视点映射到乐队成员及物体，并构建注视矩阵、转移矩阵、时序围巾图及驻留-转移相关性分析。

Result: 发现以领队为核心的轮毂-辐条注意拓扑；学习中的吉他手高达97%的人际注视集中于领队；不熟悉曲目在两次尝试间注视转移平均减少65%（个体最高达82%）；围巾图显示教学中断碎片化注意，连续演奏巩固注意。

Conclusion: 参与者的主观反馈与量化模式一致，研究为合奏教学中的注视感知工具设计提供了启示。

Abstract: Visual attention is central to ensemble coordination, yet how musicians allocate gaze during naturalistic rehearsal remains poorly understood. We present a pilot study using mobile eye tracking to examine gaze behaviour in a four-member band across three songs, each practiced twice. Musicians wore Pupil Labs Neon eye trackers, and YOLOv8-assisted scene annotations mapped fixations to ensemble members and objects in view. Analyzing fixation matrices, transition matrices, temporal scarf plots, and dwell-transition correlations, we uncover a hub-and-spoke attention topology: the session leader was the dominant gaze target for all members, while the learning guitarist concentrated up to 97% of interpersonal dwell on this single reference. Between attempts, gaze transitions decreased by up to 65% on average for unfamiliar material (up to 82% for individual participants) as scanning stabilized. Scarf plots reveal how teaching breakdowns fragment attention and uninterrupted runs consolidate it. Post-session participant reflections align with the quantitative patterns, and we discuss implications for gaze-aware tools in ensemble pedagogy.

</details>


### [163] [Warning About AI Fallibility Increases Help-Seeking in an Intelligent Tutoring System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03822)
*Tomohiro Nagashima,Mirella Hladký,Vera Rief*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 在AI数学辅导系统中，加入一个简单的透明性警告（告知学生AI可能犯错）会显著提升学生的求助行为（提示请求），但对即时任务表现没有显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI支持的学习环境中存在幻觉风险，而透明性和信任校准至关重要，因此研究者想探究轻量级的透明性干预是否会影响学习者的行为。

Method: 252名学生在课堂上使用两版智能辅导系统：一组有警告消息（提示系统可能出错），另一组无警告。通过日志数据分析求助行为、错误率和任务时间。

Result: 收到警告的学生显著更多地请求提示，但两组的实际系统行为相同；错误率和任务时间无显著差异。

Conclusion: 轻量级透明性干预可以改变学习者的交互策略（如增加求助行为），但未必立即改善或损害学习表现。

Abstract: Recent work in Technology-Enhanced Learning and Human-Computer Interaction highlights the importance of transparency and trust calibration in AI-supported learning environments as they pose a risk of hallucinations. In this study, we investigate whether a simple transparency intervention that warns students that a pedagogical agent may make mistakes affects learner behavior in a math intelligent tutoring system. We conducted a classroom experiment with 252 school students using two system versions: one including a warning message about potential system errors, and one that does not mention potential errors. Using log data, we analyzed students' problem-solving performance data, including help-seeking behavior, error rate, and time-on-task. Results show that students who were warned about potential AI errors requested significantly more hints than those in the other condition, even though the actual system behavior was exactly the same. This finding suggests that lightweight transparency interventions can influence learners' interaction strategies without necessarily improving or impairing immediate performance.

</details>


### [164] [CLI-Anything: Towards Agent-Native Computer Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03854)
*Yuhao Yang,Tianyu Fan,Chao Huang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出CLI-Anything范式，主张通过命令行界面（CLI）替代图形用户界面（GUI）作为AI Agent与计算机交互的主要方式，并构建了CLI-Hub平台来实现这一"Agent原生"的计算机使用愿景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于GUI的Agent方法存在像素级交互脆弱、时序依赖、坐标动作易因界面变更失效等问题，迫使Agent模仿人类感知局限而非发挥其在结构化数据处理和程序化控制方面的计算优势。

Method: 将现有应用程序转换为命令行工具，保留功能的同时暴露机器可读协议；通过结构化命令、显式状态表示和确定性反馈来创建与Agent自然操作方式对齐的交互界面，消除视觉到计算的损耗性转换。

Result: 构建了CLI-Hub平台，为Agent原生的计算机使用提供了方法论、架构和基础设施。

Conclusion: 应围绕Agent的优势（精确程序化控制和确定性执行）重新设计交互范式，而非构建更复杂的屏幕阅读器和点击模拟器。

Abstract: As large language models advance in reasoning and tool use capabilities, researchers increasingly seek to leverage them for computer use agents that can interact with existing software. The dominant approach develops GUI agents that control applications through visual interfaces: interpreting screenshots, locating UI elements, and executing mouse clicks to mimic human interaction. This GUI-centric paradigm fundamentally misaligns with agent capabilities. Current GUI agents struggle with brittle pixel-level interactions, timing dependencies, and coordinate-based actions that break with interface changes. They force agents to emulate human perceptual limitations rather than leverage their computational strengths in structured data processing and programmatic control. CLI-Anything argues for agent-native computer use design. Instead of forcing agents to navigate visual layouts, we create interfaces aligned with how agents naturally operate: through structured commands, explicit state representations, and deterministic feedback. We transform existing applications into command-line harnesses that preserve functionality while exposing machine-readable protocols optimized for AI-native interaction. This eliminates the lossy visual-to-computational translation that plagues GUI agents. Rather than building sophisticated screen readers and click simulators, we should redesign interaction paradigms around agent strengths: precise programmatic control and deterministic execution. We examine the methodology, architecture, evidence, and future directions for this agent-native transformation of computer use. We have built CLI-Hub as a comprehensive platform that operationalizes this agent-native computer use vision. The platform provides methodology, architecture, and infrastructure for this fundamental transformation of computer use.

</details>


### [165] [From 'What' to 'How' and 'Why': Sharing LLM-Generated Retrospective Summaries of Older Adults' Passive Tracking Data with Remote Family Members](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03876)
*Jiachen Li,Reina Szeyi Chan,Akshat Choube,Xiang Zhi Tan,Elizabeth Mynatt,Varun Mishra*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文探索利用大语言模型（LLMs）从多模态追踪数据中为老年人远程家属（RFMs）生成回顾性摘要。通过将现有系统Vital Insight定制为技术探针，对11位RFM进行访谈收集反馈，进而设计了一种多层、多智能体、洞察驱动的摘要方法。对比实验显示，重新设计的摘要相比初始版本在满意度、感知帮助性、信任度和接收意愿上均有显著提升。研究最终提出了AI生成摘要的设计启示，强调应支持RFM的认知转向：从呈现"收集了什么数据"到解释"我的亲人过得怎么样"及"为什么"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代普适计算技术中，多模态追踪系统有望为老年人远程家属（RFMs）提供及时关注与 reassurance，但将异构数据流整合为有意义的回顾性摘要仍然困难。尽管LLMs在解释多模态数据方面展现了潜力，但针对RFM这类对老年人有丰富个人知识、强烈情感责任但日常可见性有限的利益相关者，生成叙事性摘要的研究尚不充分。

Method: 首先利用并定制现有系统Vital Insight生成初始摘要（作为技术探针），对11位RFM进行访谈；基于反馈，将系统重新设计为多层、多智能体、洞察驱动的摘要方法——从客观统计与描述逐步过渡到丰富的上下文感知叙事；最后通过问卷调查对比评估重新设计的摘要与初始版本。

Result: 重新设计的摘要相比初始版本，在满意度、感知帮助性、信任度和接收意愿上均有显著提升。

Conclusion: 提出了AI生成摘要的设计启示，强调需要支持RFMs的认知转变：从仅仅呈现"收集了什么数据"到解释"我的亲人过得怎么样"以及"为什么"，为RFM及更广泛场景下的摘要设计提供指导。

Abstract: With the growing prevalence of modern ubiquitous computing technologies, multi-modal tracking systems hold promise for providing timely awareness and reassurance to stakeholders such as remote family members (RFMs) of older adults, who play a central role in care coordination. However, combining heterogeneous data streams into high-level, meaningful content - such as retrospective summaries - remains challenging. While recent work has demonstrated the promise of large language models (LLMs) for interpreting multi-modal tracking data, less attention has been given to generating narrative accounts for stakeholders like RFMs, who possess rich personal knowledge of older adults and strong emotional responsibility, yet have limited visibility into their daily lives and limited capacity for caregiving. In this work, we explore how LLMs can be used to generate retrospective summaries from multi-modal tracking data for RFMs of older adults. We leveraged and customized an existing system, Vital Insight, to generate initial summaries on different dates and data availability scenarios as technology probes, and conducted interviews with 11 RFMs to gather feedback. Based on these insights, we redesigned the system into a multi-layer, multi-agent, insight-driven summary approach that builds from objective statistics and descriptions to enriched, context-aware narratives. We then compared the redesigned summaries with the initial versions through a survey with the same 11 RFMs and found significant improvements in satisfaction, perceived helpfulness, trust, and willingness to receive the summaries. We conclude by presenting design implications for AI-generated summaries for RFMs and broader contexts, emphasizing the need to support RFMs' sensemaking shift from simply presenting ''What'' data were collected, to explaining ''How'' is my loved one doing and ''Why''.

</details>


### [166] [DiffUNet^2: Bidirectional Prediction, Probabilistic Generation and Collaborative Visual Discovery for Scientific Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03926)
*Mengdi Chu,Jiaxin Yang,Angus G. Forbes,Nathan Debardeleben,Earl Lawrence,Ayan Biswas,Han-Wei Shen*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出DiffUNet^2框架，结合条件扩散模型与交互式可视化，实现科学时序数据的双向任意时间生成、分支时间线探索、概率空间导航，支持科学家主动假设验证而非被动观察。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习方法多为确定性前向预测，无法捕捉多个合理演化结果，且缺乏后向推理能力，限制了其在科学工作流中的实用性。

Method: 提出DiffUNet^2条件扩散模型，实现跨时间的双向任意时间生成；构建交互式系统支持分支时间线探索、用户引导状态编辑和概率空间导航。

Result: 在5个跨学科科学数据集上验证了预测准确性和概率空间集成质量；通过与领域专家协作证明了该方法在科学时序数据分析工作流中的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过整合建模与可视化交互，将生成模型转化为假设驱动的科学分析工具，使科学家能够交互式探索系统动力学。

Abstract: Modeling temporal evolution is important to analyzing and reasoning about scientific phenomena, yet most machine learning methods provide deterministic forward predictions that overlook multiple plausible outcomes and rarely support backward reasoning, limiting their usefulness in practical scientific workflows. We present a framework that integrates diffusion-based generative modeling with interactive visual analytics for scientific exploration. We introduce DiffUNet^2, a conditional diffusion model that enables bidirectional, any-to-any generation across time and captures distributions of plausible system evolutions. Built upon the model, our interactive system supports branching timeline exploration, user-guided state editing, and probability-space navigation, enabling scientists to actively explore alternative hypotheses rather than passively observe predictions. We evaluate the model on 5 datasets across different scientific domains to validate its predictive accuracy and probability-space ensemble quality. In collaboration with domain experts, we demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach in supporting practical scientific temporal data analysis workflows. By integrating modeling and visual interaction, our approach enables scientists to interactively explore system dynamics, transforming generative models into tools for hypothesis-driven scientific analysis.

</details>


<div id='cs.PL'></div>

# cs.PL [[Back]](#toc)

### [167] [From Rocq to Metal: A Pipeline for Formally Verified Microcontroller Firmware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02651)
*Valentin Bergeron,Karolina Gorna*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文提出了Encore!——一个基于续延传递风格（CPS）的裸机虚拟机，能够在微控制器上运行Rocq提取的Scheme代码，实现形式化验证的固件。通过将固件结构化为纯状态转移函数，核心部分可在Rocq中完全证明，同时保持未验证的主机层恒定不变。LLM辅助的战术合成自然地融入此工作流，使AI生成的固件能够自我证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI生成代码的普及，安全关键系统中执行不变量的需求日益迫切，但支持高阶规范语言的运行时对于大多数嵌入式目标来说过于庞大，亟需一种轻量级的形式化验证方案。

Method: 构建Encore!裸机CPS虚拟机，在微控制器上运行Rocq提取的Scheme；将固件设计为纯状态转移函数，使核心完全可证；利用LLM辅助战术合成自动生成形式化证明。

Result: 实现了可在微控制器上运行的形式化验证固件，核心部分可在Rocq中完全证明，未验证的主机层复杂度与固件复杂度解耦。

Conclusion: 形式化验证固件在当前是可行的，且LLM辅助证明生成使得AI生成的固件能够自我证明，有望改变安全关键系统的开发范式。

Abstract: Enforcing invariants in safety-critical systems is increasingly urgent as AI-generated code becomes widespread. Unfortunately, the runtimes required to support high-level specification languages are too large for most embedded targets. In this article, we show how formally verified firmware is achievable today. We built Encore!, a bare-metal Continuation Passing Style (CPS) virtual machine (VM) that runs Rocq-extracted Scheme on microcontrollers. We also show how to structure firmware as a pure state-transition function, making its core fully provable in Rocq while keeping the unverified host layer constant regardless of firmware complexity. Large Language Model (LLM)-assisted tactic synthesis fits naturally into this workflow: formal theorem statements replace manual code review, allowing AI-generated firmware to prove itself.

</details>


### [168] [Fixed-Point Scaffolding in the Clef Programming Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02854)
*Houston Haynes*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于范畴论和不动点组合子的编译器框架，利用MLIR作为基础设施，在编译过程中保持类型系统的结构性质（维度、等级、转义、数值表示），并通过函子、参数性和伴随模态逻辑实现分层验证，最终将编译验证与类型安全统一在同一个不动点原语下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的编译器在将ML家族语言（具有强正确性属性）编译到低级表示时，往往丢失了高层类型结构信息。作者希望构建一个编译器，使其在降低（lowering）过程中保持类型系统的结构性质，并同时提供形式化的验证保证，而不要求开发者直接接触范畴论。

Method: 使用固定点组合子（fixed-point combinator）保留PSG中的维度、等级、转义和数值表示结构；利用MLIR的SSA、属性系统和dialect作为承载结构的具体宿主；采用从编译偏序集到目标范畴的函子构建中间端，满足组合性方程；基于Ohori的机器码证明理论、参数性和伴随模态逻辑三层代数基础；引入紧闭负类型和分数类型，并通过MLIR工具链实现。

Result: 编译器在降低过程中保持了类型结构，同一不动点原语既用于类型保留也用于证明项生成，证明项可在MLIR中持续验证完整性。框架形式化地结合了维度类型、Tarau的groupoid和细胞层（cellular sheaves），所有范畴论抽象均作为内部脚手架实现，开发者无需直接接触。

Conclusion: 该编译器框架提供了一个独特的、由范畴论和类型理论支撑的编译验证基础，实现了结构保持和完整性验证的统一，同时通过内部脚手架设计保持了开发者的可用性。

Abstract: For fans of Gabriel's "Worse is Better" it may be ironic that C++, by way of MLIR, serves as the scaffold for compiling an ML-family language whose correctness properties are structural.
