A crucial intersection in our Composer compiler initiates its lowering with a fixed-point combinator that preserves the dimensional, grade, escape, and numeric-representation structure from the Program Semantic Graph. And the MLIR that's witnessed from the PSG is no passive host. Its use of static single assignment, attribute system and dialects carry that structure materially. We show that our compiler middle end uses categorical construction for lowering code with companion verification to that strata: a functor from the compilation poset to a target category, subject to the compositionality equation. The grounding of our approach comes from three sources, each on its own algebraic object: Ohori's machine-code proof theory grounds the compilation axis, parametricity grounds the content at the base, and adjoint mode logic grounds the traversal between our verification tiers. To extend the thesis we introduce compact-closed negative and fractional types, and show the type machinery can be carried with preserved structure and realized through tooling MLIR provides. More broadly, the same fixed-point primitive that preserves types through compilation also supplies proof terms that can continue to be exercised in MLIR to verify its integrity as lowering proceeds through the pipeline. We argue that this foundation is a unique additional point anticipated by our framework that includes dimensional types, Tarau's groupoid, and cellular sheaves. Throughout, the formalism is instrumented as an internal scaffold: the abstractions support the compiler's mechanics, where a developer is never required to reach for category theory in order to rely on the guarantees the compiler provides.

</details>


<div id='cs.ET'></div>

# cs.ET [[Back]](#toc)

### [169] [Powering An Ecosystem Of Pedagogical AI Agents: A Validation Strategy For A Unified Data Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02950)
*Natalia Theodora,Ploy Thajchayapong,Ashok K. Goel*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 本文针对AI增强学习生态系统中多样化教学代理（包括对话助手、虚拟教练和自适应导师）所带来的数据架构挑战，提出了一种两阶段验证策略：先在QA环境中使用合成+真实数据验证功能正确性，再在生产环境中处理了超过270万条请求。研究为构建支持异构AI教学工具的统一数据骨干提供了可复现的测试框架，并揭示了多AI代理数据源场景下的隐私关键挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI教育应用从单一智能 tutoring 系统演变为包含对话助手、虚拟教练、自适应导师等多样化教学代理的生态系统，异构AI代理与人类教学者、学习者之间的复杂信息反馈环路需要统一且可扩展的数据架构。然而，这类数据架构的设计、开发和部署面临关键的验证问题，现有研究缺乏针对性的验证策略。

Method: 采用两阶段测试方法：第一阶段在QA环境中使用合成数据与真实数据的混合来验证各类事件类型的功能正确性；第二阶段在生产环境中处理来自大规模在线项目的真实事件数据，共完成21次成功运行，处理超过270万条生产请求。

Result: 生产环境成功处理了超过270万条请求（21次运行），验证了数据管道的实际可扩展性；同时揭示了多AI代理数据源场景下的数据隐私这一关键挑战。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一种可复制的统一数据骨干验证策略，为机构与开发者构建和支持异构AI教学工具套件提供了清晰的框架，并强调在多代理数据源环境下数据隐私是需要重点关注的挑战。

Abstract: The application of AI in education has evolved from monolithic intelligent tutoring systems to a diverse ecosystem of pedagogical agents, including conversational assistants, virtual coaches, and adaptive tutors. This shift requires a unified and scalable data architecture to manage the complex information feedback loops between human instructors, learners, and the varied AI agents. The design, development, and deployment of the data architecture in turn raises a critical issue of validation. This paper addresses this critical need by describing a practical validation strategy for a high-volume data pipeline developed as part of a data architecture for AI-augmented adult learning at the National AI Institute for Adult Learning and Online Education. Our approach involves a two-stage testing methodology to ensure both functional diversity and real-world scalability. First, the QA environment uses a blend of synthetic and real-world data to validate functional correctness across various event types produced from learner and agent interactions. Following this, the production environment successfully processed a total of over 2.7 million production requests across 21 successful runs carrying authentic event data from a large-scale online program. This validation process surfaced crucial insights into data privacy, a key challenge when handling varied data from multiple AI agent data sources. By outlining a replicable testing strategy for a unified data backbone, this research offers a clear framework for institutions and developers aiming to build and support their own heterogeneous suites of AI-powered learning tools. Keywords: Pedagogical Agents, Learning Ecosystems, Data Architecture, Validation, Scalability, Learning Analytics.

</details>


### [170] [Glass Box at Orbit: A Constitutional AI Verification Framework for Trustworthy Autonomous CubeSat Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02967)
*Karthik Barma,Anil Sanneboyina,V C Premchand Yadav*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 本文提出Glass Box，一种运行时宪法性AI验证层，可在自主轨道数据中心中拦截AI策略的每个候选动作，并依据六项物理约束和七项线性时序逻辑安全不变量进行评估，确保每个批准动作附带可解释性评分和完整的宪法审计日志。在Project October仿真中验证了其有效性，证明验证开销为O(N_c)，与模型大小无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轨道数据中心正快速发展（微软、AWS等），但缺乏治理机制——当自主AI系统在太空中做出错误决策时，无法在决策变得不可逆之前进行干预。

Method: 提出Glass Box运行时宪法性AI验证层：在每个动作到达航天器子系统前，拦截AI策略的候选动作，依据六项物理约束和七项线性时序逻辑(LTL)安全不变量进行评估，输出加权可解释性评分E(a_t)∈[0,1]及完整宪法审计日志。在Project October（五层自主轨道智能架构，面向CubeSat）仿真中验证。

Result: 验证开销为O(N_c)（与宪法规则数量相关），与模型大小或航天器状态维度无关；提供了完整的宪法约束语法形式化规范、七项经Z3和NuSMV模型检查验证的LTL安全不变量，以及Glass Box在日食入口时拦截不安全推理请求的工作示例。

Conclusion: 随着轨道计算向数据中心基础设施规模发展，运行时宪法性验证不再是研究新颖性，而是每个自主轨道平台最终必需的关键安全基础设施。

Abstract: The space industry is quietly building toward something nobody has fully reckoned with: orbital data centers running thousands of autonomous AI workloads with no human in the loop, 550 km above the Earth. Microsoft, AWS, and a growing list of orbital computing ventures are moving cloud-scale processing off the ground and into orbit. What none of them have answered yet is the governance question -- when autonomous AI systems at orbital data center scale make wrong decisions in space, what stops those decisions before they become irreversible?
  We introduce Glass Box: a runtime constitutional AI verification layer that intercepts every candidate action from an onboard AI policy and evaluates it against six physics-grounded constitutional constraints and seven Linear Temporal Logic (LTL) safety invariants before a single command reaches any spacecraft subsystem. Every approved action carries a weighted explainability score E(a_t) in [0,1] and a complete constitutional audit log. We demonstrate Glass Box within Project October: a fully simulated five-layer autonomous orbital intelligence architecture for CubeSat-class spacecraft.
  We prove that Glass Box verification overhead is O(N_c) in the number of constitutional rules, independent of model size or spacecraft state dimension. We present a complete formal specification of the constitutional constraint grammar, seven LTL safety invariants verified by Z3 and NuSMV model checking, and a detailed worked example of Glass Box intercepting an unsafe inference request at eclipse-entry under degraded battery state. As orbital computing scales toward data center infrastructure, runtime constitutional verification is no longer a research novelty -- it is mission-critical safety infrastructure that every autonomous orbital platform will eventually require.

</details>


### [171] [SA-DTS: Semantic-Aware Digital Twin Synchronization over 6G Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03617)
*Vincenzo Sammartino*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 提出了一种语义感知的数字孪生同步框架(SA-DTS)，通过轻量级神经语义编码器提取任务相关特征并传输紧凑语义描述符，结合分层知识图谱分区策略，在6G环境下实现了高达94%的带宽节省、87%的端到端同步延迟降低和超过97%的状态重建精度，将同步开销从O(N²)降至O(N log N)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字孪生技术在6G愿景中至关重要，但大规模部署时高保真同步需要巨大的持续上行带宽，威胁了系统的可行性和能效。现有方法传输原始传感器或视频数据，带宽消耗过大。

Method: 提出SA-DTS框架：(1)在物理端使用轻量级神经语义编码器提取任务相关特征，传输紧凑语义描述符；(2)在DT副本端，配对解码器结合动态知识图谱重建上下文状态；(3)采用分层知识图谱分区策略，自适应分区数G = ⌈N / log₂ N⌉，将聚合更新开销从O(N²)降至O(N log N)。

Result: 在工业机器人控制、患者监测和车辆队列三个典型DT工作负载上，实现带宽节省高达94%，端到端同步延迟降低87%，KG辅助状态重建精度超过97%。提出的语义保真度分数与标准任务指标（碰撞准确率、警报F1、间距偏差）的Pearson相关系数r > 0.97（95% CI: [0.961, 0.982]）。

Conclusion: 语义通信不仅仅是一种压缩工具，而是实现真正实时、可扩展的数字孪生生态系统的基础使能技术。

Abstract: Digital Twins (DTs) are emerging as a cornerstone of the 6G vision, enabling real-time cyber-physical mirroring for smart manufacturing, autonomous vehicles, and remote healthcare. However, maintaining high-fidelity synchronization at scale demands an enormous and sustained uplink bandwidth, threatening both the feasibility and the energy efficiency of large deployments. We propose a Semantic-Aware DT Synchronization (SA-DTS) framework that radically redefines the synchronization pipeline: instead of streaming raw sensor or video data, a lightweight neural semantic encoder at the physical-world source extracts only task-relevant features and transmits compact semantic descriptors over the 6G air interface. At the DT replica, a paired decoder coupled with a dynamic Knowledge Graph (KG) reconstructs the full contextual state. A hierarchical KG partitioning strategy with an adaptive partition count $G = \lceil N / \log_2 N \rceil$ ensures that aggregate update overhead scales as $O(N \log N)$ rather than $O(N^2)$, making the framework viable for deployments with hundreds of simultaneously twinned entities. Extensive simulations on three canonical DT workloads -- industrial robot control, patient-monitoring, and vehicular platooning -- demonstrate bandwidth savings of up to 94%, end-to-end synchronization latency reductions of 87%, and KG-assisted state-reconstruction accuracy exceeding 97%, all under realistic 6G channel conditions. Empirical correlation confirms that the proposed Semantic Fidelity Score tracks standard task metrics (collision accuracy, alarm F1, spacing deviation) with Pearson $r > 0.97$ (95% CI: [0.961, 0.982]). Our results reveal that semantic communication is not merely a compression tool but a fundamental enabler for truly real-time, scalable DT ecosystems.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [172] [Acceptance-Test-Driven Evaluation Protocols for Business-Centric LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02755)
*Eric Liang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向运营级LLM系统的验收测试驱动评估协议，将利益相关者目标转化为可执行的行为契约和发布门控，采用"红-训练-绿"生命周期替代传统开发流程，确保系统在安全、可靠、可审计和经济性方面满足确定性要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM应用需要满足确定性制度要求，但依赖于概率性生成组件，传统的后验基准测试不足以保障系统的安全性、可靠性、可审计性和经济实用性。

Method: 提出验收测试驱动的评估协议扩展，包括：1) 将利益相关者目标转化为可执行的行为契约、发布门控、监控信号和证据工件；2) 采用"红-训练-绿"生命周期（先定义失败的验收测试，再通过提示优化、检索设计、微调、护栏或数据增强改进系统，最后在满足多维门控条件时发布）；3) 提供面向治理的度量栈、参考架构和实证协议。

Result: 提出了一种面向治理的度量栈、参考架构和实证协议，用于将验收测试驱动的LLM开发与提示优先和基准测试后的工作流程进行对比评估。

Conclusion: 该协议使LLM系统在变更（提示、模型、检索或智能体修改）被接受前，必须通过预定义的行为契约和发布门控，从而弥补概率性生成组件与确定性制度要求之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) applications are increasingly expected to satisfy deterministic institutional requirements while relying on probabilistic generative components. This mismatch makes ordinary post-hoc benchmarking insufficient for systems that must be safe, reliable, auditable, and economically useful. This paper contributes an evaluation-protocol extension for operational LLM systems grounded in acceptance-test-driven development, safety engineering, and business-centric validation. The extension translates stakeholder goals into executable behavioral contracts, release gates, monitoring signals, and evidence artifacts before prompt, model, retrieval, or agent changes are accepted. It adapts the red-green-refactor discipline of test-driven development to a red-train-green lifecycle: first define failing acceptance tests for desired behavior, then improve the LLM system through prompt changes, retrieval design, fine-tuning, guardrails, or data augmentation, and finally release only when multidimensional gates are satisfied. The contribution is a governance-oriented metric stack, reference architecture, and empirical protocol for comparing acceptance-test-driven LLM development against prompt-first and benchmark-after workflows.

</details>


### [173] [Report on the Designing Accountable Software Systems Workshop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02804)
*Catherine Albiston,Travis Breaux,Kat Dearstyne,Jane Cleland-Huang,Serge Egelman,Joan Feigenbaum,Lu Feng,Max Lindquist,Stephen Miner,Ruzica Piskac,Sarah Santos,Jordan Schmerge,Anmol Singhal,Maria Smith,Daniel Weitzner,Christopher Yoo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 2024年11月举办的软件系统问责制设计研讨会（DASS）汇集了政府、学术界和行业利益相关者，围绕软件系统中的问责制进行了跨学科知识系统化，明确了问责制的维度、概念模型、法律要求来源、法律要求在软件中的可操作化、证据保全以及问责制成功与失败的情境因素，并提出了未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在美国国家科学基金会的支持下，召集政府、学术界和工业界的当前及未来利益相关者，就软件系统中的问责制这一跨学科主题进行知识系统化，以解决现有问责制在软件设计中面临的挑战。

Method: 通过为期两天的研讨会形式，组织了一系列专题讨论、特邀演讲和分组会议，以协作式知识系统化的方式展开，覆盖问责制的六个关键维度。

Result: 明确了定义澄清和责任划分的重要性及其对研究可推广性的影响；识别出需要进一步研究如何改进问责结构向软件设计过程的转化，并加强与立法者、监管者、企业高管和系统开发者等利益相关者的互动；发现DASS类研究项目对跨学科团队提出了高要求，包括团队组建与维持、跨学科学习方法、研究传播和职业发展。

Conclusion: 软件系统中的问责制需要跨学科协作，明确责任定义和法律要求的可操作化，同时需关注团队建设和跨学科学习的挑战，以推动问责制在软件系统中的有效落地。

Abstract: The Workshop on Designing Accountable Software Systems (DASS) was convened in November 2024 with support from the U.S. National Science Foundation to engage a wide range of current and future stakeholders from government, academia, and industry on the cross-disciplinary topic of accountability in software systems. Over two days, attendees engaged in a series of panels, invited talks, and breakout sessions covering: (1) the dimensions of accountability, including legal compliance as well as business and societal aspects and drivers; (2) a conceptual model of the various structures needed to realize accountability; (3) the sources of legal requirements that affect software; (4) the operationalization of legal requirements in software; (5) the requirements to preserve evidence needed to conduct investigations; and (6) a range of challenges and contextual factors beyond software that affect why some accountability structures succeed, while others fail. The workshop was conducted as a collaborative systematization of knowledge that culminated in several research directions. The findings include the importance of clarifying definitions and responsibilities within accountable organizations, which can affect whether those researching accountability are making assumptions that limit the generalizability of findings. Further research was also identified as needed to study the ways to improve the translation of accountability structures into the software design process while improving engagement with stakeholders, such as legislators, regulators, business executives and system developers. Finally, a key finding was the high demands that DASS-like research projects place on interdisciplinary teams: both in terms of team formation and sustainment, as well as, the specific demands of cross-disciplinary learning that covers both research methods, research dissemination, and career development.

</details>


### [174] [Many a Little Makes a Mickle: A Code-Centric Empirical Study of Data Minimization Principle in Android App Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02960)
*Dianshu Liao,Shidong Pan,Zhenchang Xing,Xiaoyu Sun*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文聚焦数据最小化原则在Android应用中的代码级实现，通过分析1,114个开源应用识别出10个常见场景，对9,875个真实APK进行大规模分析后提炼出31条可操作编码指南，并发现LLM生成的代码会继承真实代码中的隐私风险模式，而引入指南可消除这些问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作主要通过政策分析检测合规差距，缺乏为开发者提供在代码层面实现隐私原则的可操作指导。

Method: 对1,114个开源Android应用进行形成性研究，识别10个数据最小化场景；对9,875个真实APK进行大规模分析，提炼31条编码指南；评估LLM在Android开发中的代码生成是否符合数据最小化原则。

Result: LLM生成的代码会复制并放大真实代码中的数据最小化风险模式；引入31条指南后，所有评估模型中的问题均被消除。

Conclusion: 应转向从代码级根源响应隐私监管要求，从而在人工和AI辅助编程中实现更好的合规性。

Abstract: Modern mobile applications consume large amounts of data to function, raising significant privacy concerns and regulatory challenges. While prior work has primarily focused on detecting compliance gaps through policy analysis, there remains a lack of actionable guidance for developers to implement privacy principles at the code level. In this paper, we focus on data minimization as a developer-operationalizable principle and investigate its realization in Android applications. We conduct a formative study on 1,114 open-source Android apps to identify ten recurring data minimization scenarios across five data-handling stages. Building on this, we perform a large-scale analysis of 9,875 real-world APKs and distill 31 actionable coding guidelines to support privacy-compliant development. We further examine LLM-based code generation in Android development and find that state-of-the-art models consistently reproduce data minimization-risky practices, indicating that they inherit and amplify patterns from real-world code. Encouragingly, incorporating our guidelines eliminates these issues across all evaluated models. Our work advocates a shift toward responding to privacy regulatory requirements at their code-level root causes, enabling better compliance in both human and AI-assisted programming.

</details>


### [175] [SPOQ: Specialist Orchestrated Queuing for Multi-Agent Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03115)
*Royce Carbowitz,Dheeraj Kumar*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SPOQ是一种结合波浪式拓扑调度、双重验证门控和人类作为智能体(HaaA)集成的新方法论，通过三层智能体层级（Opus、Sonnet、Haiku）优化软件工程自动化任务中的协调开销、质量控制和人机协作问题。实验表明该方法在并行加速、规划质量、缺陷减少等方面均取得显著提升，并在开源模型上验证了可迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于多智能体的软件工程自动化方法存在三大问题：协调开销大、质量控制不足、人类监督有限，亟需一种系统性的方法论来同时解决这些瓶颈。

Method: SPOQ包含三项核心创新：(1) 基于波浪的拓扑调度，从任务依赖图中计算可并行执行的波浪；(2) 双重验证门控，在执行前进行规划验证、执行后进行代码验证；(3) 人类作为智能体(HaaA)集成，让人参与任务分解和按需咨询。采用三层智能体层级架构（Opus工蜂、Sonnet审查员、Haiku调查员）优化成本-质量权衡。

Result: 波浪调度接近关键路径下界（比率1.03-1.11，加速比达14.3x）；规划覆盖从93.0提升至99.75，消除循环规划，并行度从31.0提升至75.25；双重验证将缺陷率从0.34降至0.20，测试通过率从91.25%提升至99.75%；人类审查将残余缺陷从0.47降至0.03。在17个仓库、8589次提交、1822个任务和13866个测试上达到99.87%通过率。

Conclusion: SPOQ通过结构化编排和分层质量门控有效提升了多智能体软件工程自动化的效率和质量，且其增益归因于编排机制本身而非特定模型，具有良好的可迁移性和生态有效性。

Abstract: Multi-agent AI systems show promise for automating software engineering tasks, yet existing approaches suffer from coordination overhead, quality control gaps, and limited human oversight. We introduce SPOQ (Specialist Orchestrated Queuing), a methodology combining three innovations: (1) wave-based topological dispatch that computes parallel execution waves from task dependency graphs; (2) dual validation gates applying quality metrics before execution (planning validation) and after (code validation) to reduce rework cycles; and (3) Human-as-an-Agent (HaaA) integration, where a human specialist participates in decomposition and can be consulted during execution. SPOQ uses a three-tier agent hierarchy (Opus workers, Sonnet reviewers, Haiku investigators) to optimize cost-quality tradeoffs. We evaluate SPOQ through four experiments. Experiment 1: wave dispatch approaches the critical-path lower bound (ratio 1.03--1.11, speedup up to 14.3x); on a 2-slot local backend it delivers a stable 1.4x speedup. Experiment 2: SPOQ improves planning coverage from 93.0 to 99.75, eliminates cyclic plans, and lifts parallelism from 31.0 to 75.25. Experiment 3: dual validation reduces defects from 0.34 to 0.20 per task and lifts test pass rate from 91.25% to 99.75%. Experiment 4: human review reduces residual defects from 0.47 to 0.03 per task. Results are replicated on a locally hosted open-weights model (Qwen3.6-35B-A3B), verifying gains are attributable to orchestration rather than any specific model. A longitudinal study across 17 repositories, 8,589 commits, 1,822 tasks, and 13,866 tests (99.87% pass rate) provides ecological validation.

</details>


### [176] [AlgoTouch: An Execution-Centered Approach to Incremental Construction of Imperative Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03349)
*Michel Adam,Patrice Frison,Sabine Letellier Zarshenas,Moncef Daoud*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: AlgoTouch 是一个基于执行的交互式程序构建系统，允许开发者通过直接操作程序数据来增量构建命令式程序。该系统通过记录具体的数据转换行为，自动合成控制结构（条件语句和循环），并生成可读的 Python、C、C++、Java 代码。其核心贡献在于从执行行为中确定性合成控制结构，支持不完整程序的执行与完善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统命令式编程要求开发者预先设想指令对数据状态的影响，增加了认知负荷和早期开发阶段的出错概率。为降低这种负担，需要一种更直观、基于观察而非预先推理的程序构建方式。

Method: AlgoTouch 构建了一个显式的概念机，暴露数据存储、计算和控制流。用户通过直接操作数据执行具体的数据转换，系统记录这些操作并合成控制结构：条件语句从观察到的比较行为推导，循环行为封装为循环宏，支持非线性、增量的程序构建。系统基于内部中间表示自动生成多种命令式语言代码。

Result: 在代表性算法基准测试上的工程级验证表明，AlgoTouch 能够正确、可读地生成 Python、C、C++、Java 代码，具有正确性、表达力、鲁棒性和语言无关性。

Conclusion: AlgoTouch 通过融合执行、构建和代码生成于统一架构，提出了一种替代性的交互式程序构建模型，开创了一类以执行为中心的开发系统新范式。

Abstract: Program construction in imperative languages remains largely based on writing textual code that specifies sequences of instructions operating on program data. This approach requires developers to anticipate the effects of instructions on evolving data states, which increases cognitive load and the likelihood of errors during early and incremental development. This paper presents AlgoTouch, an execution-based system for incremental construction of imperative programs through direct manipulation of program data. Rather than assembling syntactic structures, programs are constructed by executing concrete data transformations that are recorded and incorporated into an internal intermediate representation. AlgoTouch relies on an explicit notional machine that exposes data storage, computation, and control flow, enabling continuous alignment between observed execution and program structure. A central contribution of the system lies in its deterministic synthesis of control structures from execution behavior. Conditional statements are derived from observed comparisons, while iterative behaviors are encapsulated in loop macros that support non-linear and incremental construction. This design enables partial and incomplete programs to be executed, refined, and completed while preserving semantic consistency. AlgoTouch automatically generates correct and readable programs in several mainstream imperative languages, including Python, C, C++, and Java. The system is evaluated through engineering-level validation on a representative set of algorithmic benchmarks, demonstrating correctness, expressiveness, robustness, and language independence. By integrating execution, construction, and code generation within a unified architecture, this work introduces an alternative model for interactive program construction and contributes a new class of execution-centered development systems.

</details>


### [177] [Neural Change Prediction: Relating Software Changes to Their Effects and Vice Versa](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03378)
*Laura Plein,Souhila Zidane,Jordan Samhi,Andreas Zeller*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出神经变化预测（Neural Change Prediction）技术，通过自动对代码施加大量突变并观察输出变化，学习软件变更与程序行为变化之间的关联，支持行为驱动的代码定位与变更效果预测，无需代码先验知识，在CSS和Python上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件开发的许多工作涉及理解代码变更与其效果之间的关系，若能学习和预测这种关系，可惠及多个软件工程领域。现有AI方法在无需执行代码的前提下预测代码语义仍是一大挑战。

Method: 对给定程序和测试输入自动施加大量代码突变，观察输出变化，收集（代码变更，行为变化）配对数据，构建模型实现两种预测：(1) 给定期望的行为变化，预测代码变更位置和方式（特征定位、软件演化、软件修复）；(2) 给定代码变更，预测其对输出的影响（效果预测）。

Result: 在CSS配置文件上进行了详细案例研究，并在Python程序上进行了评估，证明了该技术的通用性和广泛适用性。

Conclusion: 神经变化预测虽需大量突变和程序执行，但完全自动化且无需代码或语义先验知识，适用于任何可执行且输出可观察的软件工件。

Abstract: Much of software development revolves around understanding the relationship between software changes and their effects. If we could learn and predict those relationships, such predictions could benefit several areas of software engineering. While recent advances in artificial intelligence have shown great promise in software engineering tasks, predicting the semantics of code without executing it remains a big challenge. In this paper, we present Neural Change Prediction, a novel and fundamental technique to learn and predict associations between software changes and their dynamic effects on program behavior. Specifically, for a given program and test inputs, we automatically apply numerous mutations to the code and observe how these changes alter the program's output. From these (changes to software, changes in behavior)-pairs, we create models that: (1) for a desired change in behavior, predict where and how the code should be changed (feature localization, software evolution, and software repair); and (2) for a given code change, predict how this code change affects the output (effect prediction). We have conducted a detailed case study on CSS configuration files and an evaluation on Python programs to demonstrate the generality and wide applicability of Neural Change Prediction. While Neural Change Prediction requires numerous mutations (and thus numerous executions of the program under test), Neural Change Prediction is fully automatic and does not require any prior knowledge of the code or its semantics, making it applicable to any software artifact that can be executed and whose output can be observed.

</details>


### [178] [Human-AI Collaboration and the Transformation of Software Engineering Work](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03394)
*Mamdouh Alenezi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文基于多源证据综合分析了生成式AI和智能体AI如何将软件工程从以人类编码为中心重塑为以指导、验证和治理自主/半自主系统为中心的活动，提出了一个包含五个维度的未来工程师能力框架，并推导出九项可检验命题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着GenAI和Agentic AI融入软件开发，软件工程的核心正在从人类代码创作转向对自主系统的指导与治理，现有研究缺乏对这一范式转变的系统性理论框架和可操作的能力模型。

Method: 采用结构化解释性综合方法，基于近期同行评审和档案研究的多源证据库（包括对自主编码代理贡献数十万次拉取请求的大规模实证观察），刻画三种共存范式（传统、GenAI增强、Agentic AI增强），构建能力矩阵和转型框架。

Result: 识别了哪些传统活动被自动化、哪些被增强、哪些是新涌现的；提出了包含技术、认知、社会技术、治理、组织五个维度的能力框架及能力矩阵；推导出九项可检验命题。

Conclusion: 当代码变得丰富时，软件工程师的持久价值在于意图说明、批判性判断和负责任的监督，而非所产出代码的数量；研究对理论、行业劳动力转型、大学课程和组织领导力均有启示。

Abstract: The integration of Generative AI (GenAI) and Agentic AI into software development is reconfiguring software engineering from an activity centered on human authorship of code into a discipline centered on directing, verifying, and governing autonomous and semi-autonomous systems. Drawing on a curated, multi-source evidence base of recent peer-reviewed and archival studies -- including large-scale empirical observations of autonomous coding agents contributing hundreds of thousands of pull requests to open-source repositories -- this paper synthesizes how the locus of engineering work is shifting from individual coding productivity toward human--AI collaboration, agent orchestration, verification and validation, governance, and socio-technical systems thinking. We adopt a structured interpretive synthesis to characterize three coexisting paradigms: Traditional, Generative AI-Enabled, and Agentic AI-Enabled software engineering. We map which traditional activities are being automated, which are being augmented, and which are newly emerging, and we trace plausible role trajectories over the next decade. The paper's principal contribution is an original, theory-driven competency framework that organizes the capabilities required of future engineers into five interacting categories -- % technical, cognitive, socio-technical, governance, and organizational -- % operationalized through a competency matrix and a transformation framework linking paradigm shifts to capability demands. We derive nine empirically testable propositions and articulate implications for theory, industry workforce transformation, university curricula, and organizational leadership. We argue that, as code becomes abundant, the durable value of the software engineer increasingly resides in intent specification, critical judgment, and accountable oversight rather than in the sheer volume of code produced.

</details>


### [179] [An AutomationML Domain Library for the Formalized Process Description](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03691)
*Hamied Nabizada,Rainer Drath,Felix Gehlhoff,Alexander Fay*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于AutomationML (AML)的VDI/VDE 3682形式化过程描述（FPD）域库，实现了FPD语言元素的标准化机器可读数据交换，包括角色类、接口类、属性类型和系统单元类五个相互关联的库，并开发了双向映射工具验证其适用性，拟作为VDI/VDE 3682第3部分的候选标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VDI/VDE 3682标准的形式化过程描述（FPD）虽然提供了跨工程领域过程描述的统一图形化符号，但缺乏标准化、工具无关的机器可读模型交换数据格式，阻碍了不同工程工具间的互操作性。

Method: 基于CAEX 3.0元模型，设计并实现了包含五个子库的AML域库：RoleClassLib（语义角色）、InterfaceClassLib（连接类型）、两个AttributeTypeLibs（信息模型与图表交换）和SystemUnitClassLib（实例化模板）；讨论了继承、图表结构、层次分解和图形信息表示的关键设计决策及替代方案。

Result: 开发了基于Web的FPD建模器与AML之间的双向映射转换工具，验证了所提域库的适用性；该库已在https://aml.fpbjs.net公开提供，包含示例和反馈功能。

Conclusion: 该AML域库被提议作为VDI/VDE 3682第3部分的候选标准，为社区在标准化前提供输入和反馈机会。

Abstract: The Formalized Process Description (FPD) according to VDI/VDE 3682 provides a standardized graphical notation for describing processes across engineering domains but lacks a standardized, tool-independent data format for machine-readable model exchange. This paper presents an AutomationML (AML) domain library that formalizes the complete set of FPD language elements, their attributes, connection semantics, and graphical representation information as class libraries based on the Computer Aided Engineering Exchange (CAEX) 3.0 metamodel. The library comprises five interrelated parts: a RoleClassLib defining the semantic roles, an InterfaceClassLib for connection types, two AttributeTypeLibs for the information model and diagram interchange, and a SystemUnitClassLib providing instantiation templates. Key design decisions regarding inheritance, diagram structure, hierarchical decomposition, and the representation of graphical information are discussed along with the alternatives that were considered. A bidirectional mapping tool demonstrates the library's applicability by converting between a web-based FPD modeler and AML. The library is proposed as a candidate for Part 3 of VDI/VDE 3682. It is available together with an example and a feedback function for community input ahead of standardization at https://aml.fpbjs.net.

</details>


### [180] [Multi-Modal Assessment of Road Roughness Using Smartphone Applications, Acceleration, and Passenger Ratings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03427)
*Novel Certada,Amirhesam Aghanouri,Joseba Gorospe,Cristina Olaverri-Monreal*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文基于智能手机IRI估计、车载GNSS-IMU测量和乘客PSR评分三种数据源，在1700公里真实交通条件下评估多模态低成本路面粗糙度监测框架，发现应用间存在系统偏差，验证了IRI与PSR及垂直加速度的相关性，揭示了消费级传感与感知评估集成的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索低成本、多模态、以人为中心的路面粗糙度评估框架，作为高成本专业检测设备的替代方案。

Method: 在奥地利、匈牙利和罗马尼亚1700公里真实交通条件下，同步采集智能手机IRI估计（两款独立应用）、车载GNSS-IMU数据和乘客PSR评分；采用相关性分析、组内相关系数（ICC）和Bland-Altman方法评估应用间一致性。

Result: 两款智能手机应用IRI估计强相关但存在系统偏差，不可互换；IRI与PSR呈显著负相关，验证了感知敏感性；IRI与垂直加速度正相关，确认了路面不平度与车辆动力学的物理联系。

Conclusion: 消费级传感与感知评估集成用于路面粗糙度监测仍面临挑战，难以完全替代高成本专业检测设备。

Abstract: This paper investigates a multi-modal and human-centric framework for low-cost road roughness assessment. The evaluation was based on three complementary data sources: smartphone-based International Roughness Index (IRI) estimates from two independent smartphone-based applications; in-vehicle GNSS-IMU Receiver (Global Navigation Satellite System Receiver with Inertial Measurement Unit) measurements, and passenger Present Serviceability Ratings (PSR). Data were collected over 1700 km across Austria, Hungary, and Romania under real traffic conditions. Inter-application agreement was evaluated using correlation analysis, Intraclass Correlation Coefficient (ICC), and Bland-Altman methods. While the two smartphone applications show strong correlation, systematic bias limits their interchangeability. A significant inverse relationship between IRI and PSR confirms perceptual sensitivity to roughness, and positive correlations between IRI and vertical acceleration validate the physical linkage between pavement irregularities and vehicle dynamics. The results demonstrate the challenges of integrating consumer-grade sensing and perception-based evaluation for road roughness monitoring as an alternative to high-cost specialized survey equipment.

</details>


### [181] [Making Embodied AI Reliable: A Community Agenda from Testing to Formal Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03593)
*Xi Zheng,Dulanga Weerakoon,Yintong Huo,Teresa Yeo,Guy Van Den Broeck,Vijay Ganesh,Daniel Neider,Biplav Srivastava,Ivan Ruchkin,Archan Misra,Corina Pasareanu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文基于AAAI'26 Bridge Program讨论，提出具身AI的可靠性本质上是一个生命周期保障问题，需要通过场景测试、组合验证和运行时保障三种互补方法，并借助神经符号表示和持续反馈实现集成化保障流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身AI系统在开放世界环境中的部署日益增多，但其可靠性保障仍面临根本性挑战，亟需系统化的方法论来应对不确定性、人机交互和涌现行为带来的复杂问题。

Method: 提出三种互补技术方向：(1) 基于可信场景的测试（验证规格+有意义的覆盖指标）；(2) 基于结构化符号表示的组合验证；(3) 适应不确定性和分布漂移的运行时保障机制。强调通过神经符号表示和生命周期持续反馈实现集成化保障工作流。

Result: 揭示了具身AI可靠性问题本质上是一个跨组件紧密耦合的生命周期保障问题，而非单一技术可解；提出了三个互补方向及其集成框架。

Conclusion: 集成化的保障工作流（连接测试、验证和运行时自适应）是构建能够在复杂真实世界中安全可靠运行的具身AI系统的基础。

Abstract: Embodied AI systems are increasingly deployed in open-world environments, yet ensuring their reliability remains a fundamental challenge. Drawing on discussions from the AAAI'26 Bridge Program on "Making Embodied AI Reliable with Testing and Formal Verification", this article argues that reliability in embodied AI is inherently a lifecycle assurance problem arising from uncertainty, human interaction, and emergent behaviors across tightly coupled system components. We identify three complementary directions toward reliable embodied AI: (1) trustworthy scenario-based testing supported by validated specifications and meaningful coverage metrics, (2) compositional verification enabled by structured symbolic representations of system behavior and environmental context, and (3) runtime assurance mechanisms capable of adapting to uncertainty and distribution shifts during deployment. Rather than treating these approaches independently, we advocate integrated assurance workflows that connect testing, verification, and runtime adaptation through shared neuro-symbolic representations and continuous feedback across the system lifecycle. Such integration provides a foundation for building trustworthy embodied AI systems that can operate safely and reliably in complex real-world environments.

</details>


### [182] [DDOR: Delta Debugging for Explainable Overrefusal Testing and Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03601)
*Qinyan Zhou,Peixin Zhang,Jun Sun,Haonan Zhang,Dongxia Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: DDOR是一个全自动、可解释的黑盒框架，通过delta debugging定位最小拒绝触发片段（mRTFs），生成测试套件评估大语言模型的过度拒绝行为，并利用mRTFs进行提示词修复以降低过度拒绝率，同时保持安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全对齐和护栏虽能帮助LLM避免有害输出，但也引发了过度拒绝（overrefusal）问题——模型错误地拒绝本应无害的良性查询。现有方法缺乏可解释性和自动化修复能力。

Method: DDOR采用delta debugging定位短语级别的mRTFs作为拒绝触发原因；基于mRTFs生成多样化上下文提示，通过多oracle验证过滤不安全或模糊案例，构建模型特定的测试套件（约1K/模型）；并利用mRTFs进行针对性提示修复。

Result: 生成了可扩展、模型特定的过度拒绝测试套件（约1K个案例）；通过mRTF引导的提示修复显著降低了过度拒绝率，同时保持了原始意图和对真正有害输入的安全性。

Conclusion: DDOR提供了评估和缓解过度拒绝的端到端实用解决方案，在不牺牲安全性的前提下提高LLM的可用性。

Abstract: While safety alignment and guardrails help large language models (LLMs) avoid harmful outputs, they can also induce overrefusal, i.e., unwarranted rejection of benign queries that merely appear risky. We present DDOR (Delta Debugging for OverRefusal), a fully automated and explainable framework for overrefusal testing and repair in a black-box setting, where only model inputs and outputs are accessible and internal safety mechanisms remain opaque. DDOR applies delta debugging to localize minimal refusal-triggering fragments (mRTFs) that provide phrase-level, explainable evidence for why a refusal occurs. Conditioned on these mRTFs, DDOR generates diverse, context-rich prompts and performs multi-oracle validation to filter intrinsically unsafe or ambiguous cases, producing scalable and model-specific overrefusal test suites (approximately 1K cases per model). Beyond evaluation, we further leverage localized mRTFs to perform targeted prompt repair, substantially reducing overrefusal while preserving the original intent and maintaining safety on genuinely harmful inputs. Overall, DDOR offers a practical end-to-end solution to both evaluate and mitigate overrefusal, improving LLM usability without sacrificing safety.

</details>


### [183] [Can AI be Easy? Lessons Learned from the EZR.py Toolkit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03640)
*Tim Menzies,Srinath Srinivasan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出EZR.py——一个仅400行的Python工具包，通过反复阅读和重构AI代码来简化统一算法，证明在表格型软件工程优化任务中，许多看似不同的学习算法核心几乎相同；测试表明这些小工具在120+优化任务上性能匹敌甚至优于SHAP、LIME、SMAC3等先进方法，且速度更快、所需标注数据更少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反驳"开发者不再需要阅读代码"的流行观点，论证在表格型软件工程优化任务中，阅读和重构代码是产生洞察的有效方法。

Method: 通过反复阅读和重构现有AI工具，将朴素贝叶斯、k-means聚类、CART、模拟退火、局部搜索、主动学习、互补贝叶斯文本过滤等算法简化和统一，构建仅400行的EZR.py工具包。

Result: 在MOOT仓库120+表格型SE优化任务上，EZR性能媲美或超越SHAP、LIME、SMAC3、FASTREAD等方法；运行速度比SMAC3快500倍，所需标注数据量少数量级，可从数千变量中构建少于十个变量的决策树。

Conclusion: 在表格型SE优化任务范围内，阅读和重构代码是产生洞察的有用方法，小型统一工具包能够媲美大型库。

Abstract: Much recent press claims that developers no longer need to read code. We disagree, at least within the domain of tabular software-engineering (SE) optimization tasks: rows of $x$ and $y$ values where the $y$ values are expensive to obtain.
  As evidence we present 400 lines of EZR.py, a Python toolkit (no heavy dependencies) that implements Naive Bayes, $k$-means clustering, classification and regression trees, simulated annealing, local search, active learning, and complementary-Bayes text-mining relevance filtering for tabular SE data. EZR was built by repeatedly reading and refactoring AI tools to simplify and unify them. The result demonstrates that many seemingly different learning algorithms are nearly the same once stripped back to their core: classical algorithms collapse to a few lines each, and a state-of-the-art active learner fits in roughly 80 lines.
  Tested on the 120+ tabular SE optimization tasks in the MOOT repository, these tiny tools perform as well as or better than state-of-the-art explanation tools (SHAP, LIME), the SMAC3 optimizer, and SVM-based text-mining filters (FASTREAD), while running 500$\times$ faster than SMAC3, using orders of magnitude less labelled data, and building trees from fewer than ten variables even when thousands are available.
  We conclude that, within the scope of tabular SE optimization, reading and refactoring code is a useful method of generating insight, and small unified toolkits can rival large libraries.
  EZR is available under an open-source license. Install via \textsf{pip install ezr}; example data at \textsf{github.com/timm/moot}.

</details>


### [184] [Agentic Generation and Evolution of Knowledge Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03662)
*Man Zhang,Tao Yue,Nazareno M. Aguirre,Diego Garbervetsky,Sebastian Uchitel*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出TrustModel，一种通过三个智能子系统（建模、一致性检查、演化引导）实现知识模型自主生成与持续演化的愿景框架，以支持动态环境下复杂软件系统的可信推理与工程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂软件系统（如自动驾驶、机器人）与动态的物理、网络和社会环境交互，其行为推理、维护和安全演化需要关于系统、假设和运行上下文的可信知识。知识模型虽然为此提供了基础，但会随着系统演化变得不完整、不一致或过时。

Method: 提出TrustModel框架，包含三个智能子系统：Modeling（构建和更新知识模型）、Conformance（评估模型与系统及环境的一致性）、Evolution（生成指导以保持模型与新兴变化同步）。通过基于模型的测试展示其可实例化性。

Result: 展示了TrustModel在基于模型的测试中的实例化应用，并讨论了其在需求与假设监控、架构漂移追踪、变更影响评估等其他MDE活动中的支持潜力。

Conclusion: 将活知识模型定位为持续演化的软件系统可信工程的基石，为动态环境下的软件可信性保障提供了新范式。

Abstract: Complex software systems such as autonomous vehicles, robotics increasingly interact with dynamic physical, cyber, and social environments. Reasoning about their behavior, maintaining them under continuous change, and evolving them safely require trustworthy knowledge about the system, its assumptions, and its operating context. Knowledge models (KMs) provide a practical basis for such reasoning, but they may themselves become incomplete, inconsistent, or outdated as systems evolve. This paper presents TrustModel, a vision for the agentic generation and evolution of living KMs. TrustModel comprises three agentic subsystems: Modeling, for constructing and updating KMs; Conformance, for assessing their alignment with the system and its environment; and Evolution, for generating guidance to keep KMs synchronized with emerging changes. We demonstrate how TrustModel can be instantiated for model-based testing and discuss its potential for supporting other MDE activities, such as requirements and assumption monitoring, architectural drift tracking, and change impact assessment. Overall, TrustModel positions living KMs as a foundation for dependable engineering of continuously evolving software systems.

</details>


### [185] [FLARE: Fine-Grained Diagnostic Feedback for LLM Code Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03852)
*Yinsheng Yao,Hongxiang Zhang,Weixi Tong,Tianyi Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Flare框架，利用轻量级诊断模型预测行级可疑信号进行bug定位和代码精炼，无需候选搜索即可超越最强基线1.72%-7.42%，10个候选搜索平均提升8.50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖测试失败或自我批判等反馈信号进行代码迭代精炼，但这些信号要么过于粗粒度（如测试通过/失败），要么过于高层（如自我评论文本），无法有效告知模型具体在何处修复bug，缺乏细粒度的定位指导。

Method: 提出Flare迭代框架，核心是轻量级诊断模型，该模型预测行级可疑性信号用于bug定位；利用诊断预测的不确定性，在top-k可疑区域进行搜索，根据执行结果选择最佳候选代码。

Result: 在LiveCodeBench和BigCodeBench上使用5种基础LLM进行实验：k=1（无候选搜索）时绝对提升1.72%-7.42%；10个候选搜索平均提升8.50%；轻量级诊断模型在故障定位任务上达到最优性能。

Conclusion: Flare通过提供可靠的细粒度行级诊断指导，有效提升代码修复性能，验证了细粒度定位信号对代码精炼的重要性。

Abstract: Large language models often generate code with bugs. Existing methods rely on feedback signals such as test failures and self-critiques to iteratively refine the generated code. Such signals are either too coarse-grained or too high-level, which is not sufficient to inform the model where to fix the bug. In this work, we present Flare, an iterative framework with a lightweight diagnostic model that predicts line-level suspiciousness signals for bug localization and code refinement. Given the inherent uncertainty of diagnostic predictions, Flare searches over the top-k suspicious regions and selects the best candidate according to execution outcomes. Experiments on LiveCodeBench and BigCodeBench with five base LLMs show that, even without candidate search (k=1), Flare outperforms the strongest baseline with an absolute improvement from 1.72% to 7.42%. Furthermore, searching over 10 candidates yields an average improvement of 8.50% compared with no candidate search. When evaluated in isolation, our lightweight diagnostic model achieves the best performance compared with recent fault localization methods, demonstrating that it can provide reliable fine-grained guidance for code refinement.

</details>


### [186] [Automated Repair of Requirements for Cyber-Physical Systems in Simulink Requirements Tables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03870)
*Aren A. Babikian,Alessio Di Sandro,Federico Formica,Claudio Menghi,Marsha Chechik*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 针对CPS中系统实现与需求持续演化导致的合规性问题，提出了一种利用系统执行数据修复需求而非修改系统的框架，在六个真实案例研究中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂软件系统（如CPS）中，系统实现与需求往往独立演化，导致两者偏离。传统合规恢复技术（如自动程序修复）假设需求正确，但实际中需求本身可能存在错误、过时或不充分的问题，因此需要修复需求而非仅修复系统。

Method: 提出一个利用系统执行数据修复CPS需求的框架，评估基于时间的实值信号的声明式需求的正确性，使用MATLAB Simulink Requirements Tables语言表达，并实现了七个变体。

Result: 在六个真实案例研究、12条需求上评估了七个框架变体，结果表明该框架能有效生成正确且有用的修复后需求。

Conclusion: 所提出的需求修复框架能够有效恢复CPS中需求与系统之间的合规性，补充了传统仅关注系统修复的方法。

Abstract: The development of complex software systems, e.g., cyber-physical systems (CPSs), involves continuous evolution of both system implementations and their requirements. These two artifacts often proceed independently, creating a risk of misalignment. For example, a system may be updated due to implementation-level concerns, yielding a new version that no longer satisfies its original requirements. Traditional compliance recovery techniques, e.g., automated program repair, address this problem by modifying the system while assuming that requirements are correct. However, faulty, outdated or inadequate requirements are a well-documented challenge in practice, motivating the complementary task of requirement repair. In this paper, we propose a framework that leverages system execution data to repair misaligned CPS requirements, thereby restoring requirement-to-system compliance. Our approach evaluates the correctness of declarative requirements over time-based, real-valued signals expressed using the MATLAB Simulink Requirements Tables language. We evaluate seven variants of our framework on six real-world case studies covering 12 requirements. Results confirm the effectiveness of the proposed framework in producing correct and useful repaired requirements.

</details>


### [187] [The Impact of Configuring Agentic AI Coding Tools on Build-vs-Buy Decisions: A Study Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03907)
*Jai Lal Lulla,Matthias Galster,Jie M. Zhang,Sebastian Baltes,Christoph Treude*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一项预注册的实验协议，系统研究配置机制（如上下文文件、Skills、MCP工具、权限控制）如何影响Claude Code和OpenAI Codex等Agentic AI编码工具在"构建vs购买"决策（即从头实现功能还是引入外部库）中的行为，并测量其对库选择、披露完整性和准确性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic AI编码工具在自主编写代码时需要决定是引入外部库还是从头实现功能（build-versus-buy），这一决策直接影响软件安全、许可合规、性能和可维护性。然而，目前尚无受控实验研究揭示哪些配置机制能最有效地影响这一决策过程，因此亟需系统性的实验研究。

Method: 采用预注册实验协议，使用标准化基准测试中的编程任务（每个任务围绕可识别的build-versus-buy决策点构建），在Claude Code和OpenAI Codex两种工具上操纵多种配置条件（无配置、含软偏好和明确禁止的上下文文件、可自主发现的Skills指令、MCP驱动的库发现工具、权限控制），测量库选择、披露完整性及准确性，并验证9项预注册假设。

Result: 预期产出包括：①配置机制对build-versus-buy行为影响的经验证据；②一个可复用的基准数据集和分析流水线，用于评估Agentic AI编码工具中的build-versus-buy行为。

Conclusion: 该研究首次通过受控实验揭示配置机制对Agentic AI编码工具build-versus-buy决策的影响机制，为开发者如何通过配置手段引导AI工具的依赖管理行为提供可操作指导，同时为软件安全与合规治理提供方法论基础。

Abstract: Agentic AI coding tools write code with increasing autonomy and in doing so decide when to import a library and when to implement functionality from scratch. These decisions, whether to build functionality from scratch or buy into an external library, hereafter build-versus-buy, carry direct consequences for software security, licensing compliance, performance, and long-term maintainability. Yet no controlled experimental study has examined what governs build-versus-buy decisions in agentic AI coding tools. Configuration mechanisms, i.e., the means by which developers tailor agentic AI coding tool behavior to a project or workflow, are one of the primary means by which practitioners can influence these decisions. However, it is unclear which configuration mechanisms influence build-versus-buy decisions most effectively. We present a pre-registered protocol to study how configuration mechanisms alter build-versus-buy behavior in two popular agentic AI coding tools: Claude Code and OpenAI Codex. We will execute controlled programming tasks drawn from a benchmark of staged projects, each constructed around identifiable build-versus-buy points, and will manipulate the configuration supplied to each tool, ranging from no configuration, through context files with soft preferences and explicit prohibitions, to Skills (instructions that can be autonomously discovered), MCP-enabled library discovery tools, and permission controls, measuring which libraries the tool selects, whether it discloses newly introduced libraries, and whether those disclosures are complete and accurate. Nine pre-registered hypotheses structure the protocol. The resulting benchmark dataset and analysis pipeline will be released as a reusable artifact for evaluating build-versus-buy behavior in agentic AI coding tools.

</details>

### [188] [Oscillatory State-Space Models as Inductive Biases for Physics-Informed Neural PDE Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02623)
*Abhishek Chandra,Taniya Kapoor*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种融合振荡状态空间动力学的物理信息神经网络（PINN）方法，通过线性振荡器建模时间演化并结合PDE感知的谱基函数处理空间模态，在正向、逆向及高维（最高100维）PDE问题上相比现有序列模型PINN方法取得了更高精度和更低内存消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于序列模型的PINN方法虽能捕获时间依赖，但未显式编码PDE解的结构化动力学特征，且内存需求随序列长度和分辨率增长，限制了大规模和高维问题的应用。

Method: 利用线性振荡器状态空间动力学对时间演化进行建模，同时结合PDE感知的谱基函数处理空间模态，实现了封闭形式的空间微分和边界条件的一致性约束。

Result: 在前向、逆向和高维（最高100维）PDE问题上，相比现有序列模型PINN方法，取得了更优的精度和更低的内存开销。

Conclusion: 将结构化动力学先验融入神经PDE求解器的时间演化过程，有助于设计更符合物理规律且计算高效的PINN架构。

Abstract: Solving time-dependent partial differential equations (PDEs) is an important problem in computational science and engineering. Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) learn PDE solutions from governing equations. However, accurately capturing temporal evolution remains challenging. Recent sequence-model-based approaches parameterize time evolution using general-purpose sequence models, which capture temporal dependencies but do not explicitly encode the structured dynamics of PDE solutions. In addition, their memory requirements can scale unfavorably with sequence length and resolution, limiting applicability in large-scale or high-dimensional settings. This work introduces a PINN approach that incorporates oscillatory state-space dynamics to represent the modal structure of PDE solutions. The proposed method leverages a linear-oscillator-based temporal evolution, together with a PDE-aware spectral basis in space. This design enables closed-form spatial differentiation and consistent enforcement of boundary conditions. The method is evaluated on forward, inverse, and high-dimensional PDE problems, including cases up to 100 spatial dimensions. The results show improved accuracy and reduced memory usage compared to recent sequence-model-based PINN approaches. Overall, this work highlights the benefits of incorporating structured dynamical priors into the temporal evolution of neural PDE solvers and suggests designing more physics-aligned and computationally efficient PINN architectures.

</details>


### [189] [Spike-Aware C++ INT8 Inference for Sparse Spiking Language Models on Commodity CPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03026)
*Ting Liu*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文面向符号语言模型SymbolicLight V1，构建了将稀疏二值脉冲状态作为执行原语的C++ CPU推理运行时，通过混合布局、AVX2/FMA、INT8量化等技术，在AMD Ryzen 7 5800X上实现了22.63 tokens/s的单线程解码速度（对比TinyLlama-1.1B Q8_0的16.31 tokens/s），四线程达47.90 tokens/s，但WikiText-2困惑度24.80劣于稠密基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脉冲语言模型存在稠密Transformer运行时无法直接利用的激活稀疏性，现有方法仅做事后权重压缩，未将稀疏脉冲状态作为执行原语来系统性地提升推理效率，特别是在边缘和具身智能场景的低核心本地推理需求下。

Method: 基于SymbolicLight V1脉冲门控语言模型，实现C++ CPU推理运行时：清单驱动的权重加载器、混合行/列内存布局、AVX2/FMA内核、逐通道对称INT8量化、整数域累加的脉冲条件稀疏路径。包括标量FP32基线、混合布局AVX2 FP32和AVX2 INT8三种实现版本。

Result: 标量FP32基线9.5 tokens/s；混合布局AVX2 FP32提升至14.7 tokens/s；AVX2 INT8达到19.9 tokens/s（同一步30k导出），权重从3.49 GB降至1.06 GB。874M参数INT8导出单线程22.63 tokens/s，四线程47.90 tokens/s；512 token预填充从单线程29.86提升至八线程94.68 tokens/s。WikiText-2困惑度24.80，劣于稠密基线。

Conclusion: 脉冲感知执行能够提升稀疏脉冲语言模型的CPU吞吐量和内存行为，但模型质量、受控稠密训练基线、具身任务评估和CPU能耗测量仍有待解决。该工作作为稀疏语言运行时的推理系统研究，长远目标服务于需要近传感器低核心推理的具身和边缘智能体。

Abstract: Spiking language models expose activation sparsity that dense Transformer runtimes do not directly exploit. This paper studies that property from a systems perspective. Building on the SymbolicLight V1 spike-gated language model family, we implement a C++ CPU inference runtime that treats sparse binary spike states as an execution primitive rather than only applying post-hoc weight compression. The runtime combines a manifest-driven weight loader, mixed row/column memory layout, AVX2/FMA kernels, per-channel symmetric INT8 quantization, and integer-domain accumulation for spike-conditioned sparse paths. On an AMD Ryzen 7 5800X, an early scalar FP32 baseline decodes at 9.5 tokens/s. Mixed-layout AVX2 FP32 raises this to 14.7 tokens/s, and AVX2 INT8 reaches 19.9 tokens/s on the same step-30k export while reducing the weight footprint from 3.49 GB to 1.06 GB. For the available 186k-step 874M-parameter INT8 export, the C++ runtime decodes at 22.63 tokens/s in a single-thread CPU benchmark, compared with 16.31 tokens/s for TinyLlama-1.1B Q8_0, 11.26 tokens/s for Falcon3-1B Q8_0, and 9.70 tokens/s for Qwen2.5-1.5B Q8_0 under llama.cpp. Thread scaling reaches 47.90 tokens/s at four CPU threads, and 512-token prefill improves from 29.86 to 94.68 tokens/s from one to eight threads. The throughput result comes with a quality cost: the SNN reports WikiText-2 perplexity 24.80, worse than the dense baselines in the same benchmark. We frame the result as an inference-systems study for sparse language runtimes, with longer-term motivation in embodied and edge agents that may benefit from local, low-core inference near sensors and actuators. Spike-aware execution can improve CPU throughput and memory behavior for sparse spiking language models, while model quality, controlled dense training baselines, embodied-task evaluation, and measured CPU energy remain open problems.

</details>


### [190] [PSViT: A Methodology for Structurally Pruning Spiking Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03257)
*Rachmad Vidya Wicaksana Putra,Achyuta Muthuvelan,Alberto Marchisio,Muhammad Shafique*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出PSViT，一种针对脉冲视觉Transformer（SViT）的结构化剪枝方法，通过均匀通道级滤波器剪枝、敏感性分析和细粒度通道剪枝，在ImageNet-1K上实现22.4%的内存节省，精度损失控制在3%以内，使得SViT可在现有通用计算架构上高效部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SViT模型虽性能优异但参数量大，难以部署在资源受限的嵌入式平台；已有非结构化剪枝方法需要专用硬件支持，可扩展性差，因此需要一种可兼容现有通用计算架构的结构化剪枝方法。

Method: PSViT采用三步流程：1）均匀通道级滤波器剪枝，结构性消除非显著权重；2）敏感性分析，评估逐层通道剪枝对精度和网络规模的影响；3）基于敏感性分析和给定网络架构的细粒度通道剪枝。

Result: 在ImageNet-1K上，单次剪枝实现22.4%内存节省；未微调精度70.3%，微调后精度72.8%，相比原始SViT模型（73.3%）精度损失均在3%以内。

Conclusion: PSViT推动了SViT在资源受限应用中的高效部署进程，验证了结构化剪枝在脉冲视觉Transformer上的有效性。

Abstract: Spiking Vision Transformer (SViT) models are promising low-power ViT models for solving vision-based tasks with state-of-the-art performance. However, their large sizes limit their deployments for resource-constrained embedded platforms, underscoring the needs of model compression. One of prominent compression techniques is pruning, and the state-of-the-art works employ unstructured pruning techniques to compress SViT models. Such techniques require specialized hardware architectures tailored for the sparsity patterns to maximize their efficiency benefits, making this approach not scalable. To address this, we propose PSViT, a novel methodology to perform structured pruning on SViT models, hence making it possible to efficiently accelerate their inference using the existing and widely-used computing architectures. To do this, PSViT employs several key steps: uniform channel-wise filter pruning to structurally eliminate the non-significant weights, sensitivity analysis to evaluate the impact of channel-wise pruning of individual layer on accuracy and network size, as well as fine-grained channel-wise pruning based on the sensitivity analysis and the given network architecture. Experimental results show that PSViT effectively obtains 22.4% memory saving through single-shot pruning, while maintaining high accuracy within 3% (70.3% without fine-tuning and 72.8% with fine-tuning) from the original non-pruned SViT model (73.3%) on the ImageNet-1K. These results also show that the PSViT methodology advances the effort in enabling efficient SViT deployments on resource-constrained applications.

</details>


### [191] [PrimeSVT: An Automated Memory-aware Pruning Framework with Prioritized Compression Policy for Spiking Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03428)
*Rachmad Vidya Wicaksana Putra,Achyuta Muthuvelan,Alberto Marchisio,Muhammad Shafique*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 提出PrimeSVT框架，针对脉冲视觉Transformer（SViT）模型实现自动化、内存感知的结构化剪枝，在无需专用硬件的条件下通过逐层优先级压缩策略，在保持精度损失3%以内（70.3%无微调/72.9%有微调 vs 原始73.3%）的同时节省26.68%内存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SViT模型体积大，阻碍嵌入式部署；非结构化剪枝需要专用硬件加速器且手动调参耗时、不可扩展，亟需自动化结构化剪枝方法。

Method: 首先按层大小（参数量）排序，基于各层在不同剪枝率下的鲁棒性识别目标剪枝层；然后采用优先级压缩策略，从最大层到最小层逐层进行L2范数-based通道级滤波器剪枝，同时满足用户定义的精度和内存约束。

Result: 单次自动化剪枝节省26.68%内存，无微调精度70.3%、有微调精度72.9%，原始模型精度73.3%，精度损失在3%以内。

Conclusion: PrimeSVT框架实现了SViT的设计自动化，使其能够高效部署在通用计算架构上，适用于嵌入式场景。

Abstract: The large sizes of Spiking Vision Transformers (SViTs) still hinder their embedded implementation, highlighting the need for model compression. State-of-the-art works compress SViT models through unstructured pruning, which needs specialized hardware accelerators for their specific sparsity patterns to maximize efficiency gains. Moreover, their manual approach requires a huge design time to find an appropriate pruning setting for each network, thus making this approach not scalable. To address this limitation, we propose PrimeSVT, a novel framework that performs automated memory-aware structured pruning on pre-trained SViT models, thereby maximizing their efficiency gains during inference amenable to widely-used computing architectures. To achieve this, PrimeSVT first sorts the SViT layers based on their sizes (i.e., number of parameters), identifies the targeted pruning layers based on their robustness under different pruning rates, then leverages this order for compressing the model layer-by-layer sequentially from the largest one to the smallest one (i.e., so-called prioritized compression policy), while considering the user-defined constraints (i.e., acceptable accuracy and memory saving). In each layer, PrimeSVT employs channel-wise filter pruning based on their L2-norm values to structurally remove the non-significant weights. Experimental results show that PrimeSVT saves 26.68% memory through automated single-shot pruning, while preserving accuracy within 3% (70.3% without fine-tuning and 72.9% with fine-tuning) from the original unpruned SViT model (73.3%), thus meeting the accuracy and memory constraints. These show that our PrimeSVT framework enables design automation for SViTs and their embedded implementation.

</details>


### [192] [Signed Spiking Neuron Enabled by an Orthogonal-Easy-Axis Magnetic Tunnel Junction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03796)
*Huannan Zheng,Jingli Liu,Kezhou Yang*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于磁隧道结(MTJ)的紧凑型有符号漏积分点火(sLIF)神经元，通过正交易轴设计实现双极性尖峰生成，在CIFAR-10和CIFAR10-DVS上分别达到91.06%和77.40%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有符号尖峰神经元比标准尖峰神经元携带更丰富的信息，但现有硬件实现方案尚不充分，需要一种紧凑高效的硬件神经元设计方案。

Method: 利用自由层和钉扎层中正交易轴的MTJ器件，通过磁矩动力学映射有符号LIF膜电位演化，实现双极性尖峰生成。通过Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert仿真确定最优自由层尺寸（10nm×45nm×50nm，纵横比约2:9:10），确保器件响应遵循有符号LIF方程。

Result: 拟合的器件神经元模型在CIFAR-10上达到91.06%，在CIFAR10-DVS上达到77.40%，保留了理想有符号LIF神经元的大部分精度。

Conclusion: 提出的MTJ-based有符号LIF神经元在保持紧凑性的同时实现了与理想神经元相近的精度，为脉冲神经网络硬件实现提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Signed spiking neurons carry richer information than standard spiking neurons. This work proposes a compact magnetic tunnel junction (MTJ)-based neuron for signed leaky integrate-and-fire (LIF) operation. With orthogonal easy axes in the free and pinned layers, the device enables bipolar spike generation and maps magnetic-moment dynamics to signed LIF membrane-potential evolution. Landau--Lifshitz--Gilbert simulations show that proper free-layer dimensions allow the device response to follow a signed LIF equation. A representative design of 10 nm x 45 nm x 50 nm corresponds to an aspect ratio of about 2:9:10. Network evaluations using the fitted device-neuron model achieve 91.06% on CIFAR-10 and 77.40% on CIFAR10-DVS, retaining most of the accuracy of ideal signed LIF neurons.

</details>


### [193] [Quadratic integrate-and-fire neurons exhibit less fragmented loss landscapes and outperform leaky integrate-and-fire neurons in spike-based gradient descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03935)
*Carlo Wenig,Raoul-Martin Memmesheimer,Christian Klos*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文通过实验对比发现，使用二次积分点火（QIF）神经元替代泄漏积分点火（LIF）神经元进行梯度下降训练，能避免因尖峰(消)失导致的训练不连续问题，在Spiking Heidelberg Digits数据集上取得更好性能，并呈现出更平滑的损失和梯度景观。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LIF神经元在基于尖峰的梯度下降训练中，微小的参数变化会导致尖峰(消)失，引起后续活动紊乱、神经表征不稳定以及神经元永久静默，而QIF神经元理论上能避免这些不连续性，但其实际优势尚不明确。

Method: 在Spiking Heidelberg Digits数据集上对LIF和QIF神经元网络进行系统性超参数搜索优化，对比二者性能；可视化损失景观和梯度景观，分析单样本景观中尖峰时序变化对训练的影响。

Result: QIF神经元在性能上明显优于LIF神经元；LIF神经元的损失景观因不连续性而呈现碎片化，梯度更为不规则；单样本分析表明这些特征源于尖峰时序变化导致的破坏性尖峰(消)失。

Conclusion: 建议在梯度下降训练中用具有连续尖峰动力学的神经元模型（如QIF神经元）替代LIF神经元。

Abstract: The ability to train spiking neural networks is essential for modeling biological neural networks as well as for neuromorphic computing. However, for the extensively used leaky integrate-and-fire (LIF) neurons, arbitrarily small parameter changes can induce spike (dis)appearances that disrupt subsequent activity, leading to unstable neural representations and permanently silent neurons during exact spike-based gradient descent. Recent work shows that a class of neuron models, which includes the quadratic integrate-and-fire (QIF) neuron, avoids these discontinuities and enables continuous and even smooth spike-based gradient descent. However, it remains unclear whether these advantages translate into practice. Here, we demonstrate that they do so via a controlled comparison between networks of LIF and QIF neurons on the popular Spiking Heidelberg Digits dataset. Specifically, in a first step, we perform a thorough hyperparameter search to optimize both models, revealing a clear performance advantage of QIF neurons. In a second step, we visualize the loss and gradient landscapes. Consistent with their inferior performance, we find that the loss landscapes of LIF neurons, which are discontinuous, appear more fragmented and the related gradients more erratic. An analysis of the landscapes of single samples indicates that these features arise from changes in the temporal order of spikes, which often cause disruptive spike (dis)appearances. Overall, our results advocate replacing LIF neurons with neuron models exhibiting continuous spiking dynamics, such as QIF neurons, for gradient descent training.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [194] [Too Much of a Good Thing: When sim2real Efforts Impede Policy Learning (And What to Do About It)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02636)
*Kyle Morgenstein,Bharath Masetty,Stephen Welch,Luis Sentis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文批判了强化学习中sim2real过度投入导致的问题，提出了sim2sim2real新范式，仅以机器人运动学为设计约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前sim2real方法过度强调与真实世界的对齐，反而限制了策略学习，导致模拟器锁定和策略探索不足，需要重新审视和调整研究范式。

Method: 提出sim2sim2real范式：仅以机器人运动学作为唯一设计约束，先在sim2sim阶段进行策略学习，再通过sim2real阶段转移到真实硬件，解耦模拟器精度与策略优化过程。

Result: 诊断了当前sim2real领域存在的激励错配问题：过度约束导致策略探索受限，陷入局部最优。

Conclusion: sim2real应适度而非过度，sim2sim2real范式通过放松不必要的真实世界约束，有望提升策略学习的效率和泛化能力。

Abstract: While sim2real efforts are necessary for effective policy transfer to hardware, there is such a thing as too much of a good thing. We argue that sim2real efforts have led to misaligned incentives with policy learning, resulting in simulator lock in and poor policy exploration due to the unreasonable constraints imposed by the real world. We offer a diagnosis and explanation of the current status of the problem, and propose a potential solution via a sim2sim2real paradigm that leverages the robot's kinematics as the sole design constraint.

</details>


### [195] [CARVE: Certified Affordable Repair of Vetoed Maneuvers via Envelopes for Interactive Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02641)
*Yifan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CARVE是一个预测无关的交互修复认证层，能够为被规则否决的驾驶 maneuver 提供可证明安全的修复方案，明确修复归属、优先级代价和回退策略。在589个INTERACTION场景中，CARVE-Greedy接受了98.64%被否决的maneuver，恢复了370/378个人类判定的误否决，同时保持了100%的路权尊重和零优先级误报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶栈中，硬规则边距为负时可能通过非优先智能体的小幅合法调整恢复可行性，但规则手册、屏蔽器和可达性过滤器缺乏运行时证明对象来声明：哪些有界多智能体编辑可修复maneuver、谁拥有编辑权、请求是否具有路权可负担性，以及请求未响应的回退方案。

Method: CARVE构建了一个有限格上的预测无关证书层，包含自我和智能体拥有的战术算子。智能体拥有的请求仅在合作包络线 \(B_j(s) = β(π_j)α_j^{\max}(s)\) 内可接受，该包络线将运动学可达性与规范性优先级分离。证书记录绑定规则、修复类别、修复集、责任加权代价分配和回退方案。

Result: 在589个INTERACTION回放片段上，CARVE-Greedy接受了98.64%初始被否决的maneuver，恢复了370/378个人类判定的误否决，保持了589/589的路权尊重、零优先级智能体误报和400/400的负压力否决。

Conclusion: CARVE不预测或要求其他驾驶者合规；它在声明的假设下，证明提议的交互是否是有界的、可归因的、规范性可接受的。

Abstract: Interactive driving exposes a failure mode that is easy to miss in rule-aware autonomous-driving stacks: a hard-rule margin can be negative for an ego candidate even though a small lawful accommodation by a non-priority agent would restore feasibility. Existing rulebooks, shields, and reachability filters are strong at vetoing unsafe actions, while prediction-based planners model likely responses. Neither returns a runtime proof object that states which bounded multi-agent edit repairs the maneuver, who owns the edit, whether the request is right-of-way affordable, and what ego fallback remains if the request is not observed. We formulate this missing object as *interactive repair certification* and introduce *CARVE*, a prediction-free certificate layer over a finite lattice of ego-owned and agent-owned tactical operators. Agent-owned requests are admissible only inside \(B_j(s) = β(π_j)α_j^{\max}(s)\), a cooperation envelope that separates kinematic reachability from normative priority. The resulting certificate records the binding rule, repair category, repair set, responsibility-weighted cost split, and fallback. On 589 Lanelet2-geometry-grounded INTERACTION replay episodes, CARVE-Greedy accepts 98.64% of initially vetoed maneuvers and recovers 370/378 human-resolved false vetoes, while preserving 589/589 right-of-way respect, zero priority-agent false positives, and 400/400 negative-stress vetoes. We prove certificate soundness, structural right-of-way respect, exact finite-lattice minimality, fallback contingency, and blame-consistency conditions. CARVE does not predict or require another driver's compliance; it certifies whether a proposed interaction is bounded, attributable, and normatively admissible under declared assumptions.

</details>


### [196] [Fixed-Time Dynamic Landing of Quadrotors using Adaptive Unscented Kalman Filtering and Nonlinear Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02658)
*Mohammadreza Izadi,Zeinab Shayan,Steven Waslander,Reza Faieghi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合非线性模型预测控制与最小加加速度轨迹规划器的动态降落框架，并采用自适应无迹卡尔曼滤波增强鲁棒性，在仿真和硬件实验中实现了多旋翼无人机在移动平台上的重复性动态降落。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现多旋翼无人机在移动平台上的动态降落，并解决降落过程中定时一致性、传感质量时变导致的鲁棒性问题。

Method: 集成非线性模型预测控制（NMPC）与实时最小加加速度轨迹规划器（强制设定预定触地时间）；采用自适应无迹卡尔曼滤波（AUKF）在线更新过程与测量噪声统计量。

Result: 仿真与硬件实验表明，该方法实现了可重复的降落性能，并在平台速度预测精度上优于EKF/UKF方法。

Conclusion: 提出的框架能够有效实现多旋翼无人机在移动平台上的动态降落，具备良好的鲁棒性与定时一致性。

Abstract: This paper introduces an estimation and control framework for dynamic landing of multi-rotor uncrewed aerial vehicles on moving platforms. The proposed method integrates nonlinear model predictive control with a real-time minimum-jerk trajectory planner that enforces a prescribed touchdown time, enabling consistent timing during the terminal descent. To enhance robustness in the presence of time-varying sensing quality, we utilize an adaptive unscented kalman filter that updates the process and measurement noise statistics online. In addition, we provide a reference feasibility analysis showing that minimum-jerk references induce bounded thrust and torque commands under standard tracking hypotheses. The proposed framework is evaluated in simulation and hardware experiments, and it is shown to achieve repeatable landings and improved platform velocity prediction accuracy relative to EKF/UKF-based methods.

</details>


### [197] [Multi-Robot Bearing-only Pose Estimation via Angle Rigidity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03931)
*J. Francisco Presenza,Leonardo J. Colombo,Ignacio Mas,Juan I. Giribet*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于方位角的分布式位姿估计器，用于时变多机器人系统。该方法利用机体坐标系下的方位角计算角度，在无需机器人朝向信息的情况下估计其在ℝ³中的位置，并从估计位置、方位角及其导数中恢复SO(3)上的朝向。观测器仅需有向感知拓扑满足"角度刚性"条件（弱于常见的方位刚性），并在持续激励条件下建立了局部一致指数稳定性。仿真验证了方案的有效性和实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式位姿估计方法通常依赖方位刚性等较强拓扑条件，对感知拓扑要求较高。本文旨在放松这一条件，提出仅需角度刚性拓扑即可实现的分布式估计方案，适用于更广泛的多机器人协同场景。

Method: 利用机体坐标系下的方位角计算角度信息，先估计机器人位置（无需朝向信息），再通过估计位置、方位角及其导数恢复SO(3)上的朝向。采用分布式观测器结构，仅需有向感知拓扑满足角度刚性条件。

Result: 在部分机器人满足持续激励运动条件下，证明了观测器的局部一致指数稳定性；通过仿真验证了方案的有效性和实用性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于方位角的分布式位姿估计器在弱拓扑条件（角度刚性）下仍能实现稳定的位姿估计，降低了系统对感知拓扑的要求，拓展了分布式估计的适用性。

Abstract: This letter proposes a novel distributed bearing-based pose estimator for time-varying multi-robot systems. The method uses angles computed from body-frame bearings to estimate the robots' positions in $\mathbb{R}^3$ without knowledge of their orientations. The orientations in $\mathrm{SO}(3)$ are recovered from the estimated positions, the bearings, and the bearing derivatives. The proposed observer only requires the (directed) sensing topology to be \textit{angle-rigid}, a weaker condition than the commonly used ones like bearing rigidity. Local uniform exponential stability of the proposed observer is established under the assumption of persistently exciting motions for a subset of robots. Simulations are presented and discussed to evaluate the scheme's effectiveness and practicality.

</details>


### [198] [Motion Planning in Dynamic Environments: A Survey from Classical to Modern Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02677)
*Zongyuan Shen,Yaming Ou,Shalabh Gupta,Shancheng Zhao,Dehua Zhou,Gao Wang,Zhongqiang Ren,Junfeng Fan,Long Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文对2015-2025年间138篇动态环境下移动机器人运动规划方法进行了全面综述，将方法分为采样、图搜索、模型预测控制、学习及经典局部规划五类，并讨论了动态感知技术及预测不确定性、人机交互、冻结机器人问题等核心挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有综述多聚焦于静态环境下的运动规划，缺乏针对动态环境（如移动障碍物、人机交互、预测不确定性等）的系统性综述，因此有必要对动态环境下的运动规划方法进行全面梳理与分类。

Method: 对138篇主要发表于2015-2025年的文献进行综述，将运动规划方法分为五类：采样方法、图搜索、模型预测控制、学习方法（监督学习与强化学习）以及经典局部规划方法（速度障碍法、势场法、动态窗口法）。同时涵盖动态感知技术（相机、LiDAR、事件传感器）及其在障碍物检测与建模中的作用。

Result: 系统梳理了各类方法的原理、优势与局限性，特别分析了动态环境中的预测不确定性、人机交互问题以及冻结机器人问题等独特挑战，为研究者提供了结构化理解。

Conclusion: 本文为动态环境下运动规划研究提供了全面的分类框架与分析，有助于研究者快速了解该领域的主要方法、前沿进展及尚未解决的关键挑战。

Abstract: Motion planning in dynamic environments requires robots to continuously adapt their paths in response to environmental changes for safe and uninterrupted navigation. While many surveys have reviewed planning in static settings, systematic reviews focused on dynamic environments remain limited. This paper presents a comprehensive survey of 138 works, primarily published between 2015 and 2025, spanning both classical and learning-based approaches. The motion planning methods are grouped into five categories based on the concepts of sampling, graph search, model predictive control, learning, and additional classical local planning approaches, including velocity obstacles, potential fields and dynamic windows. The learning techniques include supervised learning and reinforcement learning. We also discuss the role of dynamic perception in motion planning, covering techniques for detecting and modeling moving obstacles using cameras, LiDAR, and event-based sensors. The survey analyzes the principles, strengths, and limitations of each method, with particular attention to challenges unique to dynamic environments, such as prediction uncertainty, human-robot interaction, and the freezing robot problem. The survey provides researchers with a structured understanding of motion planning methods in dynamic environments.

</details>


### [199] [See Less, Specify More: Visual Evidence Budgets for Generalizable VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02735)
*Yueh-Hua Wu,Tatsuya Matsushima,Kei Ota*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: S2（See Less, Specify More）框架通过“精简视觉输入、细化指令描述”双管齐下，显著提升VLA模型在干扰、外观变化和语义相似任务上的泛化能力，在真实机器人任务上将平均子任务成功率从54.2%提升至79.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型泛化能力不足，面对干扰物、外观变化和语义相似任务时，粗粒度指令导致监督混淆，无约束视觉上下文使执行器难以聚焦关键信息，亟需一种更干净的训练接口来解耦这些瓶颈。

Method: S2框架包含两个核心组件：(1) **Specify More**：保留原始指令作为高层目标，同时为每条轨迹标注细化到轨迹级和子任务级的语言描述，消除当前执行模式的歧义；(2) **See Less**：引入显式视觉证据预算机制，训练执行器仅从任务充分的视觉证据中行动，无需区域或掩码标注。该接口兼容现成的VLM规划器，可通过上下文学习使用。

Result: 在AgiBot G2兼容变体TX-G2和HSR机器人上的8个真实任务中，S2将平均子任务成功率从pi0.5基线的54.2%提升至79.0%。消融实验表明：粗指令导致可避免的监督混叠；保留目标的本地区分性指导优于直接替换指令；显式证据预算减少了对广域视觉上下文的依赖。

Conclusion: VLA泛化能力的提升关键在于训练执行器从信息丰富的本地区分性指导和任务充分的视觉证据中行动，而非从弱监督中同时恢复两者。S2框架通过改变执行器的学习问题而非增加模型容量来提升泛化。

Abstract: Generalization remains a central bottleneck for vision-language-action (VLA) models: under distractors, appearance shifts, and semantically similar tasks, the policy must often infer local execution details from coarse instructions while also deciding which parts of the image matter for control. We present S2 (See Less, Specify More), a framework for improving VLA generalization by training the executor under a cleaner interface.
  Specify More preserves the original instruction as a stable high-level goal while relabeling each trajectory into refined trajectory- and subtask-level language that disambiguates the current execution mode. Unlike native attention, See Less imposes an explicit visual evidence budget, training the executor to act from task-sufficient evidence rather than unconstrained visual context, without any region or mask annotation.
  This interface lets the executor follow detailed guidance without relying on distracting visual patches or resolving avoidable ambiguity on its own, and it remains compatible with off-the-shelf VLM planners through in-context learning. Across our main evaluation settings, S2 improves overall generalization metrics by changing the executor's learning problem: coarse instructions induce avoidable supervision aliasing, goal-preserving local guidance outperforms instruction replacement in our main ablations, and explicit evidence budgeting reduces dependence on broad visual context beyond efficiency considerations.
  Across eight real-robot tasks on TX-G2 (an AgiBot G2-compatible variant) and HSR, S2 raises mean subtask success from 54.2% to 79.0% over pi0.5. Together, these results suggest that VLA generalization improves when the executor is trained to act from informative local guidance and task-sufficient visual evidence, rather than recovering both from weak supervision.

</details>


### [200] [SeeTraceAct: Visibility-Aware Latent Planning from Cross-Embodiment Demonstration Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02745)
*Jaehyeon Son,Junhyun Kim,Kyle Kam,Jeremiah Coholich,Seok Joon Kim,Jinhoo Kim,Chris Dongjoo Kim,Jaemin Cho,Dieter Fox,Zsolt Kira*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 SeeTraceAct，一种通过可见性感知的未来末端执行器轨迹预测来增强空间定位能力的一次性演示条件视觉-语言-动作模型，在 RoboCasa-DC 和真实世界基准上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有一次性演示条件 VLA 模型在执行需要精确定位小目标区域的任务时表现不佳，且缺乏跨本体的可复现评估基准。

Method: 提出 SeeTraceAct 框架，通过可见性感知的未来末端执行器轨迹预测来增强空间定位能力；同时构建并发布了 RoboCasa-DC 数据集（RoboCasa 的演示条件扩展，包含配对的人类视频）。

Result: 在 RoboCasa-DC 的全部四个设置下取得最佳成功率，在真实世界基准上平均成功率提升 12.5 个百分点。

Conclusion: SeeTraceAct 通过可见性感知的轨迹预测有效提升了演示条件 VLA 在小目标区域精确定位任务上的性能，且跨本体演示的方式具有实用价值。

Abstract: Vision-language-action models (VLAs) are promising general-purpose robot policies, but adapting them to new tasks typically requires costly task-specific teleoperation data. As an alternative, we study one-shot demo-conditioned VLAs, where a robot policy is conditioned on a single demonstration video of an unseen task. We find that existing end-to-end approaches often struggle when successful execution requires precisely localizing small target regions. To address this limitation, we propose SeeTraceAct, a demo-conditioned VLA framework that encourages precise spatial grounding through visibility-aware prediction of future end-effector traces. To enable reproducible evaluation with cross-embodiment demonstrations, we introduce and release RoboCasa-DC, a demo-conditioned extension of RoboCasa with episode-paired humanoid videos. Experiments on RoboCasa-DC and a real-world benchmark, where a Franka Panda arm is conditioned on human demonstrations, show that SeeTraceAct outperforms baselines, achieving the best success rate across all four RoboCasa-DC settings and improving real-world average success by 12.5 percentage points.

</details>


### [201] [Hybrid Adaptive Kalman Filtering for Data-Efficient Joint Tracking and Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02767)
*Jiho Lee,Nisar R. Ahmed,Rebecca Russell*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种自监督混合自适应卡尔曼滤波器，仅利用测量数据学习系统动力学和过程噪声协方差的结构化修正，同时保持滤波器的概率结构，实现改进的估计精度和统计一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统卡尔曼滤波对模型失配和噪声协方差调优高度敏感，而基于学习的方法依赖大规模监督训练且无法产生一致的 uncertainty 估计。

Method: 提出自监督混合自适应卡尔曼滤波器，从测量数据中学习系统动力学和过程噪声协方差的结构化修正，保留概率结构以便计算创新似然，并通过广义贝叶斯推理进行模型分类。

Result: 在真实世界和模拟数据集上，该方法在低数据和大数据场景下均表现出改进的估计精度、统计一致性以及稳健的分类性能。

Conclusion: 自监督混合自适应卡尔曼滤波器能够在不依赖监督数据的情况下，有效解决模型失配问题，同时保持概率一致性和良好的分类能力。

Abstract: Kalman filtering performance is highly sensitive to model mismatch and noise covariance tuning. Learning-based approaches address these limitations but typically rely on supervised training with large datasets and do not produce consistent uncertainty estimates. In this paper, we propose a self-supervised Hybrid Adaptive Kalman Filter that learns structured corrections to system dynamics and process noise covariance from measurements alone while preserving the probabilistic structure of the filter. This allows the innovation likelihood to be computed and subsequently used for model classification via generalized Bayesian inference. Experimental results on real-world and simulated datasets demonstrate improved estimation accuracy and statistical consistency as well as robust classification performance across both low-data and large-data scenarios.

</details>


### [202] [A Measurement-Driven Digital Twin Architecture for Plant-Level Biomass Estimation and Growth Forecasting in Hydroponic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02796)
*Morgan Mayborne,Abhisesh Silwal,George Kantor*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文开发了一套水培生菜数字孪生系统，利用RGB-D图像与神经网络实时估计植株质量（误差<1.5g），并整合生长模型实现未来1-4天产量预测（误差约2g）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为应对城市中心食品分配问题，需要替代土壤园艺的方案（如水培），并实现对植物生长的精确追踪与预测。

Method: 1) 搭建水培温室及定制传感器硬件；2) 采集1300张RGB-D图像数据集；3) 训练神经网络以视觉方式连续估计植株质量；4) 将质量估计与生长模型结合，构建数字孪生模型，持续更新生长轨迹。

Result: 神经网络质量估计误差在1.5g以内；数字孪生模型对未来1-4天的产量预测误差保持在约2g。

Conclusion: 该数字孪生系统能够有效整合实时传感数据与生长模型，实现对水培生菜生长的高精度追踪与短期预测，验证了该方法在精准农业中的应用潜力。

Abstract: Alternatives to soil-based horticulture, such as hydroponics, have been developed to respond to food distribution concerns for dense urban centers. A new system was developed to track an individual lettuce plant's growth in a hydroponic environment, utilizing streams of measured information and available models to continuously update the growth trajectory estimates for a plant. These "digital twin" models were integrated into an operating hydroponic greenhouse, with custom horticultural and sensor hardware to grow and measure relevant information. To aid in updating model parameters, plant yield was continuously measured with a custom neural network, using RGB-D images of the plants as an input. The network, trained on a collected dataset of 1300 images, was able to estimate mass within 1.5 g of the ground-truth value. After integration into the custom system, digital twin growth projections could approximate future yield between one and four days in the future, maintaining around a 2 g forecasting error.

</details>


### [203] [Direct Informed Sampling on Riemannian Manifolds via Loewner Order Lower Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02879)
*Phone Thiha Kyaw,Jonathan Kelly*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于矩阵值容许启发函数的Riemannian运动规划方法，利用Loewner序在保持度量方向结构的前提下计算最紧常值下界，通过Cholesky分解将Riemannian informed集转化为标准长球超椭球体，实现了拒绝-free采样，在多个操作任务中显著加速了最优运动规划器的收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Euclidean启发函数的informed采样技术在Riemannian度量下不可容许，而标量特征值界虽能恢复容许性但丢弃了度量的方向结构，导致informed集过于保守，亟需一种既能保持容许性又能保留度量方向结构的方法。

Method: 1) 利用Loewner序在对称正定矩阵上计算度量张量的最紧常值下界矩阵；2) 对该下界矩阵进行Cholesky分解，定义到各向同性Euclidean空间的线性映射；3) 在该映射下，Riemannian informed集转化为标准长球超椭球体，可直接使用现有算法进行无拒绝采样。

Result: 在UR5(6-DoF)、Franka(7-DoF)和PR2(14-DoF)三种机器人及三种不同Riemannian度量上的实验表明，所提启发函数持续产生比Euclidean和标量特征值界更紧的informed集，加速了多种最优运动规划器的收敛。

Conclusion: 矩阵值容许启发函数通过保留度量的完整方向结构，在保持admissibility的同时显著缩小informed集，为Riemannian流形上的informed采样提供了理论最优且实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Informed sampling techniques accelerate sampling-based motion planners by focusing the search on promising regions of the state space, yet most existing methods rely on Euclidean heuristics that become inadmissible under configuration-dependent Riemannian metrics. While scalar eigenvalue bounds restore admissibility by uniformly scaling the Euclidean distance, they discard the directional structure of the metric, producing overly conservative informed sets. We propose a matrix-valued admissible heuristic that exploits the Loewner order on symmetric positive definite matrices to compute the tightest constant lower bound on the metric tensor while preserving its full directional structure. The Cholesky factorization of this bound defines a linear map to an isotropic Euclidean space in which the Riemannian informed set reduces to a standard prolate hyperspheroid, enabling direct, rejection-free sampling using existing algorithms.
